Reroute, Don't Remove: Recoverable Visual Token Routing for Vision-Language Models
Authors: Cheng-Yu Yang, Shao-Yuan Lo, Yu-Lun Liu
First: 2026-06-10T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:59:57+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/elmma/mllm-reroute/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) project images into hundreds to thousands of visual tokens, making decoder inference expensive in both attention computation and KV-cache memory. Existing visual-token reduction methods largely follow a rank-and-remove paradigm: they score visual tokens, keep a compact subset, and permanently discard the rest. We show that this irreversible action is fragile because visual-token importance changes across decoder depth; tokens ranked low at one stage may become relevant in later layers, especially for grounding-sensitive queries. We propose Reroute, a training-free plug-in that replaces removal with recoverable routing. At each routing stage, selected vision tokens pass through decoder blocks, while deferred tokens bypass the stage and re-enter the candidate pool at the next routing decision. Reroute reuses existing attention-score ranking rules and stage-wise schedules, preserving the theoretical TFLOPs and KV-cache budget class of the pruning method it augments. Across FastV, PDrop, and Nüwa variants on LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen backbones, reroute improves grounding under aggressive token reduction while maintaining general VQA performance. These results suggest that VLM token reduction should not be viewed only as irreversible pruning, but also as recoverable routing. The code can be found here: https://github.com/elmma/mllm-reroute/
中文标题/摘要
标题：重路由而非移除：面向视觉-语言模型的可恢复视觉标记路由
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）将图像投影为数百至数千个视觉标记，导致解码器推理在注意力计算和KV缓存内存方面代价高昂。现有视觉标记缩减方法大多遵循“排序并移除”范式：对视觉标记评分，保留紧凑子集，并永久丢弃其余标记。我们指出，这种不可逆操作脆弱，因为视觉标记的重要性在解码器深度间变化；某一阶段评分较低的标记在后续层可能变得相关，尤其对定位敏感的查询。我们提出Reroute，一种无需训练的插件，用可恢复路由替代移除。在每个路由阶段，选定的视觉标记通过解码器块，而延迟标记绕过该阶段并在下一阶段路由决策时重新进入候选池。Reroute重用现有注意力得分排序规则和分阶段调度，保留其增强的剪枝方法的理论TFLOPs和KV缓存预算类别。在LLaVA-1.5和Qwen骨干网络上的FastV、PDrop和Nüwa变体中，Reroute在激进标记缩减下提升定位性能，同时保持通用视觉问答性能。这些结果表明，VLM标记缩减不应仅视为不可逆剪枝，也应视为可恢复路由。代码见：https://github.com/elmma/mllm-reroute/
Summary / 总结
To address the computational burden of visual token processing in vision-language models, the paper challenges the conventional rank-and-remove approach, which permanently discards low-scoring tokens. Instead, it proposes Reroute, a training-free plug-in that enables recoverable routing: tokens not selected at a given decoder stage bypass the block and re-enter the candidate pool for future routing decisions. This method preserves existing attention-based ranking and scheduling mechanisms while maintaining theoretical efficiency in TFLOPs and KV-cache usage. Experiments on LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen backbones show that Reroute improves grounding accuracy under aggressive token reduction without sacrificing general VQA performance, demonstrating that token reduction should be viewed as routing rather than irreversible pruning.
为解决视觉语言模型因大量视觉标记导致的计算开销问题，该论文挑战了传统的按分数排序并永久移除低分标记的方法。它提出Reroute，一种无需训练的插件，将被延迟的标记重新路由至后续路由阶段的候选池，实现可恢复的路由，而不改变原有的注意力评分或调度机制。在LLaVA-1.5和Qwen骨干网络上，针对FastV、PDrop和Nüwa变体的实验表明，Reroute在大幅减少标记的同时保持通用视觉问答性能，并提升定位准确性，证明视觉标记减少应被视为可恢复路由而非不可逆剪枝。
Context-Driven Incremental Compression for Multi-Turn Dialogue Generation
Authors: Yeongseo Jung, Jaehyeok Kim, Eunseo Jung, Jiachuan Wang, Yongqi Zhang, Ka Chun Cheung, Simon See, Lei Chen
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-10T17:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:59:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern conversational agents condition on an ever-growing dialogue history at each turn, incurring redundant attention and encoding costs that grow with conversation length. Naive truncation or summarization degrades fidelity, while existing context compressors lack cross-turn memory sharing or revision, causing information loss and compounding errors in long dialogues. We revisit the context compression under conversational dynamics and empirically present its fragility. To improve both efficiency and robustness, we introduce Context-Driven Incremental Compression (C-DIC), which treats a conversation as interleaved contextual threads and stores revisable per-thread compression states in a single, compact dialogue memory. At each turn, a lightweight retrieve, revise, and write-back loop shares information across turns and updates stale memories, stabilizing long-horizon behavior. In addition, we adapt truncated backpropagation-through-time (TBPTT) to our multi-turn setting, learning cross-turn dependencies without full-history backpropagation. Extensive experiments on long-form dialogue benchmarks demonstrate superior performance and efficiency of C-DIC; notably, C-DIC shows stable inference latency and perplexity over hundreds of dialogue turns, supporting a scalable path to high-quality dialogue modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于上下文驱动的增量压缩多轮对话生成
现代对话代理在每一轮均需依赖不断增长的对话历史，导致注意力和编码开销随对话长度增长而冗余。简单截断或摘要会降低保真度，而现有上下文压缩器缺乏跨轮次记忆共享或修订能力，导致长对话中信息丢失和误差累积。我们重新审视对话动态下的上下文压缩，并实证其脆弱性。为提升效率与鲁棒性，我们提出上下文驱动增量压缩（C-DIC），将对话视为交错的上下文线程，并在单一紧凑对话记忆中存储可修订的每线程压缩状态。每轮通过轻量级检索、修订与写回循环实现跨轮次信息共享并更新过期记忆，稳定长时行为。此外，我们将截断时间反向传播（TBPTT）适配至多轮场景，无需完整历史反向传播即可学习跨轮依赖。在长对话基准上的大量实验表明，C-DIC在性能与效率上均优于现有方法；尤其在数百轮对话中，C-DIC保持稳定的推理延迟与困惑度，支持高质量对话建模的可扩展路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and fidelity loss in multi-turn dialogue generation due to growing context lengths, this work proposes Context-Driven Incremental Compression (C-DIC), which models conversations as interleaved contextual threads and maintains compact, revisable per-thread memory states. At each turn, C-DIC performs a lightweight retrieve-revise-write-back loop to share information across turns and update stale memories, stabilizing long-horizon behavior. It also adapts truncated backpropagation-through-time (TBPTT) to learn cross-turn dependencies without full-history gradients. Experiments on long-form dialogue benchmarks show C-DIC achieves superior efficiency and performance, maintaining stable inference latency and perplexity over hundreds of turns.
为了解决长对话生成中因对话历史增长导致的计算冗余与信息丢失问题，本文提出上下文驱动增量压缩（C-DIC）方法，将对话视为多个可修订的上下文线程，并在紧凑的记忆中存储各线程的压缩状态。每轮对话中，C-DIC通过轻量级的检索-修订-写回循环实现跨轮次信息共享与过时记忆更新，稳定长对话行为。同时，它改进了截断BPTT以学习跨轮依赖而不需完整历史梯度。实验表明，C-DIC在长对话基准上表现更优，保持数百轮对话中的稳定推理延迟与困惑度，显著优于基线方法。
How Seemingly Inconsequential Design Choices Dictate Performance of LLMs in Pathology
Authors: Kian R. Weihrauch, Thomas A. Buckley, William Lotter, Arjun K. Manrai
First: 2026-06-10T17:59:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:59:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
General-purpose large language models (LLMs) are routinely used as baselines when evaluating specialized pathology models on whole-slide images (WSIs). Because WSIs exceed contemporary model context limits, LLM baselines routinely use small, high-magnification patches processed independently via majority voting, without systematic evaluation of seemingly inconsequential design choices such as patch size, patch count, and magnification. Generalist LLMs have consistently underperformed specialized systems, reinforcing the perception that domain-specific training or architectural adaptation is necessary for pathology tasks involving WSIs. Here, we conduct a systematic factorial analysis of four input design factors: inference mode, patch size, magnification, and patch count. We demonstrate that prior studies have overstated the gap between specialized models and general-purpose LLMs by choosing non-optimized input configurations. On the MultiPathQA benchmark, switching to a single balanced configuration (large patches at lower magnification, processed jointly) raises GPT-5 from 15.1% to 39.5% on cancer-type classification (TCGA) and from 38.1% to 62.9% on organ classification (GTEx). Per-task optimization yields further gains up to 43.9% (TCGA) and 71.6% (GTEx). The same configuration generalizes to two other models and to a fully held-out CPTAC cohort, where it improves Gemini 3 Flash by 23.4 percentage points without any task-specific tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：看似微不足道的设计选择如何决定LLM在病理学中的表现
通用大语言模型（LLM）常被用作评估专门病理模型在全切片图像（WSI）上的基准。由于WSI超出当前模型上下文限制，LLM基准通常使用小尺寸、高倍率的独立处理图像块，并通过多数投票，而未系统评估诸如图像块大小、块数和放大倍率等看似无关紧要的设计选择。通用LLM在病理学任务中持续表现不及专门系统，强化了人们认为病理学任务需领域特定训练或架构调整的观点。本文系统性地分析了四个输入设计因素：推理模式、图像块大小、放大倍率和图像块数量。我们证明，先前研究因选择非优化输入配置，夸大了专门模型与通用LLM之间的性能差距。在MultiPathQA基准测试中，切换至单一平衡配置（大图像块、低倍率、联合处理）使GPT-5在癌症类型分类（TCGA）中从15.1%提升至39.5%，在器官分类（GTEx）中从38.1%提升至62.9%。按任务优化可进一步提升至43.9%（TCGA）和71.6%（GTEx）。同一配置还可推广至两个其他模型及完全未参与训练的CPTAC队列，使Gemini 3 Flash提升23.4个百分点，且无需任务特定调优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the widespread use of general-purpose LLMs as baselines for pathology tasks on whole-slide images despite their known underperformance, this study investigates how seemingly minor input design choices—such as patch size, magnification, and inference mode—affect model accuracy. Through a systematic factorial analysis of four key factors, the authors show that prior comparisons unfairly disadvantaged LLMs by using suboptimal configurations. When optimized, GPT-5’s performance on cancer-type classification (TCGA) and organ classification (GTEx) improved dramatically from 15.1% to 39.5% and from 38.1% to 62.9%, respectively, and further gains up to 43.9% and 71.6% were achieved with per-task tuning. The same optimal configuration generalized to other models and a held-out cohort, boosting Gemini 3 Flash by 23.4 percentage points without task-specific adaptation.
鉴于通用大语言模型在病理学全切片图像任务中常被用作基线但表现不佳，本研究探讨了看似微不足道的输入设计选择（如图像块大小、放大倍数和推理模式）如何影响模型性能。通过对四个关键因素进行因子分析，作者发现以往研究因使用次优配置而高估了专用模型与通用模型之间的差距。优化后，GPT-5在癌症类型分类（TCGA）和器官分类（GTEx）任务中的准确率分别从15.1%提升至39.5%和从38.1%提升至62.9%，并通过任务特定调优进一步提升至43.9%和71.6%。同一配置在其他模型和独立数据集上也表现出良好泛化能力，使Gemini 3 Flash准确率提升23.4个百分点，无需任务特定调整。
FACTR 2: Learning External Force Sensing for Commodity Robot Arms Improves Policy Learning
Authors: Steven Oh, Jason Jingzhou Liu, Tony Tao, Philip Han, Kenneth Shaw, Satoshi Funabashi, Ruslan Salakhutdinov, Deepak Pathak
First: 2026-06-10T17:59:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:59:35+00:00
Comments: Website at https://jasonjzliu.com/factr2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contact-rich manipulation requires force sensitivity, but many robot arms lack dedicated force sensors due to their high cost. We present Neural External Torque Estimation (NEXT), a data-driven method that estimates external joint torques without needing any dedicated force sensors. NEXT trains in 1 minute from only 10 minutes of free-motion data, yet achieves estimates comparable to dedicated joint-torque sensors. NEXT enables force-feedback teleoperation on low-cost arms and improves policy learning through Force-Informed Re-Sampling Training (FIRST), which up-samples pre-contact and contact segments during behavior cloning. Across five long-horizon tasks, FIRST outperforms prior force-aware policies by over 17% in task progress. Together, NEXT and FIRST bring force-aware teleoperation and policy learning to off-the-shelf robots without additional sensing hardware. Video results and code are available at https://jasonjzliu.com/factr2
中文标题/摘要
标题：FACTR 2：为商用机械臂学习外部力感知以提升策略学习
接触密集型操作需要力敏感性，但许多机械臂因成本高昂而缺乏专用力传感器。我们提出了神经外部转矩估计（NEXT），一种无需专用力传感器即可估计外部关节转矩的数据驱动方法。NEXT仅需10分钟自由运动数据即可在1分钟内完成训练，其估计结果与专用关节转矩传感器相当。NEXT使低成本机械臂能够实现力反馈遥操作，并通过力感知重采样训练（FIRST）提升策略学习效果，该方法在行为克隆过程中对接触前和接触段进行上采样。在五个长时任务中，FIRST相比先前的力感知策略任务进度提升超过17%。NEXT与FIRST共同实现了无需额外传感硬件即可将力感知遥操作和策略学习应用于现成机械臂。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of dedicated force sensors in commodity robot arms, which hinders force-sensitive manipulation, this work introduces NEXT, a data-driven method that estimates external joint torques using only free-motion data, achieving sensor-level accuracy within minutes of training. Coupled with FIRST, a force-informed re-sampling strategy during behavior cloning, the approach enhances policy learning by prioritizing pre-contact and contact segments. Across five long-horizon tasks, FIRST improved task progress by over 17% compared to prior force-aware methods, enabling effective force-aware teleoperation and policy learning without additional hardware.
鉴于商用机械臂普遍缺乏专用力传感器，限制了力感知操作能力，本文提出NEXT方法，仅通过自由运动数据即可数据驱动地估计外部关节力矩，训练仅需1分钟，精度媲美专用传感器。结合FIRST策略——在行为克隆中优先采样接触前与接触阶段的数据——显著提升策略学习效果。在五个长时任务中，FIRST相较现有力感知策略平均提升任务进度17%以上，实现无需额外硬件的力感知遥操作与策略学习。
World Pilot: Steering Vision-Language-Action Models with World-Action Priors
Authors: Zefu Lin, Rongxu Cui, Junjia Xu, Xiaojuan Jin, Wenling Li, Lue Fan, Zhaoxiang Zhang
First: 2026-06-10T17:59:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:59:08+00:00
Comments: Project Website: https://world-pilot.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models inherit semantic grounding from large-scale pretraining and perform competently across in-distribution manipulation tasks. This grounding, however, is built on static image-text pairs, whereas manipulation is a continuous, contact-rich process whose dynamics this pretraining cannot capture. We present World Pilot, a VLA framework that augments the policy with priors from a World-Action Model (WAM), routed into the decision chain through two complementary pathways. Latent Steering conditions the perception layer on a scene-evolution latent, and Action Steering supplies an anticipated trajectory as a motion prior to the action generator. Together the two priors equip the VLA with an anticipated view of the scene and a trajectory-level motion hint alongside its semantic conditioning, and the scene-evolution prior remains effective even when supplied by a video-pretrained world model that has not been action-post-trained. World Pilot attains a state-of-the-art Total success rate of 84.7% on the LIBERO-Plus zero-shot OOD benchmark and the highest success rate on every real-robot setting across four manipulation tasks, with the largest margins under shifts in viewpoint, geometry, deformable state, and pose. Project Website: https://world-pilot.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界飞行员：利用世界-动作先验引导视觉-语言-动作模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型从大规模预训练中继承语义 grounding，并在分布内操作任务中表现良好。然而，这种 grounding 建立在静态图像-文本对之上，而操作是一个连续且接触密集的过程，其动态特性无法通过预训练捕捉。我们提出了 World Pilot，一种 VLA 框架，通过两个互补路径将世界-动作模型（WAM）的先验引入策略决策链。潜在引导通过场景演化潜变量调节感知层，动作引导则为动作生成器提供预期轨迹作为运动先验。两者结合，使 VLA 在语义条件之外，同时获得场景演化预期和轨迹级运动提示，且即使由未进行动作后训练的视频预训练世界模型提供，场景演化先验仍保持有效性。World Pilot 在 LIBERO-Plus 零样本 OOD 基准测试中达到 84.7% 的最先进总成功率，并在四个操作任务的每种真实机器人设置中均取得最高成功率，尤其在视角、几何、可变形状态和姿态变化时优势显著。项目网站：https://world-pilot.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of vision-language-action models in handling dynamic, contact-rich manipulation tasks due to their reliance on static image-text pretraining, the authors introduce World Pilot, a framework that integrates world-action priors via two pathways: latent steering for scene evolution guidance and action steering for motion prediction. Experiments show that this dual-prior approach significantly enhances performance, achieving an 84.7% total success rate on LIBERO-Plus zero-shot OOD benchmark and top success rates across four real-robot manipulation tasks, especially under challenging conditions like viewpoint shifts and deformable states.
为了解决视觉-语言-动作模型在处理动态、接触密集型操作任务时因依赖静态图文预训练而能力不足的问题，作者提出了World Pilot框架，通过两种路径注入世界-动作先验：潜空间引导用于场景演化预测，动作引导用于运动轨迹预测。这些先验补充了语义条件，使模型具备对场景动态和运动轨迹的预期能力。实验表明，World Pilot在四个真实机器人操作任务中均取得最高成功率，并在LIBERO-Plus零样本OOD基准上以84.7%的总成功率领先，尤其在视角、几何、可变形状态和姿态变化下表现突出，即使世界模型仅经过视频预训练而未进行动作后训练仍保持有效性。
DIRECT: When and Where Should You Allocate Test-Time Compute in Embodied Planners?
Authors: Jadelynn Dao, Milan Ganai, Yasmina Abukhadra, Ajay Sridhar, Mozhgan Nasr Azadani, Katie Luo, Clark Barrett, Jiajun Wu, Chelsea Finn, Marco Pavone
First: 2026-06-10T17:58:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:58:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for embodied agents, with an emerging strategy of scaling test-time compute to improve capability. However, we observe that doing so increases latency, token usage, and FLOPs while yielding uneven, often diminishing gains in downstream success, limiting where embodied agents can be deployed. We argue that choosing when and where to spend test-time compute is central to bringing frontier performance to the real world. We introduce DIRECT, a routing framework that uses multimodal scene context to allocate compute per prompt, improving the success--cost Pareto frontier over fixed model selection. Across three dominant scaling axes, namely chain-of-thought depth, model size, and memory history, our experiments on VLABench and RoboMME show that test-time compute is not a uniform lever: different axes yield qualitatively distinct capability gains. We validate these insights on a physical Franka arm in a DROID setup spanning zero-shot manipulation and long-horizon chaining, where our router matches or exceeds a stronger model's success rate at up to 65% lower average latency. Ultimately, our results show that naively scaling test-time compute is wasteful, and that DIRECT can provide frontier-level embodied planning in robotic systems at a fraction of the cost. Project page can be found at jadee-dao.github.io/direct/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DIRECT：在具身规划器中何时何地分配测试时计算资源？
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）正越来越多地作为具身代理的高层规划器部署，一种新兴策略是扩展测试时计算以提升能力。然而，我们观察到这样做会增加延迟、令牌使用量和FLOPs，而下游成功率的提升却往往不均衡且趋于递减，限制了具身代理的部署场景。我们认为，选择何时何地分配测试时计算资源，是将前沿性能带入现实世界的核心。我们提出了DIRECT，一种利用多模态场景上下文按提示分配计算资源的路由框架，相比固定模型选择，能改善成功率-成本帕累托前沿。在三个主要的扩展维度——思维链深度、模型规模和记忆历史——上，我们在VLABench和RoboMME上的实验表明，测试时计算并非统一杠杆：不同维度带来的能力提升具有质的不同。我们在DROID设置下的物理Franka机械臂上验证了这些见解，涵盖零样本操作与长时序链式任务，我们的路由器在平均延迟低至65%的情况下，成功率与更强模型相当或更高。最终，我们的结果表明，盲目扩展测试时计算是浪费，而DIRECT能在机器人系统中以更低成本实现前沿级具身规划。项目页面见jadee-dao.github.io/direct/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniformly scaling test-time compute in embodied agents, which increases latency and resource usage without proportional gains, the paper introduces DIRECT, a routing framework that dynamically allocates compute based on multimodal scene context per prompt. Experiments across three scaling axes—chain-of-thought depth, model size, and memory history—on VLABench and RoboMME reveal that different compute allocations yield qualitatively distinct performance improvements. On a physical Franka arm in a DROID setup, DIRECT matches or exceeds a stronger model’s success rate while reducing average latency by up to 65%, demonstrating that targeted compute allocation is more effective than uniform scaling.
鉴于在具身智能体中均匀扩展测试时计算资源会导致延迟上升、资源浪费且收益递减，本文提出DIRECT框架，根据每条提示的多模态场景上下文动态分配计算资源。在链式思维深度、模型规模和记忆历史三个扩展维度上，VLABench和RoboMME实验表明不同计算分配方式带来质异的性能提升。在DROID平台的物理Franka机械臂实验中，DIRECT在保持或超越更强模型成功率的同时，平均延迟降低高达65%，证明针对性计算分配比均匀扩展更适用于实际部署。
Doc-to-Atom: Learning to Compile and Compose Memory Atoms
Authors: Xingjian Diao, Wenbo Li, Yashas Malur Saidutta, Avinash Amballa, Lazar Valkov, Srinivas Chappidi
First: 2026-06-10T17:58:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:58:20+00:00
Comments: 20 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long input sequences are central to document understanding and multi-step reasoning in Large Language Models, yet the quadratic cost of attention makes inference both memory-intensive and slow. Context distillation mitigates this by compressing contextual information into model parameters, and recent work such as Doc-to-LoRA amortizes context distillation into a single forward pass that generates one LoRA adapter per document. However, producing a single monolithic adapter for all queries leads to irrelevant-query interference, limited compositional recall, and poor scalability to long-document reasoning. To address these challenges, we propose Doc-to-Atom (Doc2Atom), a compositional parametric memory framework that decomposes each document into semantically typed knowledge atoms. Each atom is compiled into an independent micro-LoRA adapter and a provenance retrieval key. At inference time, a lightweight query router selects and assembles only the relevant atoms into a query-specific adapter, which is then injected into a frozen base model. The entire system is trained end-to-end through a multi-objective distillation framework. Experiments on six diverse QA benchmarks demonstrate that Doc2Atom outperforms Doc-to-LoRA baselines while reducing the memory cost of document internalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Doc-to-Atom：学习编译与组合记忆原子
长输入序列是大型语言模型进行文档理解与多步推理的核心，但注意力机制的二次成本导致推理既耗内存又缓慢。上下文蒸馏通过将上下文信息压缩进模型参数缓解此问题，近期工作如Doc-to-LoRA将上下文蒸馏压缩为单次前向传递，为每个文档生成一个LoRA适配器。然而，为所有查询生成单一的单体适配器会导致无关查询干扰、组合性召回受限及难以扩展至长文档推理。为解决这些问题，我们提出Doc-to-Atom（Doc2Atom），一种组合式参数化记忆框架，将每个文档分解为语义类型化的知识原子。每个原子编译为独立的微LoRA适配器和溯源检索键。推理时，轻量级查询路由器仅选择并组装相关原子组成查询专用适配器，再注入冻结的基础模型。整个系统通过多目标蒸馏框架端到端训练。在六个多样化的问答基准测试中，Doc2Atom优于Doc-to-LoRA基线，同时降低文档内化内存成本。
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency of attention mechanisms in handling long documents, Doc-to-Atom introduces a compositional memory framework that breaks down documents into semantically typed knowledge atoms, each compiled into a micro-LoRA adapter and a retrieval key. At inference, a lightweight router selects only relevant atoms to construct a query-specific adapter, which is injected into a frozen base model. Trained end-to-end via multi-objective distillation, Doc-to-Atom achieves superior performance over Doc-to-LoRA baselines across six QA benchmarks while significantly reducing memory overhead and improving scalability for long-document reasoning.
为解决长文档处理中注意力机制效率低下及单个适配器导致的无关查询干扰与可扩展性差的问题，Doc-to-Atom提出一种组合式参数化记忆框架，将文档分解为语义类型化的知识原子。每个原子编译为独立的微LoRA适配器与溯源检索键，推理时通过轻量级查询路由器仅组装相关原子生成查询专属适配器，再注入冻结的基础模型。该系统通过多目标蒸馏框架端到端训练，在六个多样化问答基准上优于Doc-to-LoRA基线，同时大幅降低长文档内化所需内存开销。
Redesign Mixture-of-Experts Routers with Manifold Power Iteration
Authors: Songhao Wu, Ang Lv, Ruobing Xie, Yankai Lin
First: 2026-06-10T17:57:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:57:36+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Router is the cornerstone component to the Mixture-of-Experts models. Serving as expert proxies, the rows of the router matrix compute their similarity to the MoE inputs to determine which subset of experts is activated. Ideally, each router row is designed to encode the expert matrix into this representative vector, such that its dot-product with token can better reflect token-expert affinity. However, there exists no design principles to enforce this condensation. In this paper, we propose to align each router row with the principal singular direction of the associated expert, as this direction provides the most expressive mathematical description of a matrix. Based on this principle, we propose a router redesign with Manifold Power Iteration (MPI). Specifically, it introduces a "Power-then-Retract" paradigm, where a power iteration step is performed on the router weights, followed by a retraction to impose a norm constraint to ensure both efficiency and stability. Theoretically, we show that MPI drives router rows to converge toward the principal singular directions of associated experts. Empirically, we pretrain MoE model across scales from 1B to 11B parameters to confirm that this alignment facilitates more effective MoE models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于流形幂迭代重设计专家混合路由器
路由器是专家混合模型的核心组件。作为专家代理，路由矩阵的每一行计算其与输入的相似度，以决定激活哪些专家子集。理想情况下，每个路由行应编码专家矩阵为代表性向量，使其与token的点积能更好地反映token与专家的亲和度。然而，目前尚无设计原则可强制实现这种压缩。本文提出将每个路由行对齐其关联专家的主奇异方向，因为该方向能最精确地数学描述矩阵。基于此原则，我们提出一种基于流形幂迭代（MPI）的路由器重设计方法。具体而言，采用“幂迭代后重缩”范式：先对路由权重执行幂迭代，再通过重缩操作施加范数约束，以确保效率与稳定性。理论上，我们证明MPI使路由行收敛至关联专家的主奇异方向。实证上，我们在1B至11B参数规模的MoE模型上预训练，验证该对齐方式可提升MoE模型效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of principled design for router rows in Mixture-of-Experts models, this paper proposes aligning each router row with the principal singular direction of its associated expert matrix to better encode expert-token affinity. The method, Manifold Power Iteration (MPI), employs a "Power-then-Retract" procedure: it first applies power iteration to router weights and then retracts them to maintain norm constraints, ensuring both convergence and numerical stability. Experimental results across MoE models pretrained from 1B to 11B parameters demonstrate that MPI significantly improves MoE performance by enhancing the alignment between router rows and expert singular directions, leading to more effective expert selection.
为解决MoE模型中路由器设计缺乏理论指导的问题，本文提出将每个路由器行对齐其关联专家矩阵的主奇异方向，以更准确地编码专家亲和性。方法Manifold Power Iteration（MPI）采用“幂迭代后重缩放”策略：先对路由器权重进行幂迭代，再通过重缩放步骤施加范数约束，确保收敛性与数值稳定性。在1B至11B参数规模的MoE模型预训练实验中，MPI显著提升了路由效率与模型性能，验证了主奇异方向对专家表征的有效性。
VLGA: Vision-Language-Geometry-Action Models for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Jin Yao, Dhruva Dixith Kurra, Tom Lampo, Zezhou Cheng, Danhua Guo, Burhan Yaman
First: 2026-06-10T17:57:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:57:06+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://yaojin17.github.io/VLGA/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models can describe scenes and reason about them in language, yet still struggle to ground their actions in the dense 3D world around them. Existing approaches either inject features from a frozen 3D foundation model without an objective that ensures the policy uses them, or constrain geometry with sparse box and map losses that provide no dense spatial signal. We introduce VLGA, the first vision-language-action model supervised to reconstruct the dense 3D world it drives through. VLGA introduces geometry as a fourth modality alongside vision, language, and action through a dedicated expert supervised by a per-pixel pointmap regression loss against LiDAR. Extensive experiments conducted on challenging nuScenes and Bench2Drive datasets for open-loop and closed-loop evaluations, respectively, show the superiority of VLGA over counterpart VLA methods. In particular, on open-loop nuScenes, VLGA sets a new state of the art among VLA methods without ego status, with the lowest L2 (0.50\,m average) and 3-second collision rate (0.18\%). On closed-loop Bench2Drive, VLGA attains the state-of-the-art driving score of 79.08, +0.71 over the strongest prior VLA, at comparable efficiency and comfort.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VLGA：面向自动驾驶的视觉-语言-几何-动作模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型能够用语言描述和推理场景，但仍难以将动作与周围密集的3D世界对齐。现有方法要么注入冻结的3D基础模型特征但缺乏确保策略使用这些特征的目标，要么通过稀疏的框和地图损失约束几何，却无法提供密集的空间信号。我们提出了VLGA，这是首个被监督重建其行驶路径中密集3D世界的视觉-语言-动作模型。VLGA通过专用专家将几何作为视觉、语言和动作之外的第四模态引入，该专家由LiDAR的逐像素点图回归损失监督。在具有挑战性的nuScenes和Bench2Drive数据集上进行的大量实验（分别用于开环和闭环评估）表明，VLGA优于同类VLA方法。特别是在开环nuScenes上，VLGA在无自身状态的VLA方法中达到新最优，平均L2误差为0.50米，3秒碰撞率为0.18%。在闭环Bench2Drive上，VLGA取得79.08的驾驶得分，比最强的先前VLA方法高出0.71，且效率与舒适度相当。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of vision-language-action models in grounding actions to dense 3D environments, VLGA introduces geometry as a fourth modality, supervised via per-pixel pointmap regression against LiDAR data. The model integrates vision, language, geometry, and action into a unified framework, enabling dense 3D world reconstruction during driving. Experiments on nuScenes and Bench2Drive show VLGA outperforms existing VLA methods: on nuScenes, it achieves the lowest L2 distance (0.50m average) and collision rate (0.18%) among ego-status-free models; on Bench2Drive, it attains the best driving score (79.08) with comparable efficiency and comfort.
VLGA 为视觉-语言-动作模型在密集三维环境中执行动作提供几何模态支持，通过与激光雷达数据的逐像素点图回归损失监督几何专家，实现对驾驶环境的三维重建。在nuScenes和Bench2Drive数据集上，VLGA显著优于现有VLA方法：在nuScenes中，其平均L2距离最低（0.50米），碰撞率最低（0.18%）；在Bench2Drive中，驾驶得分最高（79.08），且效率与舒适度相当。
System Report for CCL25-Eval Task 5: New Dataset and LoRA-Fine-Tuned Qwen2.5
Authors: Haotao Xie
First: 2026-06-10T17:54:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:54:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recently, large language models (LLMs) have achieved promising progress in the fields of classical Chinese translation and the generation of classical poetry. However, domain-specific research on precise translation and affective-semantic understanding of classical poetry remains limited. The main challenge is that most studies treat the poetic appreciation task as a general-domain problem, neglecting the distinctive features of poetic appreciation, while high-quality and domain-specific datasets are extremely limited. To address this limitation, we decompose the task into three subtasks: term interpretation, semantic interpretation, and emotional inference. Based on multiple open-source datasets, we perform data cleansing and alignment to construct the Classical Chinese Poetry Instruction Pair Dataset (CCPoetry-49K), which comprises 49,404 high-quality instruction-response pairs explicitly optimized for this domain. We then propose a domain-specialized LLM, called PoetryQwen, by applying Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to fine-tune the Qwen2.5-14B model. Experimental results on the CCL25-Eval Task 5 benchmark demonstrate that PoetryQwen achieves a score of 0.757, representing a 9.7% improvement over the Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct baseline (0.690). These findings clearly indicate that PoetryQwen significantly enhances performance in precise translation and emotional understanding of classical poetry. We present new dataset and methodological considerations intended to support the domain-specific optimization of LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CCL25-Eval任务5系统报告：新数据集与LoRA微调Qwen2.5
近期，大语言模型（LLM）在古文翻译与古典诗歌生成领域取得显著进展。然而，针对古典诗歌精准翻译与情感语义理解的领域研究仍显不足。主要挑战在于多数研究将诗歌鉴赏任务视为通用领域问题，忽视其独特性，且高质量、领域专用数据集极为稀缺。为此，我们将任务分解为术语解释、语义解释与情感推断三个子任务，基于多个开源数据集进行清洗与对齐，构建了古典诗歌指令对数据集（CCPoetry-49K），包含49,404组高质量指令-响应对，专为该领域优化。随后，我们通过LoRA微调Qwen2.5-14B模型，提出领域专用模型PoetryQwen。在CCL25-Eval任务5基准测试中，PoetryQwen得分0.757，较Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct基线（0.690）提升9.7%。结果表明，PoetryQwen显著提升了古典诗歌精准翻译与情感理解能力。我们提出新数据集与方法论，旨在支持LLM的领域专用优化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of domain-specific research and datasets for classical Chinese poetry translation and emotional understanding, this study decomposes the poetic appreciation task into term interpretation, semantic interpretation, and emotional inference, then constructs the CCPoetry-49K dataset with 49,404 high-quality instruction-response pairs. The method fine-tunes the Qwen2.5-14B model using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to create PoetryQwen, a domain-specialized LLM. Experimental results on CCL25-Eval Task 5 show PoetryQwen achieves a score of 0.757, a 9.7% improvement over the baseline Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct (0.690), demonstrating enhanced precision in translation and emotional inference.
为解决古典诗词翻译与情感理解领域缺乏专用数据与研究的问题，该研究将诗歌欣赏任务分解为术语解释、语义解释与情感推断三个子任务，通过数据清洗与对齐构建了包含49,404对高质量指令-响应对的CCPoetry-49K数据集，并基于Qwen2.5-14B模型采用LoRA微调方法构建了专用模型PoetryQwen。在CCL25-Eval Task 5评测中，PoetryQwen得分0.757，较基线Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct（0.690）提升9.7%，显著增强了古典诗词翻译的准确性与情感理解能力。
TAHOE: Text-to-SQL with Automated Hint Optimization from Experience
Authors: Zhiyi Chen, Jie Song, Peng Li
First: 2026-06-10T17:52:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:52:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have democratized database access through Text-to-SQL, but moving from prototypes to production remains difficult. Real deployments must handle strict SQL dialects, massive schemas, and evolving user preferences, while supervised fine-tuning is costly and rigid and agentic test-time scaling is expensive. We present Tahoe, a system that treats prompt optimization as a dynamic data management problem. Tahoe uses an error-driven hint learning pipeline across Development and Deployment to consolidate debugging traces into a structured Hint Bank. Compiler feedback is distilled into reusable Syntax Hints for dialect-specific rules, while execution and user feedback are converted into Semantic Hints for schema- and user-specific logic. Tahoe further introduces a Strategy Layer that models conflicting user intents as competing strategies under shared natural-language triggers, with recency signals and post-learning attribution statistics that summarize empirical success, harm, inertness, and support. At inference time, Tahoe retrieves relevant hints and guides the LLM through Logic Planning followed by SQL Synthesis. We implement and evaluate the development-phase workflow, leaving deployment-time human-feedback updates for future work. On Spider 2.0-Snow, Tahoe substantially improves Text-to-SQL without updating model parameters. On 113 supervised Spider 2.0-Snow-0212 examples using GPT-5.5, Tahoe raises pass rate from 61.95 percent to 79.42 percent and pass-at-4 from 72.57 percent to 87.61 percent, achieves 100 percent Snowflake syntax pass rate, and reduces average compiler-feedback critic rounds from 2.79 to 0.12 per sampled candidate. The same Hint Bank also transfers to weaker backbones, including a 19.7 percentage-point pass-rate gain on Doubao-2.0-lite.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TAHOE：基于经验的自动化提示优化文本到SQL系统
大语言模型（LLMs）通过文本到SQL技术使数据库访问平民化，但从原型到生产部署仍具挑战。实际部署需处理严格的SQL方言、庞大的模式及用户偏好的演变，而监督微调成本高且僵化，代理式测试时扩展昂贵。我们提出TAHOE系统，将提示优化视为动态数据管理问题。TAHOE在开发与部署阶段使用错误驱动的提示学习流水线，将调试轨迹整合为结构化提示库。编译器反馈提炼为可复用的语法提示，用于方言特定规则；执行与用户反馈则转化为语义提示，用于模式与用户特定逻辑。TAHOE进一步引入策略层，将冲突用户意图建模为共享自然语言触发器下的竞争策略，并结合近期信号与学习后归因统计，总结经验成功、损害、无效与支持情况。推理时，TAHOE检索相关提示，引导LLM完成逻辑规划后生成SQL。我们实现并评估开发阶段工作流，部署阶段的人类反馈更新留待未来工作。在Spider 2.0-Snow数据集上，TAHOE显著提升文本到SQL性能而无需更新模型参数。在113个监督Spider 2.0-Snow-0212示例中，使用GPT-5.5时，TAHOE将通过率从61.95%提升至79.42%，通过率@4从72.57%提升至87.61%，实现100% Snowflake语法通过率，且平均编译器反馈批评轮次从2.79降至0.12。同一提示库亦可迁移至较弱主干模型，如Doubao-2.0-lite，通过率提升19.7个百分点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of deploying Text-to-SQL systems in production—such as strict SQL dialects, large schemas, and costly fine-tuning—Tahoe treats prompt optimization as a dynamic data management problem. It builds a Hint Bank by consolidating debugging traces into Syntax Hints (for dialect rules) and Semantic Hints (for schema/user logic), then uses a Strategy Layer to model conflicting user intents as competing strategies with recency and performance signals. At inference, Tahoe retrieves relevant hints to guide LLMs through logic planning and SQL synthesis. Evaluated on Spider 2.0-Snow, Tahoe boosts pass rates from 61.95% to 79.42% and pass-at-4 from 72.57% to 87.61% without model updates, achieves 100% Snowflake syntax compliance, and cuts compiler feedback rounds from 2.79 to 0.12 per candidate. The Hint Bank also transfers to weaker models, yielding a 19.7 percentage-point gain on Doubao-2.0-lite.
为解决Text-to-SQL系统在生产部署中的挑战——如SQL方言限制、大型模式和昂贵的微调——Tahoe将提示优化视为动态数据管理问题。它通过聚合调试痕迹构建提示库，将编译器反馈提炼为方言规则的语法提示，将执行与用户反馈转化为针对模式和用户逻辑的语义提示。策略层将冲突用户意图建模为共享自然语言触发器下的竞争策略，利用近期信号和学习后归因统计评估提示效果。推理时，Tahoe检索相关提示引导LLM进行逻辑规划与SQL合成。在Spider 2.0-Snow上，Tahoe无需模型更新即提升通过率至79.42%（原61.95%）、pass-at-4至87.61%（原72.57%），实现100% Snowflake语法通过率，将编译器反馈轮次从2.79降至0.12。该提示库亦可迁移至较弱模型，如Doubao-2.0-lite，使其通过率提升19.7个百分点。
ATLAS: Active Theory Learning for Automated Science
Authors: Noémi Éltető, Nathaniel D. Daw, Kimberly L. Stachenfeld, Kevin J. Miller
First: 2026-06-10T17:52:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:52:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advancing scientific understanding through mechanistic modeling requires posing the right experimental questions to yield maximally informative data. To automate this pursuit within cognitive science, we introduce ATLAS (Active Theory Learning for Automated Science), an active learning framework for the data-driven discovery of interpretable behavioral models. ATLAS iterates between generating mechanistic hypotheses--instantiated as a diverse ensemble of sparse neural networks (Disentangled RNNs)--and designing experiments that optimally distinguish between them. We test this approach on the problem of recovering reinforcement learning agents from their behavior in bandit tasks. ATLAS designs varied sequences of qualitatively novel experiments with temporal structure tailored to underlying agent characteristics. The models trained on these experiments are evaluated against a comprehensive set of metrics for mechanistic modeling that capture behavioral, structural, and computational similarity. ATLAS achieves a 5-10x improvement in sample efficiency across all metrics compared to random experimentation, and its performance is further validated against expert-designed experiments derived from literature. These in silico results showcase ATLAS's potential to accelerate human-interpretable insights in cognitive science and other domains where scientific inquiry relies on discovering mechanistic models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ATLAS：面向自动化科学的主动理论学习
通过机制建模推进科学理解，需要提出恰当的实验问题以获取最具信息量的数据。为在认知科学中自动化这一过程，我们引入ATLAS（面向自动化科学的主动理论学习），这是一种用于数据驱动发现可解释行为模型的主动学习框架。ATLAS在生成机制性假设（以多样化的稀疏神经网络集合——解耦RNNs形式实现）与设计最优区分这些假设的实验之间迭代。我们在从多臂赌博机任务中行为恢复强化学习代理的问题上测试了该方法。ATLAS设计出具有时间结构、且定性新颖的多样化实验序列，以适应代理的内在特性。在这些实验上训练的模型，通过涵盖行为、结构和计算相似性的全面机制建模指标进行评估。与随机实验相比，ATLAS在所有指标上均实现5至10倍的样本效率提升，其性能还通过与文献中专家设计的实验进行验证。这些仿真结果展示了ATLAS在认知科学及其他依赖机制模型发现的科学领域中加速人类可解释洞察的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the discovery of interpretable mechanistic models in cognitive science, ATLAS employs an active learning framework that iteratively generates diverse sparse neural network hypotheses and designs experiments to maximally distinguish them. Tested on recovering reinforcement learning agents from bandit task behavior, ATLAS constructs temporally structured, qualitatively novel experiments tailored to agent characteristics. Results show a 5-10x improvement in sample efficiency across behavioral, structural, and computational metrics compared to random experimentation, and its performance matches or exceeds expert-designed experiments, demonstrating strong potential for accelerating mechanistic scientific discovery.
为自动化认知科学中可解释机制模型的发现，ATLAS采用主动学习框架，迭代生成多样化的稀疏神经网络假设并设计最优区分实验。在从多臂赌博机行为中恢复强化学习代理的任务中，ATLAS构建了针对代理特性的新颖时序实验。实验结果表明，其在行为、结构和计算层面的评估指标上比随机实验效率提升5至10倍，且表现可媲美或超越文献中的专家设计实验，展示了其加速机制发现的潜力。
SafeManip: A Property-Driven Benchmark for Temporal Safety Evaluation in Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Chengyue Huang, Khang Vo Huynh, Sebastian Elbaum, Zsolt Kira, Lu Feng
First: 2026-05-12T16:49:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:48:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic manipulation is typically evaluated by task success, but successful completion does not guarantee safe execution. Many safety failures are temporal: a robot may touch a clean surface after contamination or release an object before it is fully inside an enclosure. We introduce SafeManip, a property-driven benchmark to explicitly evaluate temporal safety properties in robotic manipulation, moving beyond prior evaluations that largely focus on task completion or per-state constraint violations. SafeManip defines reusable safety templates over finite executions using Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces (LTLf). It maps observed rollouts to symbolic predicate traces and evaluates them with LTLf-based monitors. Its property suite covers eight manipulation safety categories: collision and contact safety, grasp stability, release stability, cross-contamination, action onset, mechanism recovery, object containment, and enclosure access. Templates can be instantiated with task-specific objects, fixtures, regions, or skills, allowing the same safety specifications to generalize across tasks and environments. We evaluate SafeManip on six vision-language-action policies, including $π_0$, $π_{0.5}$, GR00T, and their training variants, across 50 RoboCasa365 household tasks. Results show that even strong models often behave unsafely. Task-success gains do not reliably translate into safer execution: many successful rollouts remain unsafe, while longer-horizon or more complex tasks expose more violations. SafeManip provides a reusable evaluation layer for diagnosing temporal safety failures and measuring safe success beyond task completion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SafeManip：用于机器人操作中时序安全评估的属性驱动基准
机器人操作通常通过任务成功率进行评估，但任务成功并不保证执行安全。许多安全失效具有时序性：机器人可能在污染后接触洁净表面，或在物体未完全进入容器前释放物体。我们提出了SafeManip，一个属性驱动的基准，用于显式评估机器人操作中的时序安全属性，超越以往主要关注任务完成或每状态约束违反的评估方法。SafeManip使用有限轨迹上的线性时序逻辑（LTLf）定义可复用的安全模板，将观测到的执行轨迹映射为符号谓词轨迹，并通过LTLf监控器进行评估。其属性集涵盖八个操作安全类别：碰撞与接触安全、抓取稳定性、释放稳定性、交叉污染、动作启动、机构恢复、物体包含和容器访问。模板可实例化为任务特定的对象、固定装置、区域或技能，使相同的安全规范可泛化至不同任务和环境。我们在六个视觉-语言-动作策略（包括π₀、π₀.₅、GR00T及其训练变体）上对SafeManip进行评估，覆盖50个RoboCasa365家庭任务。结果表明，即使强模型也常表现出不安全行为：任务成功率提升并不可靠地转化为更安全的执行；许多成功轨迹仍不安全，而长时域或更复杂任务暴露更多违规行为。SafeManip提供了一种可复用的评估层，用于诊断时序安全失效并衡量超越任务完成的安全成功。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between task success and temporal safety in robotic manipulation, SafeManip introduces a property-driven benchmark using Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces (LTLf) to evaluate safety across time. It maps robot rollouts into symbolic predicate traces and evaluates them against reusable safety templates covering eight categories like collision, contamination, and containment. Evaluated on six vision-language-action policies across 50 household tasks, the benchmark reveals that even high-performing models often violate safety properties, and task success does not reliably correlate with safe behavior — especially in longer or more complex tasks.
为弥补机器人操作中任务成功率与时间安全性的脱节，SafeManip提出基于线性时态逻辑（LTLf）的属性驱动评估基准，将机器人执行轨迹映射为符号谓词轨迹并评估其安全性。该基准涵盖碰撞、污染、抓取稳定、释放稳定等八类安全属性，支持通过任务特定对象或环境实例化安全模板。在6个视觉-语言-动作策略和50个家庭任务上评估发现，即使表现优异的模型也常违反安全属性，且任务越长或越复杂，安全违规越频繁，表明需超越任务完成度进行显式时间安全评估。
Which Models Are Our Models Built On? Auditing Invisible Dependencies in Modern LLMs
Authors: Sanjay Adhikesaven, Haoxiang Sun, Sewon Min
First: 2026-06-10T17:47:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:47:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern LLM training pipelines increasingly rely on other models to generate data, filter corpora, judge outputs, and guide development decisions. These dependencies are recursive: a model may depend on an upstream artifact whose own dependencies are documented only in separate releases and artifacts. As a result, the full dependency structure is fragmented across heterogeneous public artifacts, with complexity and recursive depth far outpacing humans' ability to trace. We introduce ModSleuth, an agentic system that recursively reconstructs LLM dependency graphs from public artifacts with source-grounded evidence. We find that the primary challenge is no longer information extraction, but defining what constitutes a dependency and reconciling artifact references across inconsistent documentation. We address these challenges through a formalization that distinguishes direct and indirect dependencies, represents heterogeneous pipeline roles through operation-centered relationships, and resolves artifact identities across names, versions, and repositories. Applying ModSleuth to four public-artifact-rich LLM releases, we recover 1,060 source-verified dependencies and construct large-scale dependency graphs of modern LLM development. These graphs reveal multi-hop license obligations, train-evaluation coupling, discrepancies between released and training-time artifacts, and documentation inconsistencies that would otherwise be difficult to uncover. We release ModSleuth and the resulting dependency graphs to support transparent analysis of the increasingly complex ecosystems underlying modern LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我们的模型基于哪些模型？现代大语言模型中隐性依赖关系的审计
现代大语言模型训练流程日益依赖其他模型来生成数据、过滤语料库、判断输出并指导开发决策。这些依赖关系具有递归性：一个模型可能依赖上游产物，而该产物自身的依赖关系仅在独立的发布和产物中记录。因此，完整的依赖结构分散在异构的公共产物中，其复杂性和递归深度远超人类追踪能力。我们提出了ModSleuth，一个基于代理的系统，可从公共产物中递归重建大语言模型依赖图，并提供源码锚定的证据。我们发现，当前主要挑战已不再是信息提取，而是定义何为依赖关系，以及在不一致文档中协调产物引用。我们通过形式化方法解决这些挑战：区分直接与间接依赖，通过操作中心关系表示异构流程角色，并在名称、版本和仓库间解决产物身份识别。将ModSleuth应用于四个富含公共产物的大语言模型发布版本，我们恢复了1060个源码验证的依赖关系，并构建了现代大语言模型开发的大规模依赖图。这些图揭示了多跳许可证义务、训练-评估耦合、发布产物与训练时产物的差异，以及难以发现的文档不一致。我们发布ModSleuth及生成的依赖图，以支持对现代大语言模型日益复杂的底层生态系统的透明分析。

Motivated by the growing complexity of modern LLM development pipelines that rely on recursive, undocumented dependencies across public artifacts, the authors introduce ModSleuth, an agentic system that recursively reconstructs dependency graphs using source-grounded evidence. The method formalizes dependencies by distinguishing direct and indirect relationships, models pipeline roles through operation-centered connections, and resolves artifact identities across names, versions, and repositories. Applied to four public LLM releases, ModSleuth recovered 1,060 verified dependencies and uncovered hidden patterns such as multi-hop licensing, train-evaluation coupling, and documentation mismatches that are otherwise hard to detect.
为应对现代大语言模型开发中日益复杂的递归依赖关系，作者提出了ModSleuth系统，通过源码锚定证据递归重建依赖图。该方法通过区分直接与间接依赖、以操作为中心建模流程角色、并统一不同命名、版本和仓库中的构件标识来解决依赖定义与跨文档一致性问题。在四个公开构件丰富的模型发布版本上，系统识别出1060个已验证依赖项，揭示了多跳许可证义务、训练与评估耦合、发布与训练时构件不一致等隐蔽问题。
APPO: Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization
Authors: Xucong Wang, Ziyu Ma, Yong Wang, Yuxiang Ji, Shidong Yang, Guanhua Chen, Pengkun Wang, Xiangxiang Chu
First: 2026-06-10T17:47:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:47:07+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, including 14 pages of main text and 11 pages of appendix; work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) have substantially improved the multi-turn tool-use capabilities of large language model agents. However, most existing methods assign credit over coarse heuristic units, such as tool-call boundaries or fixed workflows, making it difficult to identify which intermediate decisions influence downstream outcomes. In this work, we study agentic RL from two perspectives: \textit{where to branch and how to assign credit after branching}. Our pilot analysis shows that influential decision points are broadly distributed throughout the generated sequence rather than concentrated at tool calls, while token entropy alone does not reliably reflect their impact on final outcomes. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)}, which shifts branching and credit assignment from coarse interaction units to fine-grained decision points in the sequence. APPO selects branching locations using a Branching Score that combines token uncertainty with policy-induced likelihood gains of subsequent continuations, enabling more targeted exploration while filtering out spurious high-entropy positions. It further introduces procedure-level advantage scaling to better distribute credit across branched rollouts. Experiments on 13 benchmarks show that APPO consistently improves strong agentic RL baselines by nearly 4 points, while keeping efficient tool-calls and maintaining behavior interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：APPO：代理程序策略优化
近期在代理强化学习（RL）方面的进展显著提升了大型语言模型代理的多轮工具使用能力。然而，现有大多数方法在粗粒度启发式单元（如工具调用边界或固定工作流）上分配信用，难以识别哪些中间决策影响下游结果。本文从两个角度研究代理RL：\textit{分支点在哪里，分支后如何分配信用}。初步分析表明，有影响力的关键决策点广泛分布于生成序列中，而非集中在工具调用处，而单靠token熵无法可靠反映其对最终结果的影响。受此启发，我们提出\textbf{代理程序策略优化（APPO）}，将分支与信用分配从粗粒度交互单元转移到序列中的细粒度决策点。APPO使用结合token不确定性与后续延续策略诱导似然增益的分支得分选择分支位置，实现更精准的探索并过滤掉虚假高熵位置。此外，APPO引入程序级优势缩放，以更合理地在分支轨迹间分配信用。在13个基准测试中，APPO持续提升强代理RL基线近4个点，同时保持高效工具调用并维持行为可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that decision influence in agentic RL is spread across sequence tokens rather than concentrated at tool calls, the paper proposes APPO, a method that refines branching and credit assignment to fine-grained decision points. APPO uses a Branching Score combining token uncertainty and policy-induced likelihood gains to select optimal branching locations, while applying procedure-level advantage scaling to distribute credit more accurately across branched trajectories. Experiments across 13 benchmarks demonstrate that APPO consistently boosts performance of strong baselines by nearly 4 points, without increasing tool-call frequency or sacrificing interpretability.
鉴于现有方法在工具调用边界等粗粒度单元上分配信用，难以识别真正影响结果的中间决策点，作者提出APPO方法，将分支与信用分配细化至序列中的单个决策点。APPO通过结合令牌熵与后续延续的策略收益来计算分支得分，以选择有意义的分支位置，并引入过程级优势缩放以更准确地分配分支轨迹的信用。在13个基准测试中，APPO持续提升强基线性能约4分，同时保持工具调用效率和行为可解释性。
SPEA2$^+$: Improved Density Estimation in SPEA2 with Provable Runtime Guarantees
Authors: Duc-Cuong Dang, Andre Opris, Dirk Sudholt
First: 2026-06-10T17:44:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:44:23+00:00
Comments: To appear in the Proceedings of PPSN 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Strength Pareto Evolutionary Algorithm 2 (SPEA2) is a popular and prominent evolutionary algorithm for solving multi-objective optimisation problems. Despite its popularity, theoretical analyses of SPEA2 have only appeared recently. Moreover, these analyses focus exclusively on how SPEA2 handles non-dominated solutions and disregard the algorithmic components responsible for handling dominated solutions. We conduct a first runtime analysis of SPEA2 for which these components are analysed. We prove that, unlike other prominent algorithms, including NSGA-II, NSGA-III and SMS-EMOA under the same setting of constant population size and duplicate elimination, SPEA2 is unable to cover the Pareto front of the OneTrapZeroTrap benchmark efficiently. Our results indicate that using k-th nearest-neighbour distance in the fitness assignment provides an insufficient signal to maintain diversity among dominated individuals. To address this issue, we propose an improved variant, SPEA2$^+$, that considers all pairwise distances. The new algorithm achieves the same performance guarantees as the other prominent algorithms on OneTrapZeroTrap, while matching the performance of the original SPEA2 on simpler problems. Experimental results complement our theoretical findings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPEA2$^+$：具有可证明运行时间保证的改进密度估计SPEA2
强度帕累托进化算法2（SPEA2）是解决多目标优化问题的流行且重要的进化算法。尽管其流行，但SPEA2的理论分析仅最近才出现，且这些分析仅关注SPEA2如何处理非支配解，而忽略负责处理支配解的算法组件。我们首次对SPEA2进行运行时间分析，其中这些组件被纳入分析。我们证明，在相同常数种群规模和重复消除设置下，与NSGA-II、NSGA-III和SMS-EMOA等其他著名算法不同，SPEA2无法高效覆盖OneTrapZeroTrap基准的帕累托前沿。我们的结果表明，使用k近邻距离进行适应度分配不足以维持支配个体间的多样性。为解决此问题，我们提出改进版本SPEA2$^+$，考虑所有成对距离。新算法在OneTrapZeroTrap上达到与其他著名算法相同的性能保证，同时在更简单问题上与原始SPEA2性能相当。实验结果补充了我们的理论发现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of theoretical understanding of SPEA2’s handling of dominated solutions, this paper introduces SPEA2$^+$, a variant that improves density estimation by considering all pairwise distances rather than relying on k-th nearest-neighbour distances. Theoretical analysis shows that standard SPEA2 fails to efficiently cover the Pareto front on the OneTrapZeroTrap benchmark under constant population size and duplicate elimination, unlike NSGA-II, NSGA-III, and SMS-EMOA. SPEA2$^+$ restores the performance guarantees of these algorithms on the challenging benchmark while preserving original SPEA2’s efficiency on simpler problems, validating the importance of comprehensive distance-based diversity maintenance.
为弥补SPEA2对支配解处理机制缺乏理论分析的不足，本文提出SPEA2$^+$，其通过将适应度分配中的k近邻距离替换为所有成对距离。理论分析表明，在常规模群体规模与去重条件下，标准SPEA2在OneTrapZeroTrap基准上无法高效覆盖Pareto前沿，而NSGA-II、NSGA-III和SMS-EMOA表现更优。SPEA2$^+$在该基准上恢复了可证明的运行时保证，同时在简单问题上保持原SPEA2性能，证明成对距离能有效提升算法的理论稳健性与实证效率。
Illumination-Robust Camera-Based Heart-Rate Estimation for Physiological Sensing in Robots
Authors: Zhi Wei Xu, Torbjörn E. M. Nordling
First: 2026-06-10T17:42:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:42:31+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physiological awareness is important for service, social, and assistive robots that interact with humans in everyday environments. Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact heart-rate (HR) estimation from an RGB camera, making it a promising sensing modality for robot-mounted vision systems. However, illumination variation remains a major barrier to robust deployment. This paper presents an end-to-end spatial-temporal transformer framework for remote HR estimation on a new dataset with varied illumination. Our estimator integrates PRNet-based 3D face alignment, clip-level illumination augmentation, the Residual Temporal Standardization Module, and controlled hybrid temporal-frequency supervision. The training objective combines a Soft-Shifted Pearson waveform loss with a spectral Kullback-Leibler divergence loss, where a tuned weight ($\mathbfβ$) controls the contribution of frequency-domain heart-rate guidance. Experiments on a static all-level mix protocol covering three illumination levels show that $\mathbfβ=5$ provides the strongest result among the tested beta settings, achieving a best-run HR mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.79 bpm and an HR correlation of 0.982. Compared with the PhysFormer baseline evaluated on our dataset, our estimator reduces HR MAE by 93.6 %, while increasing HR correlation from 0.088 to 0.982, making it usable when illumination varies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向机器人生理感知的抗光照干扰的基于相机的心率估计
生理感知对服务、社交和辅助机器人在日常环境中与人类交互至关重要。远程光电容积描记法（rPPG）可通过RGB相机实现非接触式心率（HR）估计，使其成为机器人搭载视觉系统有前景的传感模式。然而，光照变化仍是其稳健部署的主要障碍。本文提出了一种端到端时空变换器框架，用于在光照变化的新数据集上进行远程HR估计。该估计器集成了基于PRNet的3D人脸对齐、片段级光照增强、残差时序标准化模块及受控的混合时频监督。训练目标结合了软移位皮尔逊波形损失与频谱Kullback-Leibler散度损失，其中调优权重（β）控制频域心率引导的贡献。在覆盖三种光照级别的静态全级别混合协议实验中，β=5在测试的β设置中表现最佳，实现最佳运行HR平均绝对误差（MAE）为0.79 bpm，HR相关系数为0.982。与在本数据集上评估的PhysFormer基线相比，本估计器将HR MAE降低93.6%，同时将HR相关系数从0.088提升至0.982，使其在光照变化时可用。
