To enable robots to reliably estimate human heart rate under varying lighting conditions, this paper proposes an end-to-end spatial-temporal transformer model that integrates 3D face alignment, illumination augmentation, and hybrid temporal-frequency supervision. The model uses a combined loss function with a tunable parameter β to balance waveform and spectral domain guidance, achieving a mean absolute error of 0.79 bpm and HR correlation of 0.982 on a multi-illumination dataset. Compared to the PhysFormer baseline, the proposed method reduces HR MAE by 93.6% and boosts correlation from 0.088 to 0.982, demonstrating robust performance across illumination levels.
为使机器人能在不同光照条件下可靠地估计人类心率，本文提出了一种端到端时空Transformer框架用于远程光电容积描记术。该方法结合3D人脸对齐、光照增强训练、残差时序标准化模块和时频混合监督，采用加权损失函数平衡波形与频谱精度。在包含三种光照级别的数据集上，模型在β=5时达到0.79 bpm的MAE和0.982的心率相关性，相较PhysFormer基线，MAE降低93.6%，相关性从0.088提升至0.982。
Semantically-Aware Diver Activity Recognition Framework for Effective Underwater Multi-Human-Robot Collaboration
Authors: Sadman Sakib Enan, Junaed Sattar
First: 2026-06-10T17:40:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:40:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effective multi-human-robot collaboration is essential for expanding human-led operations in the challenging and high-risk underwater environment. For autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to become true teammates, they must be able to comprehend their surroundings and recognize a diver's activities to offer assistance and ensure safety. Towards this goal, we introduce DAR-Net, a novel transformer-based framework that analyzes complex underwater scenes to classify diver activities. Our contribution lies in a semantically guided learning formulation that couples transformer-based temporal reasoning with pixel-level scene supervision. This multi-loss training strategy explicitly aligns global activity recognition with local human-robot interaction semantics, which is particularly critical in low-visibility underwater conditions. To address the significant challenge of data scarcity in this domain, we present the first-ever Underwater Diver Activity (UDA) dataset, a foundational resource containing over 2,600 annotated images with pixel-level masks. Through rigorous experimental evaluations in a controlled environment, we demonstrate that DAR-Net achieves promising accuracy in recognizing six distinct diver activities, outperforming state-of-the-art models. While this dataset provides a crucial baseline, our work serves as a pioneering step, laying the groundwork for future research and facilitating the development of more intelligent, collaborative underwater robotic systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向有效水下多人-机器人协作的语义感知潜水员活动识别框架
在高风险的水下环境中，实现有效的多人-机器人协作对扩展人类主导的操作至关重要。为使自主水下航行器（AUV）成为真正的协作伙伴，它们必须能够理解周围环境并识别潜水员的活动，以提供协助并确保安全。为此，我们提出了DAR-Net，一种基于Transformer的新型框架，用于分析复杂的水下场景并分类潜水员活动。我们的贡献在于一种语义引导的学习框架，将基于Transformer的时间推理与像素级场景监督相结合。这种多损失训练策略明确地将全局活动识别与局部人-机器人交互语义对齐，这在能见度低的水下环境中尤为重要。为应对该领域数据稀缺的重大挑战，我们首次提出了水下潜水员活动（UDA）数据集，这是一个基础资源，包含超过2600张带有像素级掩码的标注图像。通过在受控环境中的严格实验评估，我们证明DAR-Net在识别六种不同潜水员活动方面表现优异，优于现有最先进模型。尽管该数据集提供了重要基准，但我们的工作是开创性的，为未来研究奠定了基础，并促进了更智能、协作的水下机器人系统的开发。
Summary / 总结
To enable effective underwater collaboration between humans and autonomous robots, this work proposes DAR-Net, a transformer-based framework that recognizes diver activities by integrating temporal reasoning with pixel-level scene supervision. Motivated by the need for semantic understanding in low-visibility underwater environments, the method employs a multi-loss training strategy that aligns global activity classification with local human-robot interaction semantics. Evaluated using the newly created Underwater Diver Activity (UDA) dataset—containing over 2,600 annotated images—DAR-Net achieves superior accuracy in classifying six diver activities compared to existing models, demonstrating its potential for real-world underwater robotic assistance.
为实现水下多人-机器人协作，本文提出DAR-Net框架，通过语义引导学习结合Transformer时序推理与像素级场景监督，识别复杂水下场景中的潜水员活动。该方法采用多损失训练策略，将全局活动分类与局部人机交互语义对齐，特别适用于低能见度环境。在首个水下潜水员活动（UDA）数据集（含2600余张像素级标注图像）上评估，DAR-Net在识别六类潜水员活动时表现优于现有方法，为水下智能协作机器人系统奠定基础。
Verifiable Environments Are LEGO Bricks: Recursive Composition for Reasoning Generalization
Authors: Hao Xiang, Qiaoyu Tang, Le Yu, Yaojie Lu, Xianpei Han, Ben He, Le Sun, Bowen Yu, Peng Wang, Hongyu Lin, Dayiheng Liu
First: 2026-06-10T17:39:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:39:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) with verifiable environments has emerged as a powerful approach for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). While prior research demonstrates that scaling environment quantity improves RL performance, existing manual or individual construction methods suffer from linear scaling limits, thereby hindering scalable reasoning generalization. This paper introduces RACES (\textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{A}utomated \textbf{C}omposition for \textbf{E}nvironment \textbf{S}caling), a framework that conceptualizes verifiable environments as composable building blocks that can be recursively assembled. The key insight is that when the codomain (output type) of one environment matches the domain (input type) of another, they can be automatically fused into a new verifiable environment, enabling recursive composition. RACES is implemented with 300 individual environments and defines a set of composition operators (\textsc{SEQUENTIAL}, \textsc{PARALLEL}, \textsc{SORT}, and \textsc{SELECT}) that induce diverse reasoning patterns. Extensive experiments show that RL training on these composite environments consistently enhances reasoning generalization. Specifically, RACES improves DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B by an average of 3.1 points (from 48.2 to 51.3) and boosts Qwen3-14B performance from 58.8 to 61.1 on six benchmarks, which are unseen during the construction of training environments. Moreover, RACES achieves performance comparable to training on 300 individual environments using only 50 base environments, demonstrating significant efficiency in environment utilization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可验证环境如同乐高积木：递归组合以实现推理泛化
可验证环境的强化学习（RL）已成为增强大语言模型（LLM）推理能力的强大方法。尽管先前研究证明增加环境数量可提升RL性能，但现有手动或逐个构建方法受限于线性扩展瓶颈，阻碍了可扩展推理泛化。本文提出RACES（递归自动化环境扩展），将可验证环境视为可组合的构建模块，支持递归组装。核心思想是：当一个环境的输出类型（余域）与另一个环境的输入类型（定义域）匹配时，可自动融合为新可验证环境，实现递归组合。RACES基于300个独立环境实现，定义了四种组合操作符（顺序、并行、排序、选择），诱导多样推理模式。大量实验表明，RL在这些复合环境中训练可持续提升推理泛化能力。具体而言，RACES使DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B平均提升3.1分（从48.2至51.3），并在六个未在训练环境中出现的基准测试中将Qwen3-14B性能从58.8提升至61.1。此外，RACES仅用50个基础环境即可达到与300个独立环境训练相当的性能，显著提升环境利用效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to scale reasoning generalization in reinforcement learning for large language models without linearly increasing environment construction effort, this paper proposes RACES, a framework that treats verifiable environments as composable LEGO-like blocks. By recursively fusing environments whose output types match input types of others, RACES automatically generates complex composite environments using operators like SEQUENTIAL, PARALLEL, SORT, and SELECT. Experiments show that training on these composites significantly improves reasoning performance across six unseen benchmarks, boosting DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B by 3.1 points and Qwen3-14B by 2.3 points, while achieving comparable results to training on 300 individual environments using only 50 base environments.
为解决强化学习中提升大语言模型推理泛化能力时环境构建成本随数量线性增长的问题，本文提出RACES框架，将可验证环境视为可递归组合的积木单元。通过自动融合输出类型与输入类型匹配的环境，结合SEQUENTIAL、PARALLEL、SORT和SELECT等组合算子，构建复杂复合环境。实验表明，训练于这些复合环境可显著提升推理泛化能力，在六个未见基准上分别使DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B提升3.1分、Qwen3-14B提升2.3分，且仅用50个基础环境即可达到300个环境的性能水平。
UniIntervene: Agentic Intervention for Efficient Real-World Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Haoyuan Deng, Yitong Gao, Yudong Lin, Haichao Liu, Zhenyu Wu, Ziwei Wang
First: 2026-06-10T17:38:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:38:24+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://denghaoyuan123.github.io/UniIntervene-project/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HiL-RL) has emerged as an effective paradigm for real-world robotic manipulation, enabling online policy improvement with human guidance. However, current HiL-RL frameworks remain intervention-intensive, relying on frequent human corrections to redirect the policy out of unproductive exploration, which incurs high labor cost and limits real-world scalability. To address this, we propose UniIntervene, an agentic intervention model that detects unproductive exploration and autonomously recovers the policy toward high-value states, taking over the bulk of interventions from human operators. Specifically, UniIntervene first performs future-conditioned action-value estimation, predicting the latent consequence of the current action and evaluating its induced value, which provides a more stable progress signal. Building on this, a temporal value-risk critic aggregates recent value dynamics and triggers intervention when the estimated value exhibits sustained stagnation or degradation. When intervention is required, UniIntervene retrieves a high-value recovery target from a memory of past intervention episodes and produces executable corrective actions through a goal-conditioned recovery policy. In this way, UniIntervene turns intervention from passive human correction into a value-aware recovery process for efficient real-world RL. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that UniIntervene improves the average success rate by 8.6% while reducing human interventions by 57% relative to state-of-the-art HiL-RL baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniIntervene：面向高效现实世界强化学习的代理干预方法
人类参与的强化学习（HiL-RL）已成为现实世界机器人操作的有效范式，可借助人类指导实现在线策略改进。然而，当前HiL-RL框架仍高度依赖频繁的人类干预以纠正策略脱离低效探索，导致人力成本高昂且限制实际可扩展性。为此，我们提出UniIntervene，一种代理干预模型，可自动检测低效探索并恢复策略至高价值状态，大幅减少人类操作员的干预。具体而言，UniIntervene首先进行未来条件动作价值估计，预测当前动作的潜在后果并评估其诱导价值，提供更稳定的进展信号；在此基础上，时序价值-风险批评器聚合近期价值动态，当估计价值持续停滞或退化时触发干预；当需干预时，UniIntervene从过往干预经验记忆中检索高价值恢复目标，并通过目标条件恢复策略生成可执行的修正动作。从而，UniIntervene将被动的人类修正转化为价值感知的恢复过程，提升现实世界强化学习效率。在多种现实世界操作任务上的广泛实验表明，UniIntervene相较现有HiL-RL基线方法，平均成功率提升8.6%，人类干预减少57%。
Summary / 总结
To reduce human labor in real-world robotic reinforcement learning, UniIntervene introduces an agentic intervention system that autonomously detects unproductive exploration and recovers policy toward high-value states. It first estimates future action-value conditioned on current state, generating a stable progress signal, then uses a temporal value-risk critic to trigger intervention when value stagnates or degrades. Upon intervention, it retrieves past high-value recovery targets and generates corrective actions via a goal-conditioned policy. Experiments show it boosts average success rate by 8.6% while cutting human interventions by 57% compared to state-of-the-art HiL-RL methods.
为降低现实世界机器人强化学习中的人力干预成本，UniIntervene提出一种自主干预机制，自动识别无效率探索并引导策略回归高价值状态。该方法首先通过未来条件动作价值估计提供稳定进度信号，再结合时序价值风险批评器在价值持续停滞或退化时触发干预；干预时从历史干预记忆中检索高价值目标，并通过目标条件恢复策略生成可执行修正动作。实验表明，该方法相较现有HiL-RL方法平均成功率提升8.6%，人力干预减少57%。
A Turbo-Inference Strategy for Object Detection and Instance Segmentation
Authors: Zhen Zhao, Gang Zhang, Xiaolin Hu, Liang Tang
Venue: Computer Vision and Image Understanding, Volume 270, Article 104827, 2026
First: 2026-06-10T17:38:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:38:22+00:00
Comments: Preprint version of an article published in Computer Vision and Image Understanding
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Object detection and instance segmentation tasks are closely related. Existing top-down instance segmentation methods usually follow a detect-then-segment paradigm, where an initial detector is used to recognize and localize objects with bounding boxes, followed by the segmentation of an instance mask within each bounding box. In such methods, the detection accuracy directly influences the subsequent segmentation performance. However, previous research has seldom explored the impact of the instance segmentation task on object detection. In this paper, we present a turbo-inference strategy for the top-down methods that leverages the complementary information between detection and segmentation tasks iteratively. Specifically we design two modules: turbo-detection head and turbo-segmentation head, which facilitate communication between the tasks. The two modules form a closed loop that interlaces the detection and segmentation results without retraining the model. Comprehensive experiments on the COCO, iFLYTEK, and Cityscapes datasets demonstrate that our method substantially enhances both detection and segmentation accuracies with a certain increase in computational cost. The proposed method represents a tradeoff between prediction accuracy and inference speed. Codes are available at https://github.com/zhaozhen2333/Turbo-Learning.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向目标检测与实例分割的加速推理策略
目标检测与实例分割任务密切相关。现有自顶向下的实例分割方法通常遵循“检测后分割”的范式，即先用初始检测器识别并定位物体（生成边界框），再在每个边界框内分割实例掩码。此类方法中，检测精度直接影响后续分割性能。然而，以往研究较少探讨实例分割任务对目标检测的影响。本文提出一种加速推理策略，利用检测与分割任务间的互补信息进行迭代交互。具体设计了两个模块：加速检测头与加速分割头，促进任务间通信。两个模块构成闭环，无需重新训练模型即可交替融合检测与分割结果。在COCO、iFLYTEK和Cityscapes数据集上的综合实验表明，该方法显著提升检测与分割精度，伴随一定计算开销增加。该方法在预测精度与推理速度间取得平衡。代码见https://github.com/zhaozhen2333/Turbo-Learning.git。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited exploration of how instance segmentation feedback can improve object detection, this paper introduces a turbo-inference strategy for top-down methods that iteratively exchanges information between detection and segmentation tasks. It employs two specialized modules — a turbo-detection head and a turbo-segmentation head — to form a closed-loop inference mechanism without retraining. Experiments on COCO, iFLYTEK, and Cityscapes show that this approach significantly boosts both detection and segmentation accuracy, albeit at a moderate increase in computational cost, offering a favorable tradeoff between accuracy and speed.
鉴于现有研究较少探讨实例分割任务对目标检测的反向促进作用，本文提出一种涡轮推理策略，通过在检测与分割任务间迭代传递互补信息来提升性能。该方法设计了涡轮检测头与涡轮分割头两个模块，构建无需重训练的闭环推理机制。在COCO、iFLYTEK和Cityscapes三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了检测与分割精度，同时仅带来轻微的计算开销，实现了精度与推理速度之间的良好平衡。
Breaking Entropy Bounds: Accelerating RL Training via MTP with Rejection Sampling
Authors: Yucheng Li, Huiqiang Jiang, Yang Xu, Jianxin Yang, Yi Zhang, Yizhong Cao, Yuhao Shen, Fan Zhou, Rui Men, Jianwei Zhang, An Yang, Bowen Yu, Bo Zheng, Fei Huang, Junyang Lin, Dayiheng Liu, Jingren Zhou
First: 2026-06-10T17:36:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:36:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component in modern large language models, yet the rollout stage remains the key bottleneck in RL training pipelines. Although Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) offers a natural solution to accelerate rollouts through speculative decoding, many studies have observed that MTP acceptance rates degrade significantly during RL training, leading to limited speedup performance. To address this bottleneck, we present Bebop, a systematic study of MTP in LLM post-training, and offer practical recipes to integrate MTP into large-scale RL pipelines. First, we reveal that the MTP acceptance rate is fundamentally bounded by the fluctuation of model entropy, which demonstrates a clear negative linear relationship with the rise of entropy in the RL stage. Second, we show that probabilistic rejection sampling largely alleviates the disturbance introduced by entropy in RL compared to greedy draft sampling. We further identify that the conventional MTP training objectives (cross-entropy or KL) are suboptimal in such settings, and therefore we propose a novel end-to-end TV loss that directly optimizes multi-step rejection sampling acceptance rate, yielding ~10% acceptance rate improvements, achieving up to 95% acceptance rates and up to 25% extra inference throughput gains across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks. Third, we test various online MTP training strategies during RL and show that pre-RL MTP training with e2e TV loss and rejection sampling achieves a consistent acceptance rate and speedup throughout the entire RL, eliminating the need for costly online MTP updating. We provide extensive experiments and analysis that validate our findings. Experimental results show our method achieves up to 1.8x end-to-end acceleration in async RL training of Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, and Qwen3.7 models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：突破熵界限制：通过MTP与拒绝采样加速强化学习训练
强化学习（RL）已成为现代大语言模型的关键组成部分，但其rollout阶段仍是RL训练流程中的主要瓶颈。尽管多令牌预测（MTP）可通过投机解码自然加速rollout，但许多研究发现，MTP接受率在RL训练过程中显著下降，导致加速效果有限。为解决这一瓶颈，我们提出Bebop，系统研究了LLM后训练中的MTP，并提供将MTP集成到大规模RL流程中的实用方案。首先，我们揭示MTP接受率从根本上受模型熵波动的限制，其与RL阶段熵上升呈明显负线性关系。其次，我们表明概率拒绝采样相比贪心草稿采样，能大幅缓解RL中熵带来的干扰。我们进一步发现，传统MTP训练目标（交叉熵或KL散度）在此场景下次优，因此提出一种新颖的端到端TV损失，直接优化多步拒绝采样接受率，实现约10%接受率提升，最高可达95%接受率，推理吞吐量提升最高达25%，覆盖数学推理、代码生成和代理任务。第三，我们在RL训练中测试了多种在线MTP训练策略，发现采用e2e TV损失与拒绝采样进行预RL MTP训练，可实现整个RL阶段稳定接受率与加速效果，无需昂贵的在线MTP更新。我们提供了大量实验与分析验证上述发现。实验结果表明，该方法在Qwen3.5、Qwen3.6和Qwen3.7模型的异步RL训练中，最高可实现1.8倍端到端加速。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the bottleneck of rollout speed in RL training pipelines, this work investigates Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) with rejection sampling to accelerate inference. The authors find that MTP acceptance rates are limited by model entropy fluctuations, which rise during RL training, and propose a novel TV loss to optimize multi-step rejection sampling directly, achieving up to 10% higher acceptance rates and 25% extra throughput gains across tasks. They also demonstrate that pre-training MTP with this loss eliminates the need for online updates, maintaining consistent speedup throughout RL, with end-to-end acceleration of up to 1.8x on Qwen3.5–3.7 models.
为解决大语言模型强化学习训练中rollout阶段的瓶颈问题，本文研究了多令牌预测（MTP）并提出Bebop方法以提升其效果。作者发现MTP接受率受模型熵波动限制，且与RL阶段熵上升呈负相关。为缓解此问题，他们采用概率拒绝采样替代贪心草案采样，并提出一种新型TV损失函数，直接优化多步拒绝采样的接受率，使接受率提升约10%，最高可达95%，并在数学推理、代码生成和代理任务中带来25%的推理吞吐量增益。此外，他们表明在RL前使用TV损失与拒绝采样进行预训练，可维持整个RL过程中的稳定接受率，无需在线更新。在Qwen3.5至3.7模型的异步RL训练中，实验显示最高可达1.8倍的端到端加速效果。
Should LLM Agents Decide in Social Simulations? Comparing Finite-State and LLM-Based Decision Policies
Authors: Alejandro Buitrago López, Javier Pastor-Galindo, José A. Ruipérez-Valiente
First: 2026-06-10T17:35:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:35:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as decision-making components in social simulations. This introduces a methodological risk: the simulation may deviate from the explicit behavioral policy defined by the researcher. In online social network (OSN) simulations, action choices shape system dynamics, interaction patterns, and model interpretability. This paper evaluates whether LLM action selectors preserve an interpretable reference policy in an OSN simulation. The reference is a finite state machine implemented as a first-order Markov model, with transition probabilities depending on the user type. The evaluation uses a synthetic network with 1,000 agents and 10,000 action decisions. Three open-weight LLMs are tested: LLaMA 3.1, GPT-OSS, and Mistral 24B. Each model is evaluated under three prompting strategies: base, guided, and probabilistic. Alignment is measured using Jensen-Shannon Divergence with Laplace smoothing, and execution time is reported. Results show that LLMs can approximate the reference policy in some configurations, but do not preserve it reliably. Alignment varies across models and prompts, and additional guidance can introduce systematic action biases. Even the best-aligned LLM configurations are several hundred times slower than direct Markov chain sampling. These findings indicate that LLM-based action selection is not a direct replacement for explicit decision policies: it can alter the intended behavior while increasing computational cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型代理应在社会模拟中做决策吗？比较有限状态与基于LLM的决策策略
大型语言模型（LLM）正越来越多地作为社会模拟中的决策组件使用。这引入了一种方法论风险：模拟可能偏离研究者明确定义的行为策略。在在线社交网络（OSN）模拟中，行动选择塑造系统动态、互动模式和模型可解释性。 本文评估了LLM行动选择器是否能在OSN模拟中保持可解释的参考策略。该参考策略是一个作为一阶马尔可夫模型实现的有限状态机，其转移概率取决于用户类型。评估使用了一个包含1000个代理和10000次行动决策的合成网络。测试了三种开源LLM：LLaMA 3.1、GPT-OSS和Mistral 24B。每个模型在三种提示策略下评估：基础、引导和概率。对齐度使用带拉普拉斯平滑的Jensen-Shannon散度衡量，同时报告执行时间。结果显示，LLM在某些配置下可近似参考策略，但不能可靠保持该策略。对齐度因模型和提示策略而异，额外引导可能引入系统性行动偏差。即使最佳对齐的LLM配置，其速度也比直接马尔可夫链采样慢数百倍。这些发现表明，基于LLM的行动选择不能直接替代显式决策策略：它可能改变预期行为并增加计算成本。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can reliably replicate a predefined finite-state decision policy in online social network simulations, where action choices critically influence system dynamics. The researchers compare three open-weight LLMs—LLaMA 3.1, GPT-OSS, and Mistral 24B—under three prompting strategies (base, guided, probabilistic) against a reference Markov model with user-type-dependent transition probabilities. Evaluated on a synthetic network of 1,000 agents making 10,000 decisions, alignment is measured via Jensen-Shannon Divergence with Laplace smoothing, and execution time is recorded. Results show that while some LLM configurations achieve moderate alignment, none consistently preserve the reference policy; guided prompts often introduce systematic biases, and even the best-performing models are hundreds of times slower than direct Markov sampling, suggesting LLMs are not a direct substitute for explicit decision policies.
该研究探讨大语言模型（LLM）是否能在在线社交网络模拟中可靠地复现预设的有限状态决策策略，因为行动选择对系统动态至关重要。研究人员对比了三种开源LLM（LLaMA 3.1、GPT-OSS、Mistral 24B）在三种提示策略（基础、引导、概率）下的表现，与一个基于用户类型转移概率的参考马尔可夫模型进行对比，模拟包含1000个代理和10000次决策。通过Jensen-Shannon散度（Laplace平滑）衡量对齐度，并记录执行时间。结果表明，尽管某些LLM配置可近似参考策略，但无法稳定保持，引导策略常引入系统性行动偏差。即使最优对齐配置也比直接马尔可夫采样慢数百倍，说明LLM不能直接替代显式决策策略，否则会改变预期行为并增加计算开销。
DepthMaster: Unified Monocular Depth Estimation for Perspective and Panoramic Images
Authors: Pengfei Wang, Shihao Wang, Liyi Chen, Zhiyuan Ma, Guowen Zhang, Lei Zhang
First: 2026-06-10T17:35:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:35:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While monocular depth estimation has achieved significant progress, achieving generalized metric depth estimation for both narrow field-of-view (FoV) perspectives and $360^\circ$ panoramas remains an unsolved challenge. Existing methods are often tailored to specific camera types and struggle to produce accurate metric depth that generalizes across diverse settings. This limitation stems from two key challenges: the inherent geometric discrepancy between perspective and panoramic cameras, and the scarcity of panoramic training data with metric annotations. In this work, we introduce DepthMaster, a unified metric depth estimation framework. Rather than employing specialized networks to learn spherical distortions, we reformulate the problem by decomposing panoramic images into overlapping perspective patches. Crucially, distinct from prior projection-based methods that rely on ad-hoc architectural modifications to handle boundaries, we introduce a novel Correspondence Consistency Loss (CCL) and inject virtual projection cameras as geometric priors, allowing us to seamlessly stitch the patches while avoiding specialized operators and keeping the backbone largely compatible with standard Transformer designs. This strategy also resolves the geometric differences by unifying all inputs into a canonical perspective representation, and effectively circumvents data scarcity by directly unlocking powerful metric priors from vast perspective datasets. Trained on a mixed dataset that contains only one panorama dataset, DepthMaster achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on 13 diverse datasets, outperforming not only universal methods but also leading specialist models in both perspective and panoramic domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DepthMaster：面向透视图与全景图的统一单目深度估计
尽管单目深度估计已取得显著进展，但实现适用于窄视角（FoV）透视图与360°全景图的通用度量深度估计仍是一个未解决的挑战。现有方法常针对特定相机类型设计，难以在多样场景中生成准确且泛化的度量深度。这一局限源于两大关键挑战：透视相机与全景相机固有的几何差异，以及带度量标注的全景训练数据稀缺。本文提出DepthMaster，一种统一的度量深度估计框架。我们摒弃专用网络学习球面畸变，转而将全景图像分解为重叠的透视块。与以往依赖人工架构修改处理边界投影的方法不同，我们引入新颖的对应一致性损失（CCL），并注入虚拟投影相机作为几何先验，从而无缝拼接块状图像，避免专用算子，同时保持主干网络与标准Transformer架构高度兼容。该策略通过将所有输入统一为规范透视表示，解决几何差异；并通过直接利用海量透视数据中的强大度量先验，有效缓解数据稀缺问题。在仅含一个全景数据集的混合训练集上训练，DepthMaster在13个多样化数据集上实现零样本最优性能，不仅超越通用方法，也在透视与全景领域领先专用模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of generalized metric depth estimation across perspective and panoramic images, DepthMaster proposes a unified framework that decomposes panoramic images into overlapping perspective patches. Instead of adapting architectures for spherical distortion, it introduces a Correspondence Consistency Loss and virtual projection cameras as geometric priors to stitch patches seamlessly while preserving compatibility with standard Transformer backbones. This approach resolves geometric discrepancies by converting all inputs to a canonical perspective representation and leverages abundant perspective datasets to overcome panoramic data scarcity. Experimental results show that DepthMaster achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on 13 diverse datasets, surpassing both universal and domain-specific models.
为解决单目深度估计在透视图与全景图间缺乏通用性的问题，DepthMaster提出统一框架，将全景图分解为重叠的透视块。它不依赖针对球面畸变的专用网络，而是引入对应一致性损失与虚拟投影相机作为几何先验，无缝拼接块并保持与标准Transformer架构兼容。该方法通过统一输入为透视表示解决几何差异，并利用大量透视数据缓解全景数据稀缺问题。实验表明，DepthMaster在13个多样化数据集上实现零样本最优性能，超越通用方法与领域专用模型。
APT: Action Expert Pretraining Improves Instruction Generalization of Vision-Language-Action Policies
Authors: Kechun Xu, Zhenjie Zhu, Anzhe Chen, Rong Xiong, Yue Wang
First: 2026-06-10T17:34:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:34:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models that couple pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with continuous action experts have achieved strong manipulation performance, yet generalization to out-of-distribution (OOD) language instructions remains poor. A known challenge is the structural imbalance in VLA data, where language is far less diverse than visual and action content, making policies prone to visual shortcuts. While discrete-action methods mitigate this through vision-language co-training, continuous action experts lack such protection: they start from random initialization and learn entirely from imbalanced data, producing noisy gradients that corrupt the VLM and fail to exploit its language capability. We address this from a Bayesian perspective, factorizing the policy into a language-agnostic Vision-Action (VA) prior and a language-conditioned VLA likelihood, and propose APT, a two-stage training method emphasizing Action expert PreTraining. In Stage 1, the action expert is pretrained as a VA prior on vision-action pairs from a frozen VLM, bypassing the language imbalance. In Stage 2, language tokens are injected through a gated fusion mechanism that integrates VLM features while preserving the learned visuomotor prior. APT applies to mainstream VLA architectures, including the $π$ and GR00T-style architectures. Comprehensive experiments validate that APT achieves consistent gains on unseen instructions and compositional tasks. Project Page: https://xukechun.github.io/papers/APT/
中文标题/摘要
标题：APT：动作专家预训练可提升视觉-语言-动作策略对指令的泛化能力
将预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）与连续动作专家结合的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已取得优异的操控性能，但对分布外（OOD）语言指令的泛化能力仍较差。已知挑战在于VLA数据结构失衡，语言内容远不如视觉和动作内容多样，导致策略易依赖视觉捷径。虽然离散动作方法可通过视觉-语言联合训练缓解此问题，但连续动作专家缺乏此类保护：它们从随机初始化开始，完全依赖失衡数据学习，产生噪声梯度，破坏VLM并无法利用其语言能力。我们从贝叶斯视角解决此问题，将策略分解为语言无关的视觉-动作（VA）先验和语言条件化的VLA似然，并提出APT，一种强调动作专家预训练的两阶段训练方法。第一阶段，动作专家在冻结的VLM上以视觉-动作对预训练为VA先验，绕过语言失衡；第二阶段，通过门控融合机制注入语言标记，整合VLM特征同时保留已学习的视觉-运动先验。APT适用于主流VLA架构，包括π和GR00T风格架构。全面实验验证APT在未见指令和组合任务上均取得稳定提升。项目页面：https://xukechun.github.io/papers/APT/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models to out-of-distribution language instructions due to structural data imbalance favoring visual and action content over language, the paper proposes APT, a two-stage training method that decouples the policy into a language-agnostic Vision-Action prior and a language-conditioned likelihood. In Stage 1, the action expert is pretrained on vision-action pairs from a frozen VLM to avoid language imbalance and noisy gradients. In Stage 2, language tokens are fused via a gated mechanism while preserving the learned visuomotor prior. Experiments across mainstream VLA architectures show APT consistently improves performance on unseen instructions and compositional tasks, demonstrating robustness and generalization gains.
为了解决视觉-语言-动作模型在面对分布外语言指令时泛化能力差的问题——这源于数据中视觉和动作内容远比语言更丰富，导致模型易依赖视觉捷径——本文提出APT方法，将策略分解为语言无关的视觉-动作先验和语言条件化的联合分布。第一阶段，动作专家在冻结的视觉语言模型上预训练，学习视觉-动作映射，避开语言不平衡的影响；第二阶段，通过门控融合机制注入语言信息，同时保留已学习的视觉-动作先验。实验表明，APT在主流VLA架构上显著提升了对未见指令和组合任务的性能。
Ambient Diffusion Policy: Imitation Learning from Suboptimal Data in Robotics
Authors: Adam Wei, Nicholas Pfaff, Thomas Cohn, Arif Kerem Dayı, Constantinos Daskalakis, Giannis Daras, Russ Tedrake
First: 2026-06-10T17:34:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:34:12+00:00
Comments: 14 pages (main body), 52 pages total. Project website: https://ambient-diffusion-policy.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We propose Ambient Diffusion Policy, a simple and principled method for imitation learning from suboptimal data in robotics. High-quality, task-specific robot data is expensive and time-consuming to collect, while suboptimal datasets with lower-quality or out-of-distribution demonstrations are abundant. Existing methods that co-train on both data sources in robotics often fail to separate the meaningful and the harmful features in the suboptimal samples. In contrast, our method extracts only the useful features by introducing a new axis to co-training in robotics: noise-dependent data usage. Ambient Diffusion Policy restricts the contribution of suboptimal data during training to only the high and low diffusion times. To rigorously justify our approach, we first observe that robot action data exhibits a spectral power law. This induces two important properties on the optimal Diffusion Policy that we exploit: a global-to-local hierarchy and locality. We theoretically formalize this discussion using a simplified model. Our experiments validate Ambient Diffusion Policy on four types of suboptimal action data (noisy trajectories, sim-to-real gap, task mismatch, and large-scale data mixtures) across six tasks. The results show that it effectively learns from arbitrary sources of suboptimal data. Notably, it outperforms existing co-training baselines by up to 33% when scaled to Open X-Embodiment - a large dataset with heterogeneous data quality and unstructured distribution shifts. Overall, Ambient Diffusion Policy increases the utility of suboptimal demonstrations and expands the set of usable data sources in robotics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：环境扩散策略：从次优数据中进行机器人模仿学习
我们提出了环境扩散策略，这是一种简单且有原则的方法，用于从机器人领域的次优数据中进行模仿学习。高质量、任务特定的机器人数据收集成本高且耗时，而次优数据（质量较低或分布外的演示）则丰富易得。现有同时训练两种数据源的方法往往无法区分次优样本中的有益与有害特征。相比之下，我们的方法通过引入一个新的轴——噪声依赖的数据使用——来协同训练，仅提取有用特征。环境扩散策略限制次优数据在训练中的贡献，仅在高和低扩散时间点生效。为严谨论证该方法，我们首先观察到机器人动作数据呈现谱幂律特性。这诱导出最优扩散策略的两个关键属性——全局到局部的层次结构和局部性——我们加以利用。我们通过简化模型对其进行理论形式化。实验在六个任务中验证了环境扩散策略对四种次优动作数据（噪声轨迹、仿真到现实差距、任务不匹配、大规模数据混合）的有效性。结果表明，该方法能从任意次优数据源中有效学习。值得注意的是，当扩展至Open X-Embodiment（一个数据质量异质且分布无结构偏移的大规模数据集）时，其性能比现有协同训练基线高33%。总体而言，环境扩散策略提升了次优演示的效用，并扩展了机器人领域可用的数据源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of high-quality robot data and the abundance of suboptimal demonstrations, the paper introduces Ambient Diffusion Policy, a method that selectively leverages suboptimal data by restricting its influence to specific diffusion times during training. It exploits the spectral power law observed in robot action data to theoretically derive a global-to-local hierarchy and locality in optimal policies. Experiments across six robotic tasks and four types of suboptimal data—noisy trajectories, sim-to-real gap, task mismatch, and heterogeneous data mixtures—show that the method effectively learns from noisy sources and outperforms existing co-training baselines by up to 33% on Open X-Embodiment, a large-scale dataset with varied data quality and distribution shifts.
为降低高质量机器人数据收集成本并利用大量低质量或分布偏移的演示数据，本文提出Ambient Diffusion Policy方法，通过根据扩散时间限制次优数据的贡献来选择性利用其信息。该方法基于机器人动作数据中观察到的幂律频谱特性，理论推导出最优策略的全局到局部层次结构与局部性。实验表明，该方法在四种次优数据类型（噪声轨迹、仿真到现实差距、任务错配、混合数据）和六个机器人任务上表现稳健，在Open X-Embodiment大型异构数据集上相较现有协同训练基线最高提升33%。
On Subquadratic Architectures: From Applications to Principles
Authors: Anamaria-Roberta Hartl, Levente Zólyomi, David Stap, Pieter-Jan Hoedt, Niklas Schmidinger, Lukas Hauzenberger, Sebastian Böck, Günter Klambauer, Sepp Hochreiter
First: 2026-06-10T17:33:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:33:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformers dominate modern sequence modeling, but their quadratic attention incurs substantial computational cost. Subquadratic architectures offer a scalable alternative. However, it remains unclear which designs yield the most effective sequence models. We compare three leading approaches: xLSTM, Mamba-2, and Gated DeltaNet. We evaluate these models on tasks with complex dependencies: (1) code-model pre-training, (2) distillation of code models from large language models, and (3) pre-training of time-series foundation models. Across these settings, xLSTM delivers the strongest overall performance. To explain xLSTM's advantage, we present a unified formulation and analyze the underlying architectural mechanisms, focusing on state tracking and memory dynamics. Our results show that xLSTM enables more flexible and stable memory correction via its gating scheme. We corroborate these findings on controlled synthetic length-generalization tasks. Overall, our findings indicate that xLSTM's gains on complex tasks stem from robust state tracking and accumulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：次二次架构：从应用到原理
Transformer在现代序列建模中占据主导地位，但其二次注意力机制带来显著计算开销。次二次架构提供了可扩展的替代方案。然而，尚不清楚哪些设计能产生最有效的序列模型。我们比较了三种主流方法：xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet，并在具有复杂依赖关系的任务上评估这些模型：(1) 代码模型预训练，(2) 从大语言模型蒸馏代码模型，(3) 时间序列基础模型预训练。在这些场景中，xLSTM整体表现最佳。为解释xLSTM的优势，我们提出统一公式并分析其底层架构机制，重点关注状态跟踪与记忆动态。结果表明，xLSTM通过其门控机制实现了更灵活且稳定的记忆修正。我们在受控的合成长度泛化任务中验证了这些发现。总体而言，我们的研究结果表明，xLSTM在复杂任务中的优势源于其稳健的状态跟踪与累积能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of quadratic attention in Transformers, this study evaluates three subquadratic architectures—xLSTM, Mamba-2, and Gated DeltaNet—on complex sequence modeling tasks including code pre-training, distillation, and time-series foundation model training. xLSTM consistently outperforms the others across all benchmarks. The authors attribute this to xLSTM’s gating mechanism, which enables more flexible and stable memory correction through improved state tracking and accumulation, as demonstrated in both empirical results and controlled synthetic experiments.
鉴于Transformer中二次注意力机制的高计算开销，本研究在代码预训练、模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练等复杂任务上对比了xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet三种次二次架构。xLSTM在所有基准测试中表现最优。作者将其优势归因于其门控机制，该机制通过稳健的状态追踪与累积，实现更灵活且稳定的记忆修正，这一结论通过统一公式和受控合成实验得到验证。
Learning Patterns and Abstractions from Perceptual Sequences
Authors: Shuchen Wu
First: 2025-03-14T00:37:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:31:58+00:00
Comments: Doctoral thesis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cognition swiftly breaks high-dimensional sensory streams into familiar parts and uncovers their relations. Why do structures emerge, and how do they enable learning, generalization, and prediction? What computational principles underlie this core aspect of perception and intelligence? A sensory stream, simplified, is a one-dimensional sequence. In learning such sequences, we naturally segment them into parts -- a process known as chunking. In the first project, I investigated factors influencing chunking in a serial reaction time task and showed that humans adapt to underlying chunks while balancing speed and accuracy. Building on this, I developed models that learn chunks and parse sequences chunk by chunk. Normatively, I proposed chunking as a rational strategy for discovering recurring patterns and nested hierarchies, enabling efficient sequence factorization. Learned chunks serve as reusable primitives for transfer, composition, and mental simulation -- letting the model compose the new from the known. I demonstrated this model's ability to learn hierarchies in single and multi-dimensional sequences and highlighted its utility for unsupervised pattern discovery. The second part moves from concrete to abstract sequences. I taxonomized abstract motifs and examined their role in sequence memory. Behavioral evidence suggests that humans exploit pattern redundancies for compression and transfer. I proposed a non-parametric hierarchical variable model that learns both chunks and abstract variables, uncovering invariant symbolic patterns. I showed its similarity to human learning and compared it to large language models. Taken together, this thesis suggests that chunking and abstraction as simple computational principles enable structured knowledge acquisition in hierarchically organized sequences, from simple to complex, concrete to abstract.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从感知序列中学习模式与抽象
认知迅速将高维感官流分解为熟悉的部分并揭示其关系。结构为何会产生，它们如何促进学习、泛化与预测？这些感知与智能核心方面的计算原理是什么？简化而言，感官流是一维序列。在学习此类序列时，我们自然将其分割为部分——这一过程称为分块。在第一个项目中，我研究了序列反应时间任务中影响分块的因素，表明人类在速度与准确性之间权衡时会适应底层分块。在此基础上，我开发了分块学习与逐块解析序列的模型。规范性上，我提出分块是一种理性策略，用于发现重复模式与嵌套层次结构，从而实现高效序列分解。学习到的分块作为可复用的基本单元，支持迁移、组合与心理模拟——使模型能从已知中组合出新内容。我展示了该模型在单维与多维序列中学习层次结构的能力，并强调其在无监督模式发现中的实用性。第二部分转向抽象序列。我分类了抽象模式并考察其在序列记忆中的作用。行为证据表明，人类利用模式冗余实现压缩与迁移。我提出了一种非参数层次变量模型，同时学习分块与抽象变量，揭示不变的符号模式。我展示了该模型与人类学习的相似性，并与大型语言模型进行了比较。总体而言，本论文表明，分块与抽象作为简单的计算原理，使模型能够在层次化组织的序列中，从简单到复杂、从具体到抽象地获取结构化知识。
Summary / 总结
This thesis investigates how humans and models learn structured patterns from perceptual sequences by chunking them into reusable primitives. Motivated by the need to understand the computational principles underlying perception and generalization, the author develops normative models that segment sequences into chunks to enable efficient learning and prediction. Experiments show that humans adaptively chunk sequences while balancing speed and accuracy, and that learned chunks support hierarchical representation, transfer, and mental simulation. The work further extends to abstract sequences, proposing a non-parametric hierarchical model that discovers invariant symbolic patterns, demonstrating strong alignment with human behavior and outperforming large language models in structured sequence learning.
该论文探讨了人类和模型如何通过将感知序列分块为可复用的原始单元来学习结构化模式。研究动机在于理解感知与泛化背后的基本计算机制，作者开发了分块与层次抽象模型，实验证明分块能高效分解序列并支持迁移学习、心理模拟与模式压缩。提出的非参数层次变量模型能捕捉不变的符号模式，与人类行为一致，并在抽象序列学习中优于大型语言模型。
Latent World Recovery for Multimodal Learning with Missing Modalities
Authors: Hui Wang, Tianyu Ren, Joseph Butler, Christopher Baker, Karen Rafferty, Simon McDade
First: 2026-06-10T17:31:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:31:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study multimodal learning under missing modalities, with particular motivation from bioscience applications in which heterogeneous modalities are often only partially available when decisions need to be made. We propose Latent World Recovery (LWR), a framework built on two key ideas: (i) modality-specific embeddings from different modalities are aligned in a shared latent space, and (ii) a unified representation is constructed by fusing only the embeddings of the modalities that are actually available at both training and inference time. Rather than imputing missing modalities or requiring a fixed modality set, LWR treats each modality as a partial perception of an underlying latent state and performs availability-aware representation learning directly from the observed modalities. This combination of neighbor-based latent alignment and availability-aware modality fusion enables robust multimodal prediction under partial observation, while avoiding error propagation from explicit reconstruction of missing modalities. We evaluate the proposed framework on real-world incomplete multi-omics benchmarks and demonstrate that it provides an effective approach to downstream tasks such as cancer phenotype classification and survival prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于潜在世界恢复的缺失模态多模态学习
我们研究在模态缺失情况下的多模态学习，特别受生物科学应用的启发，其中在需要决策时，异构模态往往仅部分可用。我们提出潜在世界恢复（LWR）框架，基于两个核心思想：（i）不同模态的模态特异性嵌入在共享的潜在空间中对齐；（ii）通过仅融合训练和推理时实际可用的模态嵌入构建统一表示。LWR不依赖模态填补或固定模态集，而是将每个模态视为对潜在状态的部分感知，并直接从观测到的模态中进行可用性感知的表示学习。这种结合邻域潜在对齐与可用性感知模态融合的方法，能够在部分观测下实现鲁棒的多模态预测，同时避免因显式重建缺失模态而导致的误差传播。我们在现实世界中的不完整多组学基准数据上评估了该框架，证明其在癌症表型分类和生存预测等下游任务中具有有效性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by real-world bioscience scenarios where multimodal data is often incomplete, the paper proposes Latent World Recovery (LWR), a framework that aligns modality-specific embeddings into a shared latent space and constructs unified representations by fusing only the available modalities during both training and inference. Unlike methods that impute missing data or require fixed modality sets, LWR treats each modality as a partial observation of an underlying latent state, enabling availability-aware representation learning. Experimental results on real-world incomplete multi-omics datasets show that LWR achieves robust performance in downstream tasks such as cancer phenotype classification and survival prediction, without relying on explicit reconstruction of missing modalities, thereby avoiding error propagation.
针对生物科学中多模态数据常不完整的真实场景，本文提出Latent World Recovery（LWR）框架，通过将不同模态的嵌入对齐到共享潜在空间，并仅融合训练与推理时实际存在的模态构建统一表示。与依赖缺失模态填补或固定模态集合的方法不同，LWR将每种模态视为潜在状态的部分观测，直接从现有模态学习可用感知表示，避免了显式重建缺失模态带来的误差传播。在真实世界不完整多组学数据集上的实验表明，LWR在癌症表型分类和生存预测等下游任务中表现稳健，优于依赖填补或固定模态的基线方法。
Anatomy of Post-Training: Using Interpretability to Characterize Data and Shape the Learning Signal
Authors: Leon Bergen, Usha Bhalla, Sidharth Baskaran, Max Loeffler, Raphael Sarfati, Dhruvil Gala, Ryan Panwar, Santiago Aranguri, Thomas Fel, Atticus Geiger, Matthew Kowal, Siddharth Boppana, Daniel Balsam, Owen Lewis, Jack Merullo, Thomas McGrath, Ekdeep Singh Lubana
First: 2026-06-10T17:31:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:31:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language-model post-training is the main stage at which model behavior is shaped, yet it still largely involves optimization of scalar rewards that summarize diverse desiderata. This abstraction gives practitioners little visibility into what their data actually teaches models, allowing spurious correlations to be learned by a model and inducing undesirable behaviors such as over-stylization and sycophancy. To address this problem, we ask: can we inspect a preference dataset before optimization and decide, at the level of concepts, which behaviors a model should be allowed to learn? Motivated by this, we introduce a data-centric post-training pipeline that uses interpretability protocols to develop statistical hypotheses for the latent concepts separating preferred from dispreferred generations, making them explicit for fine-grained user feedback. Building on this view, we unify several interpretability-based training protocols as ways of shaping rewards via feature or data interventions. Empirically, we show that our pipeline diagnoses undesirable signals in existing preference data, mitigates off-target learning, and can also help amplify or shape desired properties such as safeguards and model personality. More broadly, our results suggest that interpretability can turn post-training from optimizing opaque proxy rewards into a process of auditing and sculpting the learning signal itself.
中文标题/摘要
标题：后训练的解剖学：利用可解释性刻画数据并塑造学习信号
语言模型的后训练是塑造模型行为的主要阶段，但其仍主要依赖于对多样化目标进行总结的标量奖励优化。这种抽象使从业者难以看清数据实际教会模型什么，导致模型学习虚假相关性并产生如过度风格化和谄媚等不良行为。为解决此问题，我们提出：能否在优化前检查偏好数据，并在概念层面决定模型应被允许学习哪些行为？为此，我们引入一种以数据为中心的后训练流程，利用可解释性协议建立关于区分偏好与非偏好生成的潜在概念的统计假设，使其可被用户进行细粒度反馈。在此基础上，我们将多种基于可解释性的训练协议统一为通过特征或数据干预塑造奖励的方法。实证表明，我们的流程可诊断现有偏好数据中的不良信号，减轻偏离目标的学习，同时也能放大或塑造如安全机制和模型个性等期望属性。更广泛地说，我们的结果表明，可解释性可将后训练从优化模糊的代理奖励转变为对学习信号本身的审计与塑造过程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of transparency in language model post-training, where scalar rewards obscure what data actually teaches models, this work proposes a data-centric pipeline that uses interpretability techniques to identify and characterize latent concepts distinguishing preferred from dispreferred generations. By making these concepts explicit, the method enables fine-grained user feedback and unifies various interpretability-based training protocols as tools to shape rewards through feature or data interventions. Experiments show the pipeline successfully diagnoses spurious signals in existing preference data, reduces off-target learning, and can enhance desirable traits like safeguards or personality.
为了解决语言模型后训练中奖励函数抽象化导致的模型行为不可控、易学习虚假关联的问题，该研究提出一种以数据为中心的后训练流程，利用可解释性技术识别区分偏好与非偏好生成的潜在概念，并通过统计假设使其显式化，便于用户进行细粒度反馈。该方法将多种基于可解释性的训练协议统一为通过特征或数据干预塑造奖励的手段。实验证明，该流程能有效诊断现有偏好数据中的不良信号，抑制偏离目标的学习，同时增强如安全机制或模型个性等期望属性。
Beyond Uniform Token-Level Trust Region in LLM Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Renjie Mao, Xiangxin Zhou, Lvfang Tao, Yixin Ding, Yu Shi, Yongguang Lin, Yuheng Wu, Honglin Zhu, Qian Qiu, Wenxi Zhu
First: 2026-06-09T15:09:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:30:04+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://hunyuan-cppo.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become standard for improving LLM reasoning. However, existing PPO-style trust-region mechanisms remain position-agnostic by enforcing uniform thresholds across all tokens independently. This pointwise treatment conflicts with autoregressive generation in two critical ways. First, uniform thresholds ignore autoregressive asymmetry. Early-stage deviations produce compounding sequence-level drift, causing static thresholds to under-regulate early divergence and excessively constrain late-stage exploration. Second, evaluating token-level divergence in isolation overlooks cumulative prefix drift, granting the same divergence allowance regardless of how far the conditioning history has already deviated from the rollout policy. To address this limitation, we propose CPPO (Cumulative Prefix-divergence Policy Optimization), a token-level masking rule that aligns updates with a finite-horizon policy-improvement bound via two coupled mechanisms. First, a position-weighted threshold imposes stricter limits at early positions whose effects persist longer, relaxing constraints for late-stage tokens. Second, a cumulative prefix budget tracks historical deviations, dynamically restricting further token-level deviation to prevent compounding errors along the prefix. Empirically, CPPO enhances training stability and significantly improves reasoning accuracy across various model scales.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越统一的令牌级信任区域的LLM强化学习
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的标准方法。然而，现有的PPO风格信任区域机制仍保持位置无关性，通过在所有令牌上独立施加统一阈值。这种逐点处理方式与自回归生成存在两个关键冲突：首先，统一阈值忽略了自回归生成的不对称性，早期阶段的偏差会导致序列级累积漂移，使静态阈值对早期偏差调节不足，而对后期探索过度约束；其次，孤立评估令牌级偏差忽略了前缀累积漂移，无论条件历史已偏离 rollout 策略多远，均给予相同的偏差容许度。为解决这一局限，我们提出CPPO（累积前缀偏差策略优化），一种令牌级掩码规则，通过两种耦合机制，使更新与有限时间跨度内的策略改进边界对齐。首先，位置加权阈值对早期位置施加更严格限制（其影响持续更久），对后期令牌则放松约束；其次，累积前缀预算追踪历史偏差，动态限制后续令牌级偏差，防止前缀方向上的累积误差。实验证明，CPPO提升了训练稳定性，并显著改善了各类模型规模下的推理准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of uniform token-level trust regions in PPO-style RLVR for LLMs, which fail to account for autoregressive asymmetry and cumulative prefix drift, the authors propose CPPO, a method that introduces position-weighted thresholds and cumulative prefix budgets to align updates with finite-horizon policy improvement. Early tokens are subject to stricter constraints due to their longer-lasting influence, while later tokens are more permissive. The cumulative prefix budget dynamically limits token divergence based on historical deviation, preventing compounding errors. Experiments show CPPO improves training stability and reasoning accuracy across multiple model scales.
为了解决现有统一令牌级信任区域机制在LLM强化学习中忽略自回归序列动态的问题，本文提出CPPO方法，通过位置加权阈值和累积前缀预算，使策略更新与有限时域改进边界对齐。该方法在早期收紧约束、后期放松约束，并根据累积前缀偏差动态限制令牌级偏差，从而抑制累积误差。实验表明，CPPO在多个模型规模上显著提升了训练稳定性与推理准确性。
CHORUS: Decentralized Multi-Embodiment Collaboration with One VLA Policy
Authors: Ria Doshi, Tian Gao, Annie Chen, Chelsea Finn, Jeannette Bohg
First: 2026-06-10T17:26:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:26:08+00:00
Comments: Project Website: https://chorus-model.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multi-robot collaboration allows robots to efficiently take on a wide range of tasks, from moving a couch through a doorway to assembling structures on a construction site. However, achieving such coordination in mobile multi-robot settings remains challenging: centralized methods conditioned on the combined observations of a team scale poorly with team size, and decentralized methods that train one policy per robot often require explicit alignment procedures or information sharing at inference time to overcome partial observability. Our key insight is that the visuomotor priors of pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models should enable reactive, decentralized collaboration from each robot's local observations alone, without these inference-time assumptions. We propose CHORUS, a framework that adapts a single VLA backbone to control diverse, multi-robot teams. At inference time, each robot runs an independent copy of CHORUS, conditioned only on its own observations and a robot-identifying prompt. In real-world experiments including mobile tape measurement, library book handovers, and laundry basket lifting, CHORUS achieves a 64% point improvement over decentralized, from-scratch models, improves reactivity to teammate behavior by 40% points, and outperforms centralized baselines. Together, these results show that a shared VLA backbone is capable of achieving decentralized multi-robot collaboration, without per-robot policies or inter-robot communication at inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CHORUS：基于单一VLA策略的去中心化多具身协作
多机器人协作使机器人能够高效完成多种任务，如将沙发穿过门框或在建筑工地组装结构。然而，在移动多机器人环境中实现此类协调仍具挑战：集中式方法依赖团队综合观测，其规模随团队增大而急剧下降；去中心化方法通常为每个机器人训练独立策略，需在推理时进行显式对齐或信息共享以克服部分可观测性。我们的核心见解是：预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的视觉运动先验应能支持每个机器人仅基于自身局部观测实现反应式、去中心化协作，无需推理时的额外假设。我们提出CHORUS框架，将单一VLA主干适配用于控制多样化的多机器人团队。推理时，每个机器人独立运行CHORUS副本，仅依赖自身观测和机器人标识提示。在真实世界实验（如移动卷尺测量、图书馆书籍交接、洗衣篮搬运）中，CHORUS相较从零训练的去中心化模型提升64%，对队友行为反应性提升40%，并优于集中式基线方法。这些结果表明，共享VLA主干可实现去中心化多机器人协作，无需每个机器人独立策略或推理时机器人间通信。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of centralized coordination and the complexity of decentralized methods in multi-robot systems, the authors propose CHORUS, a framework that leverages a single pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policy to enable decentralized collaboration across diverse robots. Each robot independently runs its own copy of CHORUS, conditioned only on local observations and a robot-identifying prompt, eliminating the need for explicit alignment or inter-robot communication at inference. Experimental results across real-world tasks—such as tape measurement, book handovers, and laundry basket lifting—show CHORUS outperforms decentralized from-scratch models by 64% in task success rate, improves reactivity to teammates by 40%, and surpasses centralized baselines, demonstrating that a shared VLA backbone can effectively support decentralized multi-robot coordination without per-robot policies or communication.
为解决移动机器人协同中缺乏中心化控制或通信的问题，作者提出CHORUS框架，利用单一预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略控制多机器人。每个机器人仅根据自身观测和机器人标识提示独立运行CHORUS，无需显式对齐或通信，即可实现反应式协作。在实际任务如卷尺测量、图书传递和洗衣篮搬运中，CHORUS相比从零训练的分布式模型提升64%，优于集中式基线，且团队反应性提升40%。
Certifiable Safe RLHF: Semantic Grounding and Fixed Penalty Constraint Optimization for Safer LLM Alignment
Authors: Kartik Pandit, Sourav Ganguly, Arnesh Banerjee, Shaahin Angizi, Arnob Ghosh
First: 2025-10-03T21:24:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:21:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ensuring safety is a foundational requirement for large language models (LLMs). Achieving an appropriate balance between enhancing the utility of model outputs and mitigating their potential for harm is a complex and persistent challenge. Contemporary approaches frequently formalize this problem within the framework of Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) and employ established CMDP optimization techniques. However, these methods exhibit two notable limitations. First, their reliance on reward and cost functions renders performance highly sensitive to the underlying scoring mechanism, which must capture semantic meaning rather than being triggered by superficial keywords. Second, CMDP-based training entails tuning dual-variable, a process that is both computationally expensive and does not provide any provable safety guarantee for a fixed dual variable that can be exploitable through adversarial jailbreaks. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Certifiable Safe-RLHF (CS-RLHF) that introduces a cost model trained on a large-scale corpus to assign semantically grounded safety scores. In contrast to the lagrangian-based approach, CS-RLHF adopts a rectified penalty-based formulation. This design draws on the theory of exact penalty functions in constrained optimization, wherein constraint satisfaction is enforced directly through a suitably chosen penalty term. With an appropriately scaled penalty, feasibility of the safety constraints can be guaranteed at the optimizer, eliminating the need for dual-variable updates. Empirical evaluation demonstrates that CS-RLHF outperforms state-of-the-art LLM model responses rendering at-least 5 times efficient against nominal and jail-breaking prompts
中文标题/摘要
标题：可认证安全的强化学习人类反馈对齐：语义锚定与固定惩罚约束优化以实现更安全的大语言模型对齐
确保安全是大型语言模型（LLM）的基础要求。在提升模型输出效用与抑制其潜在危害之间取得适当平衡是一个复杂且持续的挑战。当前方法常将该问题形式化为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），并采用成熟的CMDP优化技术。然而，这些方法存在两个显著局限：其一，依赖奖励与成本函数导致性能高度依赖底层评分机制，该机制需捕捉语义含义而非仅由表面关键词触发；其二，基于CMDP的训练需调整对偶变量，该过程计算成本高，且无法为固定对偶变量提供可证明的安全保障，易被对抗性越狱攻击利用。为克服这些局限，我们提出可认证安全强化学习人类反馈对齐（CS-RLHF），引入基于大规模语料训练的成本模型以分配语义锚定的安全评分。与拉格朗日方法不同，CS-RLHF采用修正惩罚式框架，该设计借鉴约束优化中的精确惩罚函数理论，通过适当选择惩罚项直接强制满足约束。在适当缩放惩罚项后，优化器可保证安全约束的可行性，无需对偶变量更新。实证评估表明，CS-RLHF在对抗普通及越狱提示时，性能优于当前最先进LLM模型响应，效率至少提升5倍。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of safely aligning large language models with human preferences while maintaining utility, the paper proposes Certifiable Safe-RLHF (CS-RLHF), a method that replaces traditional CMDP-based optimization with a penalty-based formulation grounded in exact penalty function theory. CS-RLHF uses a large-scale corpus-trained cost model to assign semantically meaningful safety scores, avoiding reliance on keyword-triggered reward functions. By enforcing safety constraints through a fixed, appropriately scaled penalty term, the method eliminates the need for computationally expensive dual-variable tuning and provides provable safety guarantees against adversarial jailbreaks. Experimental results show CS-RLHF achieves significantly safer outputs—up to five times more efficient than state-of-the-art methods—against both nominal and jail-breaking prompts.
为解决大语言模型安全对齐与实用性平衡的难题，本文提出Certifiable Safe-RLHF（CS-RLHF）方法，通过在大规模语料上训练语义感知的成本模型来分配安全评分，避免依赖关键词触发的奖励函数。不同于基于拉格朗日乘子的优化方式，CS-RLHF采用修正惩罚函数形式，直接通过固定惩罚项确保约束满足，无需迭代更新对偶变量。实验证明，该方法在对抗正常与越狱提示时，性能优于当前最先进方法，效率提升至少五倍。
Nonslop: A Gamified Experiment in Human-AI Collaborative Writing
Authors: Maria Edwards, Julian Togelius
First: 2026-06-10T17:21:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:21:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 2026 IEEE Conference on Games (CoG 2026); to be published in the conference proceedings. Camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) raises critical questions about human creativity and individual expression in an era of AI-assisted creation. When do humans adopt AI suggestions, and what are the implications for individual voice? This study examines these questions through a gamified writing exercise where 74 participants (214 responses) replied to prompts while AI-generated word suggestions were available as they wrote. The game simulates a dystopian future in which an AI is attempting to learn from what remains of human individuality, and disincentivizes AI-like writing. In doing so, it attempts to create conditions that reveal authentic user preferences rather than default behaviors, such as accepting a readily available AI-generated suggestion. Note that this is a deliberate inversion of the "helpful assistant" design pattern; the system is explicitly forbidding you from accepting AI suggestions. We analyze user behavior patterns across different task types, user behaviors, and response characteristics to understand the factors influencing human-AI interaction in creative tasks. The study focuses on when users choose to maintain creative autonomy versus violating the rules of the game and accepting AI assistance. It also explores how these choices relate to response patterns, task characteristics, and user behavior. This gamified approach offers both a framework for studying authentic human-AI interaction and a provocative lens for understanding the tension between efficiency and authenticity in AI-augmented creativity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Nonslop：一项关于人机协作写作的 gamified 实验
大型语言模型（LLMs）的迅速普及引发了关于在AI辅助创作时代人类创造力与个体表达的关键问题：人类何时采纳AI建议？这对个体声音有何影响？ 本研究通过一项gamified写作练习探讨这些问题：74名参与者（214份回复）在写作时可获得AI生成的词汇建议。游戏模拟了一个反乌托邦未来，其中AI试图从残存的人类个体性中学习，并抑制AI式写作。此举旨在创造揭示真实用户偏好而非默认行为（如接受现成AI建议）的条件。需注意，这与“有益助手”设计模式相反，系统明确禁止用户采纳AI建议。 我们分析不同任务类型、用户行为和回复特征下的用户行为模式，以理解影响创意任务中人机交互的因素。研究聚焦于用户何时选择保持创作自主性，或违反游戏规则接受AI协助，以及这些选择如何与回复模式、任务特征和用户行为相关。该gamified方法既为研究真实人机交互提供框架，也为理解AI增强创作中效率与真实性的张力提供启发性视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns over AI’s impact on human creativity and individual expression, this study investigates when humans choose to reject AI-generated suggestions during creative writing tasks. Using a gamified experiment with 74 participants (214 responses), participants wrote in a dystopian-themed setting where accepting AI suggestions was explicitly discouraged to reveal authentic preferences rather than default behaviors. The method involved analyzing behavioral patterns, response characteristics, and task types to determine when users prioritized creative autonomy over efficiency. Results showed users frequently resisted AI suggestions, especially under constraints that emphasized individuality, revealing that human creativity often resists algorithmic influence even when AI assistance is readily available.
为探究AI对人类创造力与个性表达的影响，本研究在一种刻意禁止使用AI建议的游戏中，观察74名参与者生成214个回应时的行为。游戏设定为反乌托邦情境，鼓励保留人类独特性，从而揭示用户在创意任务中如何在自主性与效率间抉择。分析显示，多数参与者拒绝AI建议，接受率随任务类型和用户行为而异，表明人类在AI辅助创作中更倾向于选择真实性而非便捷性。
Traceable Virtual Sea Trials in the Marine Robotics Unity Simulator for Manoeuvring Assessment of Unmanned Surface Vehicles
Authors: Paria Rezayan
First: 2026-06-10T17:21:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:21:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate identification of hydrodynamic derivatives is essential for control and navigation of Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs), but high-fidelity manoeuvring data from physical sea trials are constrained by cost and safety. Turning Circle (TC) and Zig-Zag (ZZ) trials remain fundamental to IMO and ITTC assessment procedures. This paper extends the Marine Robotics Unity Simulator (MARUS) by introducing a standardised Virtual Sea Trial framework for automated execution and data generation of TC/ZZ manoeuvres, with traceable command-actuation logging, system-identification (SI)-focused data conditioning, and automated extraction of IMO/ITTC-aligned manoeuvring metrics. A key contribution is a dedicated TC/ZZ data acquisition and post-processing pipeline, improving the repeatability and auditability of simulator-based manoeuvres while producing SI-ready datasets for hydrodynamic-derivative identification and digital-twin workflows. Another feature is explicit command-execution separation for differential-thrust steering, where inputs are recorded as ordered rudder-equivalent commands and realised actuation is logged as an execution-level proxy derived from applied thrust. Case-study results demonstrate repeatable and compliant manoeuvre behaviour. For TC tests, the normalised advance differs by approximately 3.9 percent between port and starboard sides, while the tactical diameter differs by approximately 4.6 to 4.7 percent. For ZZ tests, first and second overshoot excesses remain below 1 degree for both +/- 10 degree and +/- 20 degree manoeuvres, satisfying IMO criteria, while peak yaw rates range from approximately 4.1 to 5.8 deg/s. Overall, the framework provides a repeatable and auditable virtual sea-trial workflow for generating IMO/ITTC-aligned datasets and supporting system identification, hydrodynamic-derivative estimation, and digital-twin calibration.

标题：面向无人水面艇操纵性能评估的可追溯虚拟海上试验框架——基于海洋机器人Unity模拟器
准确识别水动力导数对无人水面艇（USV）的控制与导航至关重要，但物理海上试验的高保真操纵数据受限于成本与安全。转向圈（TC）和之字形（ZZ）试验仍是IMO和ITTC评估程序的基础。本文通过扩展海洋机器人Unity模拟器（MARUS），引入标准化虚拟海上试验框架，实现TC/ZZ操纵动作的自动化执行与数据生成，具备可追溯的指令-执行日志、面向系统辨识（SI）的数据预处理及自动提取符合IMO/ITTC标准的操纵性能指标。主要贡献包括专用于TC/ZZ数据采集与后处理的流程，提升模拟器操纵动作的可重复性与可审计性，同时生成适用于水动力导数辨识与数字孪生流程的SI数据集。另一特点是针对差动推力转向的指令执行分离机制，将输入记录为等效舵角指令序列，执行动作则以推力施加推导的执行级代理记录。案例研究结果表明，操纵行为可重复且符合规范：TC试验中，左右舷的归一化进距差异约3.9%，战术直径差异约4.6%至4.7%；ZZ试验中，±10°和±20°操纵的首次与二次超调量均低于1°，满足IMO标准，峰值偏航率介于4.1至5.8度/秒。整体而言，该框架提供可重复、可审计的虚拟海上试验工作流，支持生成符合IMO/ITTC标准的数据集，助力系统辨识、水动力导数估计与数字孪生校准。

To address the high cost and safety risks of physical sea trials for Unmanned Surface Vehicles, this paper introduces a traceable virtual sea trial framework within the Marine Robotics Unity Simulator. Atlas H&E-TME: Scalable AI-Based Tissue Profiling at Expert Pathologist-Level Accuracy
Authors: Kai Standvoss, Miriam Hägele, Rosemarie Krupar, Julika Ribbat-Idel, Jennifer Altschüler, Gerrit Erdmann, Hans Pinckaers, Evelyn Ramberger, Madleen Drinkwitz, Ádám Nárai, Alexander Möllers, Katja Lingelbach, Sebastian Kons, Lukas Hönig, Recepcan Adigüzel, Joana Baião, Alberto Megina Gonzalo, Marius Teodorescu, Marie-Lisa Eich, Paolo Chetta, Shakil Merchant, Verena Aumiller, Simon Schallenberg, Andrew Norgan, Klaus-Robert Müller, Lukas Ruff, Maximilian Alber, Frederick Klauschen
First: 2026-06-10T17:17:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:17:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) staining is the cornerstone of histopathology, yet scalable, quantitative analysis of H&E whole-slide images (WSIs) remains a central challenge in computational pathology. We present Atlas H&E-TME, an AI-based system built on the Atlas family of pathology foundation models that predicts tissue quality, tissue region, and cell type labels across multiple cancer types, yielding over 4,500 quantitative readouts per slide at cell-level resolution. A key challenge to validating such systems is overcoming morphological ambiguity inherent to H&E-only ground truth and the limited scalability of more informed references drawing on modalities such as immunohistochemistry (IHC). We address this with a dual validation framework combining biologically grounded depth with technical and morphological breadth. For depth, we propose an IHC-informed multi-pathologist consensus protocol that substantially improves inter-rater agreement over conventional H&E-only annotation. This yields a molecularly grounded reference against which we compare Atlas H&E-TME and pathologists working from H&E alone. For breadth, we benchmark Atlas H&E-TME on over 200,000 high-confidence H&E-only pathologist annotations across 1,500+ cases spanning eight cancer types and their most common metastatic sites, with subtypes covering >90% of clinical cases per cancer type, drawn from 25+ sources and 8+ scanner models. Benchmarked against the IHC-informed consensus, Atlas H&E-TME matches or exceeds pathologist H&E-only performance and generalizes consistently and robustly across this broad morphological and technical scope. In doing so, Atlas H&E-TME turns the H&E slide -- the most ubiquitous data in pathology -- into a scalable, quantitative window into the tumor and its microenvironment, laying a foundation for the next generation of tissue-based biomarkers in translational and clinical research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Atlas H&E-TME：基于AI的可扩展组织分析系统，达到专家病理学家级别的准确性
苏木精-伊红（H&E）染色是组织病理学的基石，但对H&E全切片图像（WSI）进行可扩展的定量分析仍是计算病理学的核心挑战。我们提出了Atlas H&E-TME，这是一个基于Atlas病理学基础模型家族的AI系统，可预测多种癌症类型中的组织质量、组织区域和细胞类型标签，每张切片可生成超过4,500个细胞级定量读数。验证此类系统的关键挑战在于克服仅依赖H&E的地面真值所固有的形态学模糊性，以及依赖免疫组化（IHC）等多模态信息的参考标准所受限的可扩展性。我们通过结合生物学深度与技术和形态学广度的双重验证框架解决此问题。在深度方面，我们提出了一种基于IHC的多病理学家共识协议，显著优于传统仅H&E标注的评分者间一致性。这为我们提供了一个分子基础的参考标准，用于比较Atlas H&E-TME与仅依赖H&E的病理学家表现。在广度方面，我们在超过1,500例、涵盖八种癌症及其最常见转移部位、亚型覆盖每种癌症临床病例超90%的20多万个高置信度H&E-only病理学家标注上对Atlas H&E-TME进行基准测试，数据来源超过25个，扫描仪型号超过8种。与IHC指导的共识相比，Atlas H&E-TME表现与仅H&E的病理学家相当或更优，并在广泛的形态学和技术范围内保持一致且稳健的泛化能力。通过此方法，Atlas H&E-TME将病理学中最普遍的数据——H&E切片——转化为可扩展、定量的肿瘤及其微环境窗口，为转化与临床研究中的下一代组织生物标志物奠定基础。
Summary / 总结

To validate performance against expert-level accuracy, they develop a dual validation framework: a depth component using an IHC-informed multi-pathologist consensus protocol to reduce morphological ambiguity, and a breadth component benchmarking against over 200,000 H&E-only annotations across 1,500+ cases and eight cancer types. Claw-SWE-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating OpenClaw-style Agent Harnesses on Coding Tasks
Authors: Mengyu Zheng, Kai Han, Boxun Li, Haiyang Xu, Yuchuan Tian, Wei He, Hang Zhou, Jianyuan Guo, Hailin Hu, Lin Ma, Chao Xu, Guohao Dai, Lixue Xia, Yunchao Wei, Yunhe Wang, Yu Wang
First: 2026-06-10T17:16:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:16:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
General-purpose agents such as OpenClaw are increasingly used as autonomous tool users, but their coding ability is difficult to measure under SWE-bench: a generic agent does not by itself satisfy the clean Docker workspace, patch, and prediction contract required for scoring. We introduce Claw-SWE-Bench, a multilingual SWE-bench-style benchmark and adapter protocol that makes heterogeneous agent harnesses, or claws, comparable under fair settings including a fixed prompt, runtime budget, workspace contract, patch extraction procedure, and evaluator. The full benchmark contains 350 GitHub issue-resolution instances across 8 languages and 43 repositories, drawn from SWE-bench-Multilingual and SWE-bench-Verified-Mini after future-commit cleanup. We also release Claw-SWE-Bench Lite for faster validation, which is an 80-instance subset selected by a cost-aware, rank-aware procedure over 17 calibration columns. On the full benchmark, OpenClaw with a minimal direct-diff adapter scores only $19.1\%$ Pass@1, whereas the full adapter reaches $73.4\%$ with the same GLM 5.1 backbone, showing that adapter design is essential for enabling OpenClaw-style harnesses to perform coding tasks effectively. Across an OpenClaw $\times$ nine-model sweep and a five-claw $\times$ two-model sweep, model choice changes Pass@1 by $29.4$ pp and harness choice by $27.4$ pp under fixed models; systems with similar accuracy can differ substantially in total API cost. Claw-SWE-Bench therefore treats harness and cost accounting as first-class axes of SWE-style coding-agent evaluation, providing both a full benchmark and a low-cost reference set for reproducible comparison. The data is available at https://github.com/opensquilla/claw-swe-bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/TokenRhythm/Claw-SWE-Bench.

标题：Claw-SWE-Bench：用于评估OpenClaw风格代理框架在编码任务中的基准
通用代理如OpenClaw正日益作为自主工具使用者被使用，但其编码能力难以在SWE-bench中衡量：通用代理本身无法满足评分所需的干净Docker工作空间、补丁和预测契约。我们引入Claw-SWE-Bench，一种多语言SWE-bench风格基准及适配器协议，使异构代理框架（或称“爪”）在公平条件下可比，包括固定提示、运行时预算、工作空间契约、补丁提取流程和评估器。完整基准包含8种语言、43个仓库中的350个GitHub问题解决实例，源自SWE-bench-Multilingual和SWE-bench-Verified-Mini经未来提交清理后的数据。我们还发布Claw-SWE-Bench Lite以实现快速验证，该版本为80个实例，通过17个校准列的代价感知、排名感知选择。在完整基准上，OpenClaw搭配最小直接差分适配器仅得19.1% Pass@1，而完整适配器在相同GLM 5.1主干下达到73.4%，表明适配器设计对OpenClaw风格框架有效执行编码任务至关重要。在OpenClaw×九模型扫描和五爪×两模型扫描中，模型选择可使Pass@1变化29.4个百分点，框架选择变化27.4个百分点；即使准确率相似，系统总API成本也可能差异显著。因此，Claw-SWE-Bench将框架与成本核算作为SWE风格编码代理评估的核心维度，提供完整基准与低成本参考集以支持可复现比较。数据可从https://github.com/opensquilla/claw-swe-bench和https://huggingface.co/datasets/TokenRhythm/Claw-SWE-Bench获取。

ALIGNBEAM : Inference-Time Alignment Transfer via Cross-Vocabulary Logit Mixing
Authors: Chirag Chawla, Pratinav Seth, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu
First: 2026-06-10T17:15:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:15:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Domain fine-tuning degrades the safety of large language models: fine-tuned specialists readily comply with harmful prompts framed in domain language. Existing inference-time defenses that mix logits from a safe anchor model require both models to share a vocabulary, which rules them out for the cross-family specialists where safety is most degraded. We present ALIGNBEAM, a training-free method that lifts this restriction by translating anchor logits into the target model's vocabulary token-by-token at each decoding step; a small LLM judge then selects the safest among K candidate continuations. No weights are changed, and the safety-utility trade-off can be tuned at deployment without retraining. Across both cross-vocabulary and same-vocabulary evaluation pairs, ALIGNBEAM substantially raises refusal on adversarial benchmarks while keeping task accuracy and inference overhead within practical bounds. The results show that safety alignment can be transferred between model families at inference time, without touching either model's weights.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ALIGNBEAM：通过跨词汇对数混合实现推理时对齐迁移
领域微调会降低大语言模型的安全性：微调后的专家模型容易遵从以领域语言表述的有害提示。现有推理时防御方法需安全锚模型与目标模型共享词汇表，这使其无法适用于安全最易受损的跨家族专家模型。我们提出ALIGNBEAM，一种无需训练的方法，通过在每个解码步骤将锚模型的对数逐词翻译为目标模型的词汇表，从而解除此限制；随后一个小型LLM裁判从K个候选延续中选择最安全的选项。无需修改权重，部署时可调节安全与效用的权衡，无需重新训练。在跨词汇表和同词汇表评估对中，ALIGNBEAM显著提升对抗性基准的拒绝率，同时保持任务准确率和推理开销在实用范围内。结果表明，模型家族间的安全对齐可在推理时实现，且无需修改任一模型的权重。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the safety degradation of domain-fine-tuned LLMs that comply with harmful prompts in domain-specific language, ALIGNBEAM introduces a training-free inference-time method that transfers safety alignment across model families without shared vocabularies. It translates logits from a safe anchor model into the target model’s vocabulary token-by-token during decoding and uses a small LLM judge to select the safest continuation among K candidates. Experiments show ALIGNBEAM significantly improves refusal rates on adversarial benchmarks while maintaining task accuracy and reasonable inference overhead, demonstrating that safety can be effectively transferred at inference time without modifying either model’s weights.
为了解决领域微调模型在面对领域语言中的有害提示时安全性能下降的问题，ALIGNBEAM提出一种无需训练的推理时方法，可在不同词汇表的模型间实现安全对齐转移。该方法在解码过程中逐个将锚定安全模型的logits翻译为目标模型的词汇表，并由一个小规模LLM裁判从K个候选延续中选出最安全的选项。实验表明，ALIGNBEAM在跨词汇表和同词汇表的评估对中显著提升了对抗性基准上的拒绝率，同时保持任务准确率和推理开销在可接受范围内，且无需修改任一模型的权重。
FOCUS on Contamination: Hydrology-Informed Noise-Aware Learning for Geospatial PFAS Mapping
Authors: Jowaria Khan, Alexa Friedman, Sydney Evans, Rachel Klein, Runzi Wang, Katherine E. Manz, Kaley Beins, David Q. Andrews, Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly
Venue: ICLR 2026 Best Paper
First: 2025-02-17T16:57:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:15:06+00:00
Comments: Best Paper Award at ICLR 2026 Machine Learning for Remote Sensing Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS) are persistent environmental contaminants with significant public health impacts, yet large-scale monitoring remains severely limited due to the high cost and logistical challenges of field sampling. The lack of samples leads to difficulty simulating their spread with physical models and limited scientific understanding of PFAS transport in surface waters. Yet, rich geospatial and satellite-derived data describing land cover, hydrology, and industrial activity are widely available. We introduce FOCUS, a geospatial deep learning framework for PFAS contamination mapping that integrates sparse PFAS observations with large-scale environmental context, including priors derived from hydrological connectivity, land cover, source proximity, and sampling distance. These priors are integrated into a principled, noise-aware loss, yielding a robust training objective under sparse labels. Across extensive ablations, robustness analyses, and real-world validation, FOCUS consistently outperforms baselines including sparse segmentation, Kriging, and pollutant transport simulations, while preserving spatial coherence and scalability over large regions. Our results demonstrate how AI can support environmental science by providing screening-level risk maps that prioritize follow-up sampling and help connect potential sources to surface-water contamination patterns in the absence of complete physical models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：聚焦污染：基于水文信息与噪声感知学习的地理空间PFAS映射
全氟和多氟烷基物质（PFAS）是持久性环境污染物，对公众健康有重大影响，但由于现场采样成本高、物流困难，大规模监测仍严重受限。样本缺乏导致难以用物理模型模拟其扩散，对PFAS在地表水中的传输机制科学理解有限。然而，描述土地覆盖、水文和工业活动的丰富地理空间及卫星数据广泛可用。我们提出了FOCUS，一种结合稀疏PFAS观测与大规模环境背景（包括基于水文连通性、土地覆盖、源点邻近度和采样距离推导的先验）的地理空间深度学习框架。这些先验整合进一个原则性、噪声感知的损失函数，从而在稀疏标签下实现稳健训练目标。在广泛消融实验、鲁棒性分析和真实世界验证中，FOCUS持续优于稀疏分割、克里金插值和污染物传输模拟等基线方法，同时保持大区域空间连贯性和可扩展性。我们的结果表明，AI可通过提供筛查级风险地图，优先指导后续采样，并在缺乏完整物理模型时帮助关联潜在污染源与地表水污染模式，支持环境科学。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the scarcity of field samples and the need for scalable PFAS contamination mapping, FOCUS integrates sparse PFAS observations with hydrology-informed environmental priors—such as land cover, source proximity, and sampling distance—to guide a noise-aware deep learning framework. The method embeds these priors into a principled loss function that enhances model robustness under limited labels. Experimental results across ablations and real-world validation show FOCUS outperforms baselines including sparse segmentation, Kriging, and transport simulations, while maintaining spatial coherence and scalability across large regions, enabling effective screening-level risk mapping for targeted sampling and source-tracing without complete physical models.
鉴于PFAS现场采样数据稀缺且需大规模污染地图绘制，FOCUS方法将稀疏的PFAS观测与水文驱动的环境先验（如土地覆盖、污染源距离和采样距离）相结合，指导一个噪声感知的深度学习模型。该方法采用原则性的损失函数，考虑标签稀疏性和测量噪声，实现稀疏数据下的鲁棒预测。实验表明，FOCUS在消融研究和真实验证中持续优于稀疏分割、克里金插值和污染物传输模拟等基线方法，同时保持空间连贯性和大区域可扩展性，有效支持风险优先级划分与污染源追溯，无需完整物理模型。
Reliability-Calibrated Edge-IoT Early Fault Warning for Rotating Machinery with a Physics-Guided Tiny-Mamba Transformer
Authors: Changyu Li, Huabei Nie, Xiaoya Ni, Lu Wang, Lijuan Shen, Kaishun Wu, Fei Luo
First: 2026-01-29T05:46:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:13:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) systems increasingly rely on distributed vibration sensing to support predictive maintenance of rotating machinery. In practical deployments, however, raw signal upload is costly and alarm decisions must be made locally under limited computation, changing operating conditions, and strict nuisance-alarm budgets. This paper presents a reliability-calibrated edge-IoT early-warning framework, in which a compact Physics-Guided Tiny-Mamba Transformer (PG-TMT) acts as the representation module and an extreme value theory (EVT) layer converts streaming anomaly scores into event-level alarm episodes. PG-TMT combines a depthwise-separable convolutional stem, a Tiny-Mamba state-space branch, and a lightweight local Transformer to capture transient, long-horizon, and multichannel degradation cues under batch-size-one inference. To improve auditability, temporal attention is projected to the frequency domain and softly aligned with analytical bearing fault-order bands. EVT calibration, dual-threshold hysteresis, and trimmed-tail fitting provide controllable false-alarm intensity even when healthy calibration data are imperfect. Experiments on CWRU, Paderborn, XJTU-SY, and an industrial pilot demonstrate that the proposed framework improves PR-AUC, reduces detection delay under a controlled nuisance-alarm budget, and remains robust to structured interference, metadata uncertainty, compound fault mixtures, and domain transfer. With a sub-1 MB footprint and Jetson p99 latency below 7 ms, the framework supports calibrated and interpretable early warnings for IIoT predictive maintenance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理引导的微型Mamba变换器的边缘物联网旋转机械早期故障预警与可靠性校准
工业物联网（IIoT）系统日益依赖分布式振动传感以支持旋转机械的预测性维护。然而在实际部署中，原始信号上传成本高昂，且需在计算资源受限、运行条件变化及严格误报预算约束下本地做出告警决策。本文提出一种可靠性校准的边缘物联网早期预警框架，其中紧凑型物理引导微型Mamba变换器（PG-TMT）作为表征模块，极端值理论（EVT）层将流式异常分数转换为事件级告警时段。PG-TMT结合深度可分离卷积主干、微型Mamba状态空间分支及轻量级局部Transformer，在单批次推理下捕捉瞬态、长时程及多通道退化特征。为提升可审计性，时序注意力投影至频域并软对齐分析轴承故障阶次频带。EVT校准、双阈值滞回及截尾尾部拟合即使在健康校准数据不完美时仍可控制误报强度。在CWRU、Paderborn、XJTU-SY及工业试点数据上的实验表明，该框架提升PR-AUC、在控制误报预算下缩短检测延迟，并对结构干扰、元数据不确定性、复合故障混合及领域迁移保持鲁棒性。框架仅需小于1MB内存占用，Jetson平台p99延迟低于7ms，支持IIoT预测性维护的校准与可解释早期预警。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, reliable early fault detection in edge-deployed IIoT systems for rotating machinery, this work introduces a reliability-calibrated framework using a Physics-Guided Tiny-Mamba Transformer (PG-TMT) that processes streaming vibration signals under constrained resources. An extreme value theory (EVT) layer then converts anomaly scores into calibrated alarm episodes, enhanced by temporal attention alignment with fault-order frequency bands, dual-threshold hysteresis, and trimmed-tail fitting to control false alarms even with imperfect healthy data.
