为解决工业物联网边缘部署中旋转机械早期故障预警的资源受限与可靠性需求，本文提出一种基于物理引导的Tiny-Mamba Transformer（PG-TMT）与极值理论（EVT）的可靠性校准框架。PG-TMT融合深度可分离卷积、Tiny-Mamba状态空间模块与轻量局部Transformer，在单批次推理下捕捉瞬态与长程退化特征；EVT层将异常分数转化为事件级报警，结合频域注意力对齐与EVT校准，确保在健康数据不完整时仍能控制误报强度。在CWRU、Paderborn、XJTU-SY及工业试点数据上，该框架显著提升PR-AUC、降低检测延迟（在固定误报预算下），并具备对结构干扰、元数据不确定性、复合故障及域迁移的鲁棒性，模型体积小于1MB，Jetson平台延迟低于7ms。
OCELOT: Inference-Leakage Budgets for Privacy-Preserving LLM Agents
Authors: Jin Xie, Songze Li
First: 2026-06-10T17:13:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:13:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly act on a user's behalf -- reading personal files, calling tools, transacting with external services -- possibly leaking personally identifiable information (PII) across trust boundaries at every step. Privacy here is a property not of a single output but of an entire trajectory, and three properties make it hard: leakage is cumulative, as individually innocuous releases accumulate across honest-but-curious or colluding sinks into inferences about a protected secret; bidirectional, as a malicious observation can inject instructions that turn the agent's own reasoning model against the user; and task-dependent, as the same field is necessary for one recipient yet gratuitous for another. Per-release contextual-integrity filters, information-flow controls, and posterior-leakage monitors each address part of this but none controls cumulative, inference-based leakage at runtime. We recast agent privacy as \emph{posterior-risk control} and present OCELOT, a runtime mediator that budgets how much an adversary's belief about a secret may improve across a trajectory, rather than filtering outputs. Its mechanism, \emph{Witness-Verified Declassification}, separates judgment from trust: an untrusted, locally fine-tuned defender model inspects each candidate release and emits structured evidence -- labeled atoms and proposed declassification operators -- which a deterministic verifier audits, charging a certified min-entropy cost for the chosen variant and authorizing the least-disclosing useful release under a sink-trust-weighted budget recorded on a tamper-evident ledger. Across diverse agent benchmarks and recent defenses, OCELOT attains significantly lower leakage at higher task utility, resists adaptive injection, jailbreak, cumulative inference, and sink collusion, and adds only modest overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OCELOT：用于隐私保护大语言模型代理的推理泄露预算
大型语言模型（LLM）代理越来越多地代表用户执行操作——阅读个人文件、调用工具、与外部服务交易——可能在每一步跨越信任边界泄露个人可识别信息（PII）。此处的隐私并非单个输出的属性，而是整个轨迹的属性，且有三个特性使其难以保障：泄露具有累积性，即单个看似无害的信息释放，经由诚实但好奇或合谋的接收方累积后，可推断出受保护的秘密；具有双向性，恶意观察可注入指令，使代理自身的推理模型反向攻击用户；具有任务依赖性，同一信息对某些接收方是必需的，对另一些则属冗余。现有每项释放的上下文完整性过滤器、信息流控制和后验泄露监控器各自解决部分问题，但均无法在运行时控制累积的、基于推理的泄露。我们将代理隐私重新定义为\emph{后验风险控制}，并提出OCELOT，一种运行时中介，其预算的是对手在轨迹中对秘密信念的提升程度，而非过滤输出。其机制\emph{见证验证解密}将判断与信任分离：一个不可信的、本地微调的防御模型检查每个候选释放并输出结构化证据——带标签的原子和提议的解密操作——由确定性验证器审计，为所选变体收取经认证的最小熵成本，并在基于接收方信任权重的、写入防篡改账本的预算内授权最小泄露且最有用的释放。在多样化的代理基准和近期防御方案中，OCELOT在保持更高任务效用的同时显著降低泄露，抵抗适应性注入、越狱、累积推理和接收方合谋，并仅增加少量开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of cumulative, bidirectional, and task-dependent privacy leakage in LLM agents that interact with external systems, the paper introduces OCELOT, a runtime mediator that controls posterior inference risk rather than filtering outputs. OCELOT employs Witness-Verified Declassification: a locally fine-tuned defender model generates structured evidence for each potential release, which a deterministic verifier audits and charges a min-entropy cost, authorizing the least-disclosing useful release under a trust-weighted budget. Experiments show OCELOT achieves significantly lower leakage with higher task utility, resists adaptive injection, jailbreak, cumulative inference, and sink collusion, while adding only modest overhead.
为应对大语言模型代理在与外部系统交互时可能产生的累积性、双向性和任务依赖性隐私泄露问题，本文提出OCELOT，一种运行时中介机制，通过控制后验风险而非过滤输出来保障隐私。其核心机制“见证验证解密”中，本地微调的防御模型评估每个潜在输出并生成结构化证据，由确定性验证器审计并按最小熵成本计费，最终授权在信任加权预算内最不泄露且最有用的输出。实验表明，OCELOT在保持较高任务效用的同时显著降低泄露，能抵御自适应注入、越狱攻击、累积推理和接收端合谋，且仅增加少量开销。
Echoes of the Prior: A Computational Phenomenology of Forgetting
Authors: Gege Gao, Bernhard Schölkopf, Andreas Geiger
Venue: SIGGRAPH
First: 2026-06-10T17:10:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:10:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Memory is not merely the storage of data; it is the scaffolding of reality. When biological memory fades, the world does not simply turn black; it regresses into an unrecognizable chaos. Echoes of the Prior is an interactive installation that attempts to visualize this subjective phenomenology of forgetting. By inducing controlled synaptic decay within a Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction model, we create an artistic analogy for the erosion of the brain's predictive priors. We position the Neural Network not as a tool for engineering, but as a cognitive proxy - a silicon brain whose structural degeneration evokes the disorienting, poetic, and terrifying experience of losing one's grip on the world. Ultimately, we offer this framework as a catalyst, inviting the wider community to explore the uncharted potential of neuromorphic aesthetics in visualizing the fragility of intelligence. Interactive demo see https://decart-4d.github.io/.

该研究旨在通过模拟神经元衰减，将遗忘的主观体验转化为视觉现象。方法上，采用前馈3D重建模型，通过诱导可控的突触衰减，使神经网络作为认知代理而非工程工具，呈现现实结构崩塌的视觉效果。实验结果显示，随着衰减加剧，渲染场景的结构连贯性逐步瓦解，最终生成混沌不可辨识的图像，映射记忆丧失的心理体验。
Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics
Authors: Dominik Dahlem, Diego Maniloff, Mac Misiura
First: 2026-05-06T13:25:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:09:08+00:00
Comments: 48 pages, 6 figures, 7 tables; 81-page online supplement (proofs, additional experiments, dataset statistics) as an ancillary file
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When a language model processes a hallucinated response, its attention routing tends to fail in one of two shapes: over-concentrating on a narrow set of positions, or spreading so diffusely that relevance is diluted, and the shape of the failure carries diagnostic signal. We study these shapes as a diagnostic characterization, computed from attention matrices under \emph{forced scoring} of benchmark-labeled responses rather than during live generation. A widely used family of spectral methods analyzes the symmetric component of the degree-normalized attention operator, which governs transport \emph{capacity}; we prove that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of this operator is structurally \emph{orientation-blind} (it cannot distinguish an operator from its transpose, and therefore cannot detect information-flow direction), with a converse to the blindness theorem bounding any Lipschitz diagnostic's transpose sensitivity by the asymmetry coefficient $G$. Pairing this with a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures, we show that uniform causal attention satisfies an $n$-independent floor $φ\ge 1/5$, while window attention pierces the floor as $O(w/n)$; failure modes are shape-different, not just value-different. This floor is an idealized-architecture benchmark, not an empirical attractor: the fraction of real attention heads that pierce it is itself an architectural signature. The resulting two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for direction) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction: bottleneck- and diffuse-dominated benchmarks should exhibit opposite polarity. Under length-controlled evaluation, transport features retain interpretable signal (0.62-0.84 LC-AUROC) across the tested decoder-only, encoder-only, and encoder-decoder models, with polarity reversing as predicted between HaluEval and MedHallu.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自注意力作为传输：对称谱诊断的局限
当语言模型处理幻觉响应时，其注意力路由往往呈现两种失败形态：过度集中于少数位置，或扩散过广导致相关性稀释，失败形态本身携带诊断信号。我们将其作为诊断特征研究，通过在强制评分基准标注响应时计算注意力矩阵，而非实时生成过程中。广泛使用的谱方法分析度归一化注意力算子的对称分量，该分量控制传输容量；我们证明，该算子所有转置不变的谱诊断在结构上是“方向盲”的（无法区分算子与其转置，因而无法检测信息流方向），其逆定理指出，任何Lipschitz诊断的转置敏感度被不对称系数G所限制。 结合对典型因果架构的闭式二分Cheeger景观，我们表明，均匀因果注意力满足与n无关的下限φ≥1/5，而窗口注意力则以O(w/n)突破该下限；失败模式形态不同，而非仅数值不同。该下限是理想化架构基准，而非经验吸引子：实际注意力头突破该下限的比例本身即为架构特征。由此得到的双轴诊断（φ表容量，G表方向）可产生可证伪的极性预测：瓶颈主导与扩散主导的基准应呈现相反极性。在长度控制评估下，传输特征在测试的解码器独占、编码器独占及编码器-解码器模型中均保留可解释信号（0.62-0.84 LC-AUROC），且在HalucEval与MedHallu间极性按预测反转。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to diagnose attention failures in hallucinated language model responses, this paper introduces a spectral diagnostic framework based on attention matrix analysis under forced scoring. It proves that symmetric spectral methods are orientation-blind, unable to distinguish information flow direction, and bounds their sensitivity to transpose asymmetry via the asymmetry coefficient G. By combining this with a bipartite-Cheeger landscape for causal architectures, the study shows that uniform causal attention maintains a capacity floor φ ≥ 1/5, while window attention degrades as O(w/n), revealing distinct failure shapes. The resulting two-axis diagnostic (φ for transport capacity, G for direction sensitivity) predicts polarity reversal between benchmarks, which is empirically validated across decoder-only, encoder-only, and encoder-decoder models, yielding interpretable transport signals (0.62–0.84 LC-AUROC) that reverse polarity as expected.
为诊断语言模型在生成幻觉响应时的注意力失效模式，该研究提出一种基于强制评分下注意力矩阵的谱诊断框架。证明了对称谱方法本质上是方向盲的，无法区分信息流动方向，并通过不对称系数G量化其敏感性。通过分析因果架构的二分Cheeger景观，发现均匀因果注意力具有容量下限φ≥1/5，而窗口注意力衰减为O(w/n)，揭示了不同的失效形态。由此构建的双轴诊断（φ表容量，G表方向敏感性）预测不同基准数据集应呈现相反极性，实验在长度控制下验证了该预测，各类模型（解码器、编码器、编解码器）均达到0.62–0.84的LC-AUROC，且HaluEval与MedHallu的极性反转符合理论预期。
Fast-SDE: Efficient Single-Microphone Sound Source Distance Estimation in Reverberant Environments
Authors: Jiang Wang, Runwu Shi, Yaozhong Kang, Benjamin Yen, Takeshi Ashizawa, Kazuhiro Nakadai
First: 2026-06-10T17:08:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:08:14+00:00
Comments: To appear in the 35th IEEE International Conference on Robot and Human Interactive Communication (RO-MAN)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Sound source distance estimation (SDE) is a critical capability in human-robot interaction. An inappropriate interaction distance not only reduces the reliability of speech acquisition and understanding, but also compromises the naturalness and comfort of the interaction. Most existing SDE methods rely on microphone arrays, however, multi-microphone systems typically require careful hardware synchronization, geometric calibration, and additional space and computational resources, which limits applicability to size-constrained and computability-limited embodied platforms. To alleviate these issues, we propose Fast-SDE, a lightweight single-microphone SDE framework that is suited for deployment on robot platforms with limited computational resources and strict size constraints. Specifically, Fast-SDE employs a subband-based backbone that decomposes the frequency axis into multiple subbands, rather than processing the entire spectrum with a wide full-band backbone. A shared subband encoder then maps each subband to a compact latent representation and learns the relationship between acoustic structure and time-frequency patterns. Finally, a lightweight regression head converts the fused subband representations into the estimated distance. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate the merits of the proposed method. To benefit the broader research community, we have open-sourced our code at https://github.com/JiangWAV/FAST-SDE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Fast-SDE：在混响环境中的高效单麦克风声源距离估计
声源距离估计（SDE）是人机交互中的关键能力。不恰当的交互距离不仅降低语音采集与理解的可靠性，也影响交互的自然性和舒适性。现有大多数SDE方法依赖麦克风阵列，但多麦克风系统通常需要精细的硬件同步、几何校准及额外的空间和计算资源，限制其在计算资源受限和尺寸受限的具身平台上的应用。为缓解这些问题，我们提出Fast-SDE，一种轻量级单麦克风SDE框架，适用于计算资源有限和尺寸受限的机器人平台。具体而言，Fast-SDE采用基于子带的骨干网络，将频率轴分解为多个子带，而非使用宽频全带骨干网络处理整个频谱。共享子带编码器将每个子带映射为紧凑的潜在表示，并学习声学结构与时频模式之间的关系。最后，轻量级回归头将融合的子带表示转换为估计距离。大量仿真和现实世界实验验证了该方法的优势。为促进更广泛的研究社区，我们已开源代码：https://github.com/JiangWAV/FAST-SDE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient sound source distance estimation in human-robot interaction, especially on resource-constrained robotic platforms, Fast-SDE introduces a lightweight single-microphone framework that avoids the hardware and computational overhead of multi-microphone systems. The method decomposes the audio spectrum into subbands, processes each with a shared encoder to extract compact latent representations, and fuses them for distance regression via a lightweight head. Experimental results across simulated and real-world reverberant environments show that Fast-SDE achieves competitive accuracy while maintaining low computational cost, making it suitable for deployment on embedded robotic systems.
为满足资源受限机器人平台对高效声源距离估计的需求，Fast-SDE 提出了一种轻量级单麦克风框架，避免了多麦克风系统在硬件同步与计算资源上的开销。该方法将频谱划分为多个子带，通过共享编码器将每个子带映射为紧凑的潜在特征，并融合后由轻量回归头输出距离估计。在模拟与真实混响环境中的实验表明，Fast-SDE 在保持低计算开销和小模型体积的同时，实现了与现有方法相当的精度，适用于嵌入式机器人平台部署。
Adjoint Method versus Physics-Informed Neural Networks in PDE-Constrained Inverse Problems
Authors: Zhen Zhang, Alessandro Alla, George Em Karniadakis
First: 2026-06-10T17:07:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:07:09+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse problems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) are central to computational mechanics and are commonly solved by adjoint-based optimization, while physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a flexible alternative. Their relative performance remains difficult to assess because the two approaches are often compared under different formulations, parameterizations, optimizers, and regularization choices. We present a fair comparison of adjoint optimization and PINNs for PDE-constrained inverse problems. From a common abstract formulation, we instantiate both methods on identical domains, governing equations, observation models, and regularization terms, while matching the optimizer, unknown parameterization, and arithmetic precision wherever applicable. The benchmarks include unsteady Burgers, noisy Darcy permeability inversion, three-dimensional Allen--Cahn reaction identification, and unsteady Navier--Stokes viscosity identification. The results show that the representation of the unknown largely determines the preferred method: grid-based fields favor the discrete adjoint, whereas neural representations are native to PINNs and relevant for closure and constitutive modeling. For time-dependent problems, adjoint inversion can be dominated by trajectory storage and differentiation, while PINNs provide satisfactory reconstructions at lower cost. A PINN-warm-started adjoint strategy then recovers adjoint-level accuracy at substantially reduced cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：伴随方法与物理信息神经网络在偏微分方程约束反问题中的比较
由偏微分方程（PDE）支配的反问题在计算力学中居于核心地位，通常通过伴随优化求解，而物理信息神经网络（PINNs）则作为一种灵活的替代方案出现。由于两种方法常在不同公式化、参数化、优化器和正则化选择下进行比较，其相对性能难以评估。本文对PDE约束反问题中的伴随优化与PINNs进行了公平比较。从共同的抽象公式出发，我们在相同的域、控制方程、观测模型和正则化项上实例化两种方法，并在适用范围内匹配优化器、未知参数化和算术精度。基准测试包括非稳态Burgers方程、含噪Darcy渗透率反演、三维Allen-Cahn反应识别及非稳态Navier-Stokes黏度识别。结果表明，未知量的表示形式很大程度上决定了优选方法：基于网格的场更适合离散伴随方法，而神经网络表示则天然适合PINNs，且适用于闭合关系和本构建模。对于时变问题，伴随反演可能受轨迹存储与微分运算主导，而PINNs则以更低成本提供满意重建。采用PINN预热启动的伴随策略，可在显著降低成本下恢复伴随级精度。
Summary / 总结
This study motivates a fair comparison between adjoint methods and physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving PDE-constrained inverse problems, addressing prior inconsistencies in benchmarking. The authors implement both approaches under identical problem formulations, domains, equations, observation models, and regularization, while aligning optimizers, parameterizations, and precision. Experimental results across four benchmarks—unsteady Burgers, noisy Darcy permeability, 3D Allen-Cahn, and unsteady Navier-Stokes—reveal that grid-based unknowns favor adjoint methods, while neural representations suit PINNs better for closure and constitutive modeling. For time-dependent problems, adjoint methods face high computational cost due to trajectory storage and differentiation, whereas PINNs offer efficient reconstructions. A hybrid PINN-warm-started adjoint strategy achieves adjoint-level accuracy at significantly lower cost.
该研究旨在公平比较伴随方法与物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在求解偏微分方程约束反问题中的表现，解决此前因实验设置不一致导致的性能评估偏差。作者在相同问题框架、域、方程、观测模型、正则化项下，对两种方法进行统一实现，并匹配优化器与精度。在四个基准测试——非稳态Burgers方程、噪声Darcy渗透率反演、三维Allen–Cahn反应识别及非稳态Navier–Stokes黏度识别中，结果表明未知参数的表示形式决定方法优劣：网格化场更适合伴随方法，而神经网络表示则更适用于PINNs，尤其在闭合关系与本构建模中。对于时变问题，伴随方法因轨迹存储与微分计算成本高昂，而PINNs则以更低开销提供满意重建结果；采用PINN预热启动的伴随策略，可显著降低计算成本的同时恢复伴随方法级别的精度。
Fourier Features Let Agents Learn High Precision Policies with Imitation Learning
Authors: Balázs Gyenes, Emiliyan Gospodinov, Jan Frieling, Enrico Krohmer, Nicolas Schreiber, Xiaogang Jia, Niklas Freymuth, Gerhard Neumann
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-10T17:05:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:05:50+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
High-precision robotic manipulation requires fine-grained spatial reasoning that is often difficult to achieve with RGB-only policies due to depth ambiguity and perspective scale issues. Policies that leverage 3D information directly, such as those based on point clouds, offer a stronger geometric prior over purely image-based ones, yet their performance remains highly task-dependent. We hypothesize that this discrepancy may be due to the spectral bias of neural networks towards learning low frequency functions, which especially affects architectures conditioned on slow-moving Cartesian features. We thus propose to map point clouds from Cartesian space into high-dimensional Fourier space, effectively equipping the point cloud encoder with direct access to high-frequency features. We experimentally validate the use of Fourier features on challenging manipulation tasks from the RoboCasa and ManiSkill3 benchmarks and on a real robot setup. Despite their simplicity, we find that Fourier features provide significant benefits across diverse encoder architectures and benchmarks and are robust across hyperparameters. Our results indicate that Fourier features let policies leverage geometric details more effectively than Cartesian features, showing their potential as a general-purpose tool for point cloud-based imitation learning. We provide source code and videos on our project page: https://fourier-il.github.io/fourier-il
中文标题/摘要
标题：傅里叶特征使模仿学习中的智能体能够学习高精度策略
高精度机器人操作需要精细的空间推理，而仅依赖RGB图像的策略常因深度模糊和视角尺度问题难以实现。直接利用3D信息的策略（如基于点云的策略）相比纯图像策略具有更强的几何先验，但其性能高度依赖任务。我们假设这种差异源于神经网络对低频函数的频谱偏置，尤其影响依赖缓慢变化笛卡尔特征的架构。因此，我们提出将点云从笛卡尔空间映射到高维傅里叶空间，使点云编码器可直接访问高频特征。我们在RoboCasa和ManiSkill3基准的挑战性操作任务及真实机器人设置上实验验证了傅里叶特征的有效性。尽管方法简单，傅里叶特征在多种编码器架构和基准测试中均表现出显著优势，且对超参数鲁棒。结果表明，傅里叶特征使策略能更有效地利用几何细节，展现出其作为点云模仿学习通用工具的潜力。项目代码和视频见：https://fourier-il.github.io/fourier-il
Summary / 总结
To address the difficulty of achieving high-precision robotic manipulation using RGB-only policies due to depth ambiguity and perspective scale issues, the authors propose mapping point clouds into high-dimensional Fourier space to enable neural networks to access high-frequency geometric details that Cartesian features often miss. This method leverages Fourier features to overcome the spectral bias of neural networks toward low-frequency functions, which hampers performance in tasks requiring fine-grained spatial reasoning. Experiments on RoboCasa, ManiSkill3, and real robot setups show that Fourier features consistently improve policy performance across diverse architectures and hyperparameters, demonstrating their effectiveness in enhancing geometric reasoning for imitation learning.
为解决RGB图像政策在机器人高精度操作中因深度模糊和视角尺度问题导致的空间推理不足，作者提出将点云从笛卡尔空间映射到高维傅里叶空间，使神经网络能直接获取高频率几何细节，弥补笛卡尔特征的不足。该方法在RoboCasa和ManiSkill3等多个基准及真实机器人实验中均显著提升策略性能，且对超参数不敏感，适用于多种编码器架构。实验表明，傅里叶特征能更有效地利用几何细节，是点云驱动模仿学习中具有普适潜力的简单工具。
Measuring Semantic Progress in Multi-turn Dialogue via Information Gain
Authors: Paul He, Shiva Kasiviswanathan, Dominik Janzing
First: 2026-06-10T17:04:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:04:59+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 26 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating multi-turn dialogue is challenging because quality emerges across turns rather than within individual responses. We focus on a key dimension of information-seeking dialogue: semantic progress, defined as the accumulation of new, question-relevant, and non-redundant information over the course of a conversation. We formalize semantic progress as question-conditioned uncertainty reduction and introduce an information-theoretic metric that approximates it in embedding space. Our main estimator uses a tractable Gaussian formulation with closed-form updates, while a complementary maximum-entropy argument shows why log-determinant structure arises more broadly when only second-order embedding information is retained. This formulation yields desirable theoretical properties, including monotonicity, additive decomposition of total information gain across turns, and diminishing returns for redundant evidence. Unlike LLM-as-a-judge approaches, our metric requires no autoregressive inference at evaluation time and is fully reproducible for a fixed embedding model. Experiments on MT-Bench, Chatbot Arena, and UltraFeedback show that the proposed metric achieves competitive agreement with human judgments despite targeting only semantic progress, with improved alignment on MT-Bench and UltraFeedback compared to several LLM-based judges. Notably, the method remains effective with lightweight embedding models under CPU-only execution, indicating that semantic progress can be captured without reliance on large model capacity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过信息增益衡量多轮对话中的语义进展
评估多轮对话具有挑战性，因为对话质量是在多轮交互中逐步显现，而非单个回复内。我们聚焦于信息寻求对话的关键维度——语义进展，即对话过程中积累的新、相关且非冗余的信息。我们将语义进展形式化为条件于问题的不确定性降低，并引入一种信息论度量，该度量在嵌入空间中近似语义进展。我们的主要估计器采用可处理的高斯公式并具有闭式更新，而互补的最大熵论证解释了为何仅保留二阶嵌入信息时，对数行列式结构更广泛出现。该公式具有理想的理论性质，包括单调性、跨轮次总信息增益的可加分解，以及冗余证据的边际收益递减。与LLM作为裁判的方法不同，我们的度量在评估时无需自回归推理，且对于固定嵌入模型完全可复现。在MT-Bench、Chatbot Arena和UltraFeedback上的实验表明，尽管仅针对语义进展，该度量仍能与人类判断达成竞争性一致，并在MT-Bench和UltraFeedback上相较若干LLM裁判表现更优。值得注意的是，该方法在仅使用CPU执行的轻量级嵌入模型下仍有效，表明语义进展无需依赖大模型容量即可捕捉。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of evaluating multi-turn dialogue where quality accumulates across responses, this work formalizes semantic progress as question-conditioned uncertainty reduction and introduces an information-theoretic metric approximated in embedding space. Using a tractable Gaussian formulation with closed-form updates and supported by a maximum-entropy argument, the method captures monotonic, additive, and diminishing-return properties of information gain. Experiments on MT-Bench, Chatbot Arena, and UltraFeedback demonstrate that the metric achieves competitive alignment with human judgments while being computationally lightweight and fully reproducible, even under CPU-only execution with small embedding models.
该论文针对多轮对话评估难题，聚焦语义进展——即对话过程中累积的新、非冗余、与问题相关的信息。其将语义进展形式化为条件不确定性降低，并在嵌入空间中提出一种信息论度量，采用可计算的高斯模型与闭式更新。该方法基于最大熵论证，具备单调性、跨轮次可加分解等理想性质。在MT-Bench、Chatbot Arena和UltraFeedback上的实验表明，该度量在仅关注语义进展的前提下，与人类判断高度一致，且在MT-Bench和UltraFeedback上优于多个基于大模型的评估器，同时在仅使用CPU和轻量级嵌入模型时仍保持有效性。
PROJECTMEM: A Local-First, Event-Sourced Memory and Judgment Layer for AI Coding Agents
Authors: Ripon Chandra Malo, Tong Qiu
First: 2026-06-10T17:02:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T17:02:56+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures, 1 table. Code: https://github.com/riponcm/projectmem
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI coding assistants now support a growing share of software work, from quick scripts to production applications. Yet these agents remain largely stateless: each new session re-reads project files, re-derives prior decisions, and - most costly - may repeat debugging attempts that already failed. Reconstructing this context can consume an estimated 5,000-20,000 tokens per session; the bottleneck is often not model capability but missing project memory. We present projectmem, an open-source, local-first memory and judgment layer for AI coding agents. projectmem records development as an append-only, plain-text event log of typed events - issues, attempts, fixes, decisions, and notes - and deterministically projects that log into compact, AI-readable summaries served through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Beyond storage, projectmem adds a deterministic pre-action gate that warns an agent before it repeats a previously failed fix or edits a known-fragile file. We frame this as Memory-as-Governance: memory that does not merely answer the agent but acts on its next action. The system runs fully offline with no telemetry; its immutable log also serves as a provenance trail for reproducible, auditable AI-assisted development. projectmem ships as a three-dependency Python package (14 MCP tools, 19 CLI commands, 37 automated tests) and is evaluated through a two-month self-study across 10 projects comprising 207 logged events. Source code: https://github.com/riponcm/projectmem.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PROJECTMEM：面向AI编码代理的本地优先、事件溯源式记忆与判断层
AI编码助手如今已支持越来越多的软件工作，从快速脚本到生产级应用。然而这些代理仍大多无状态：每次新会话均需重新读取项目文件、重新推导先前决策，且最昂贵的是可能重复已失败的调试尝试。重建此上下文每会话约消耗5,000至20,000个token；瓶颈往往并非模型能力不足，而是缺少项目记忆。我们提出projectmem，一个面向AI编码代理的开源、本地优先记忆与判断层。projectmem将开发过程记录为仅追加、纯文本的事件日志（包含问题、尝试、修复、决策与注释），并确定性地将该日志投影为紧凑、AI可读的摘要，通过模型上下文协议（MCP）提供服务。除存储外，projectmem还增加一个确定性预动作门控，可在代理重复先前失败的修复或编辑已知脆弱文件前发出警告。我们将此机制称为“记忆即治理”：记忆不仅回答代理，更作用于其下一步行动。系统完全离线运行，无任何遥测；其不可变日志亦作为可复现、可审计的AI辅助开发的溯源记录。projectmem以一个仅含三个依赖的Python包形式发布（含14个MCP工具、19个CLI命令、37个自动化测试），并通过在10个项目（共207个日志事件）上为期两个月的自研评估。源代码：https://github.com/riponcm/projectmem。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of stateless AI coding agents that repeatedly reprocess project context and waste tokens on redundant debugging, the authors introduce projectmem, a local-first, event-sourced memory layer that logs typed development events as an append-only text log. This log is deterministically summarized via the Model Context Protocol for efficient model consumption and includes a pre-action gate that prevents agents from repeating failed fixes or editing fragile files. Evaluated across 10 projects over two months with 207 logged events, projectmem reduced redundant work and improved agent reliability without requiring cloud connectivity or telemetry.
为了解决AI编程代理因缺乏项目记忆而重复劳动、浪费大量token的问题，作者提出了projectmem，一个本地优先、事件溯源的记忆层，以追加式纯文本日志记录开发过程中的操作事件。该日志通过模型上下文协议生成可被AI读取的紧凑摘要，并在代理执行前加入一个预检门禁，阻止其重复失败修复或修改脆弱文件。在10个项目为期两个月的自研测试中，共记录207个事件，结果显示该系统显著减少了重复劳动，提升了代理的可靠性，且无需云端连接或遥测数据。
UI2Code^N: UI-to-Code Generation as Interactive Visual Optimization
Authors: Zhen Yang, Wenyi Hong, Mingde Xu, Xinyue Fan, Weihan Wang, Jiale Cheng, Xiaotao Gu, Jie Tang
First: 2025-11-11T13:00:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:57:17+00:00
Comments: 27 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
UI-to-code aims to translate UI screenshots into executable front-end code. Despite progress with vision-language models (VLMs), most existing methods formulate UI-to-code as a single-pass generation, which mismatches real-world UI development that is inherently iterative and feedback-driven. We reformulate UI-to-code as an interactive visual optimization problem, where code generation is embedded in a closed-loop process of execution, visual inspection, and iterative refinement driven by rendered visual feedback. To address the non-differentiability of visual objectives and the noise of absolute visual evaluators, we propose Relative Visual Policy Optimization (RVPO), a preference-based reinforcement learning method that optimizes relative visual rankings among rendered candidates under execution feedback. We instantiate this paradigm in UI2Code^N, an open-source 9B model trained via continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on UI drafting, UI polishing, and UI editing benchmarks, even outperforming larger models, with performance consistently improving through iterative visual optimization. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/zai-org/UI2Code_N.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UI2Code^N：将UI转换为代码视为交互式视觉优化
UI到代码旨在将UI截图转换为可执行的前端代码。尽管视觉语言模型（VLMs）已取得进展，但现有大多数方法将UI到代码视为单次生成，这与现实世界中固有的迭代和反馈驱动的UI开发不符。我们重新将UI到代码定义为交互式视觉优化问题，其中代码生成嵌入在一个闭环过程中，包括执行、视觉检查和由渲染视觉反馈驱动的迭代优化。为解决视觉目标的不可微性和绝对视觉评估器的噪声问题，我们提出相对视觉策略优化（RVPO），这是一种基于偏好的强化学习方法，通过执行反馈优化渲染候选方案之间的相对视觉排序。我们将在UI2Code^N中实现这一范式，这是一个开源的9B模型，通过持续预训练、监督微调和强化学习训练而成。实验表明，该方法在UI草拟、UI润色和UI编辑基准测试中达到最先进性能，甚至优于更大模型，且性能随迭代视觉优化持续提升。我们的代码和模型可在https://github.com/zai-org/UI2Code_N获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between single-pass UI-to-code generation and real-world iterative UI development, this work reformulates the task as interactive visual optimization, embedding code generation within a closed-loop process of execution, visual inspection, and refinement. It introduces Relative Visual Policy Optimization (RVPO), a reinforcement learning method that optimizes code based on relative visual rankings rather than absolute metrics, mitigating noise from visual evaluators. Evaluated on UI drafting, polishing, and editing benchmarks, UI2Code^N—a 9B model trained via continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and RL—achieves state-of-the-art results, often surpassing larger models, with performance improving iteratively through visual feedback.
为弥合现有单次生成方法与真实迭代式UI开发之间的差距，本文将UI到代码生成重构为交互式视觉优化问题，将代码生成嵌入执行、视觉检查与迭代优化的闭环流程。为应对视觉目标不可微分及评估器噪声问题，提出相对视觉策略优化（RVPO）方法，基于渲染候选结果的相对视觉排序进行强化学习优化。在UI草拟、润色与编辑基准测试中，开源的9B模型UI2Code^N通过持续预训练、监督微调与强化学习训练，达到当前最优性能，甚至超越更大模型，且性能随视觉反馈迭代持续提升。
Synthetic Homes: A Multimodal Generative AI Pipeline for Residential Building Data Generation under Data Scarcity
Authors: Jackson Eshbaugh, Chetan Tiwari, Jorge Silveyra
First: 2025-09-11T18:53:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:55:53+00:00
Comments: 37 pages; 2 appendices; 6 figures; 2 tables. Code available at https://github.com/Lafayette-EshbaughSilveyra-Group/synthetic-homes
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Computational models have emerged as powerful tools for multi-scale energy modeling research at the building and urban scale, supporting data-driven analysis across building and urban energy systems. However, these models require large amounts of building parameter data that is often inaccessible, expensive to collect, or subject to privacy constraints. We introduce a modular, multimodal generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework that integrates image, tabular, and simulation-based components and produces synthetic residential building datasets from publicly available county records and images, and present an end-to-end pipeline instantiating this framework. To reduce typical Large Language Model (LLM) challenges, we evaluate our model's components using occlusion-based visual focus analysis. Our analysis demonstrates that our selected vision-language model achieves greater visual focus than a GPT-based alternative for building image processing. We also assess realism of our results against a national reference dataset, finding that our synthetic data overlaps more than 95% for three of the four selected variables. This work reduces dependence on costly or restricted data sources, lowering barriers to building-scale energy research and Machine Learning (ML)-driven urban energy modeling, and therefore enabling scalable downstream tasks such as energy modeling, retrofit analysis, and urban-scale simulation under data scarcity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合成住宅：在数据稀缺条件下用于住宅建筑数据生成的多模态生成式人工智能管道
计算模型已成为建筑与城市尺度多尺度能源建模研究的强大工具，支持建筑与城市能源系统的数据驱动分析。然而，这些模型需要大量建筑参数数据，而这些数据往往难以获取、收集成本高昂或受隐私限制。我们提出了一种模块化、多模态生成式人工智能框架，整合图像、表格和仿真组件，从公开的县记录和图像生成合成住宅建筑数据集，并展示实现该框架的端到端管道。为降低大型语言模型（LLM）的典型挑战，我们采用基于遮挡的视觉焦点分析评估模型各组件。分析表明，所选视觉-语言模型在建筑图像处理中比基于GPT的替代方案具有更高的视觉焦点。我们还通过与国家级参考数据集对比评估结果的真实性，发现所生成数据在四个选定变量中的三个重合度超过95%。本工作减少了对昂贵或受限数据源的依赖，降低了建筑尺度能源研究和基于机器学习的城市能源建模的门槛，从而在数据稀缺条件下支持可扩展的下游任务，如能源建模、改造分析和城市尺度仿真。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity and high cost of real residential building data for energy modeling, this work proposes a multimodal generative AI pipeline that synthesizes building datasets from public county records and images. The framework integrates vision, language, and simulation components in a modular design, and employs occlusion-based visual focus analysis to evaluate and optimize its vision-language model against LLM alternatives. Experimental results show the selected model achieves superior visual focus for building image processing and produces synthetic data that overlaps with a national reference dataset in over 95% of key variables, demonstrating high realism and utility for downstream energy modeling and urban simulation tasks under data constraints.
为应对住宅建筑数据稀缺对能源建模研究的制约，本文提出一种多模态生成式AI管道，通过公开的县区记录与图像合成建筑数据集。该框架融合视觉-语言模型、表格数据与仿真组件，并采用遮挡式视觉焦点分析评估模型性能。实验表明，所选视觉-语言模型在图像处理上优于GPT类替代方案，且合成数据在四个关键变量中的三个与国家级参考数据重合度超过95%，证明其高真实性和在数据稀缺条件下支持能源建模等下游任务的可行性。
A Five-Plane Reference Architecture for Runtime Governance of Production AI Agents
Authors: Krti Tallam
First: 2026-06-10T16:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:54:47+00:00
Comments: 65 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables. Reference architecture with a reference implementation of the policy-engine core and microbenchmark results; full-system evaluation identified as future work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Enterprise security was built to govern data boundaries: the protected surface was data at rest and in transit, and the controls -- access control, data-loss prevention, perimeter inspection -- governed crossings of that boundary. Production AI agents dissolve this assumption. An agent reads context, calls tools, invokes connectors, and modifies systems of record on an enterprise's behalf, so risk moves inside the workflow, into sequences of individually-permitted actions that may transform a business process no one authorized. Existing policy engines do not extend to this regime: they evaluate request-time decisions against atomic principals, where agentic systems require stateful evaluation against composite principals whose authority attenuates through delegation chains. We present a reference architecture for the runtime governance of production agents, built from four composable primitives: a five-plane decomposition (a reasoning plane that adjudicates intent, and four enforcement planes -- network, identity, endpoint, data -- that realize the decision), stop-anywhere mediation, composite principals with capability attenuation, and audit as a structured evidence substrate. We define a taxonomy of six interruption primitives that generalize allow and deny, state and argue for four correctness invariants, and demonstrate the foreclosure of seven production-agent threats across five concrete workflows. A reference implementation of the policy-engine core supplies measured evidence: attenuation correctness and evidence reconstructability hold on every trial, adjudication runs in single-digit microseconds, and the audit substrate's tamper-evidence behaves exactly as designed. We are explicit about scope: the architecture governs delegated action, not model behavior, and a full-system evaluation against a live agent benchmark is the invited next step.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向生产级AI代理运行时治理的五层参考架构
企业安全体系旨在管理数据边界：受保护的表面是静态和传输中的数据，控制措施——访问控制、数据防泄漏、边界检查——管理跨越该边界的活动。生产级AI代理则打破了这一假设：代理读取上下文、调用工具、调用连接器，并代表企业修改记录系统，因此风险进入工作流，转化为一系列经逐项授权的动作，可能改变无人授权的业务流程。现有策略引擎无法适应这一环境：它们在请求时评估原子性主体，而代理系统需要基于复合主体的有状态评估，其权限通过委托链递减。 我们提出了一种面向生产代理运行时治理的参考架构，由四个可组合的基本构件构成：五层分解（一个用于裁定意图的推理层，以及四个实现决策的执行层——网络、身份、终端、数据层）、任意位置中断调解、具备权限递减能力的复合主体，以及作为结构化证据基底的审计机制。我们定义了六种中断原语，泛化了允许与拒绝，阐述了四个正确性不变量，并在五个具体工作流中演示了对七类生产代理威胁的遏制。策略引擎核心的参考实现提供了可衡量的证据：权限递减正确性与证据可重构性在每次试验中均成立，裁定过程耗时在毫秒级，审计基底的篡改证据行为完全符合设计。我们明确界定范围：该架构治理的是委托行为，而非模型行为，完整系统评估针对真实代理基准测试是下一步工作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to govern AI agents that operate within enterprise workflows by modifying systems and delegating actions — unlike traditional data-boundary security — the paper proposes a five-plane reference architecture for runtime governance. The method decomposes enforcement into reasoning and four operational planes (network, identity, endpoint, data), supports composite principals with capability attenuation, and introduces interruption primitives and audit as structured evidence. Experimental results show the architecture successfully mitigates seven production-agent threats across five workflows, with the policy engine achieving sub-millisecond adjudication, correct attenuation in all trials, and tamper-evident audit logs, while explicitly excluding model behavior from scope.
为应对生产级AI代理带来的动态、状态化治理需求，该论文提出一种五平面参考架构，将运行时控制分解为意图裁决的推理平面与网络、身份、终端、数据四个执行平面。架构引入带能力衰减的复合主体、任意中断调解机制与结构化审计证据，以处理代理授权链。在五个工作流中实验证明可有效防御七类代理威胁，策略引擎核心实现亚毫秒级裁决与完美衰减正确性及审计可重建性，明确限定治理范围为授权行为，不涉及模型行为。
MemToolAgent: Leveraging Memory for Tool Using Agents Based on Environment and User Feedback
Authors: Suleyman Armagan Er, Danilo Ribeiro, Yogesh Virkar, Surafel Lakew, Adi Kalyanpur, James Gung, Thomas Delteil, Arshit Gupta
First: 2026-06-06T00:15:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:54:40+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern large language model (LLM) agents can use external tools to help users solve complex tasks. However, for problems that require learning from long-term historical events or from previous agent-environment interactions, LLM agents are required to use memory mechanisms to store and retrieve experiences. While sophisticated memory systems exist for dialogue agents, few studies have empirically examined how to improve agents' tool-using capabilities through past user-agent conversations. We propose MemToolAgent, a framework that improves tool use through memory management. Our approach contains a memory extraction module that processes past experiences into structured memory entries, and a retrieval module that dynamically selects a subset of the stored memory entries. This enables more personalized and accurate responses aligned with user preferences and feedback without requiring LLM fine-tuning. In summary, this work has three main contributions: (1) a unified memory entry format that improves both general-purpose and personalized tool use without LLM fine-tuning, (2) a reflection-based memory extraction that uses environment and user feedback to distill wrong executions into critiques to store, and (3) a retrieval module that chooses how many past experiences to use based on the memory similarity distribution. MemToolAgent achieves 29%, 80%, and 17% relative improvements compared to strong baselines on the WorkBench, NESTFUL, and PEToolBench benchmarks, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemToolAgent：基于环境与用户反馈的内存增强工具使用代理
现代大语言模型（LLM）代理可借助外部工具帮助用户解决复杂任务。然而，对于需要从长期历史事件或先前代理-环境交互中学习的问题，LLM代理需使用记忆机制存储和检索经验。尽管对话代理已有复杂的记忆系统，但鲜有研究实证考察如何通过过往用户-代理对话提升代理的工具使用能力。我们提出MemToolAgent框架，通过记忆管理提升工具使用能力。该方法包含记忆提取模块，将过往经验处理为结构化记忆条目；以及检索模块，动态选择存储记忆条目子集。此方法无需对LLM进行微调，即可实现更个性化、更精准、符合用户偏好与反馈的响应。本工作主要贡献有三：（1）统一的记忆条目格式，无需LLM微调即可提升通用与个性化工具使用能力；（2）基于反思的记忆提取机制，利用环境与用户反馈将错误执行提炼为批评并存储；（3）检索模块根据记忆相似度分布动态决定使用多少过往经验。MemToolAgent在WorkBench、NESTFUL和PEToolBench基准测试中分别相较强基线模型实现29%、80%和17%的相对性能提升。

Motivated by the need for LLM agents to effectively use tools in tasks requiring long-term memory of past interactions, the paper introduces MemToolAgent, a framework that enhances tool usage through memory management without fine-tuning the LLM. It employs a memory extraction module to convert past agent-environment and user feedback interactions into structured memory entries, and a retrieval module that dynamically selects relevant memories based on similarity distribution. Experiments across three benchmarks—WorkBench, NESTFUL, and PEToolBench—show that MemToolAgent outperforms strong baselines by 29%, 80%, and 17% respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness in improving personalized and accurate tool use through memory-driven reflection and retrieval.
为提升大语言模型代理在需要长期记忆历史交互的任务中使用工具的能力，本文提出MemToolAgent框架，通过记忆管理增强工具使用效果而无需微调模型。该框架包含记忆提取模块，将过往代理-环境交互与用户反馈转化为结构化记忆条目，并通过检索模块根据相似度分布动态选择相关记忆。在三个基准测试中，MemToolAgent相较强基线分别提升29%、80%和17%，验证了其在个性化与精准工具使用方面的有效性。
Anatomically Conditioned Recurrent Refinement for Topology-Aware Circle of Willis Segmentation
Authors: Juraj Perić, Marija Habijan, Dario Mužević, Irena Galić, Danilo Babin, Aleksandra Pižurica
First: 2026-06-10T16:53:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:53:13+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 4 figures, 1 table. Accepted at EUSIPCO 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Segmenting the Circle of Willis (CoW) from Magnetic Resonance Angiography (MRA) is challenging due to complex topology and thin vascular structures that are prone to fragmentation. Standard Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) often fail to capture these topological constraints, resulting in "broken vessel" artifacts. To address this, we propose the Anatomically Conditioned Recurrent Refinement U-Net (AC2RUNet). Our architecture decouples segmentation into two streams: a Static Stream that extracts invariant anatomical features and a lightweight Dynamic Stream that iteratively refines topological errors over time. We further introduce a dynamic curriculum learning strategy that transitions from high-recall geometric supervision to topology-aware constraints. Validated on the TopCoW dataset, AC2RUNet substantially reduces Hausdorff Distance (4.72 mm vs 9.17 mm) and Betti number errors (0.19 vs 0.40), improving topological connectivity over the nnU-Net baseline while maintaining comparable volumetric Dice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于解剖学条件的递归精炼网络用于拓扑感知的Willis环分割
从磁共振血管成像（MRA）中分割Willis环（CoW）具有挑战性，因为其拓扑结构复杂且血管结构纤细易断裂。标准卷积神经网络（CNN）常无法捕捉这些拓扑约束，导致出现“断裂血管”伪影。为此，我们提出解剖学条件递归精炼U-Net（AC2RUNet）。该架构将分割分为两个流：静态流提取不变的解剖特征，轻量级动态流则随时间迭代修正拓扑错误。我们进一步引入动态课程学习策略，从高召回率几何监督过渡到拓扑感知约束。在TopCoW数据集上的验证表明，AC2RUNet显著降低Hausdorff距离（4.72 mm vs 9.17 mm）和贝蒂数误差（0.19 vs 0.40），在保持与nnU-Net基线相当体积Dice值的同时，提升了拓扑连通性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of accurately segmenting the Circle of Willis due to its complex topology and thin, easily fragmented vessels, the authors propose AC2RUNet, a dual-stream architecture combining a static anatomical feature extractor with a lightweight recurrent refinement module that iteratively corrects topological errors. The model employs a dynamic curriculum learning strategy that gradually shifts from geometric to topology-aware supervision. Experiments on the TopCoW dataset show that AC2RUNet significantly reduces Hausdorff Distance from 9.17 mm to 4.72 mm and Betti number error from 0.40 to 0.19, while achieving comparable volumetric Dice scores to the nnU-Net baseline, demonstrating improved topological fidelity.
为了解决由于解剖结构复杂和血管纤细易断裂导致的Willis环分割难题，作者提出AC2RUNet架构，该架构包含静态解剖特征提取器与轻量级递归精炼模块，后者通过迭代修正拓扑错误。模型采用动态课程学习策略，逐步从几何监督过渡到拓扑感知监督。在TopCoW数据集上的实验表明，AC2RUNet显著降低Hausdorff距离（4.72毫米 vs 9.17毫米）和贝蒂数误差（0.19 vs 0.40），在保持与nnU-Net相当的体积Dice值的同时，实现了更优的拓扑连通性。
Harness In-Context Operator Learning with Chain of Operators
Authors: Minghui Yang, Ling Guo, Liu Yang
First: 2026-06-10T16:53:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:53:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural operators approximate mappings between function spaces, but often generalize poorly to other operators and usually require fine-tuning or retraining. In-Context Operator Networks (ICON) addresses this issue by prompting the model with numerical context so that the model learns specific operators from prompts and adapt to different operators without fine-tuning. However, ICON may still fail to generalize to out-of-distribution (OOD) operator tasks. Inpired by the success of harness engineering of Large Language models (LLMs), we introduce Chain of Operators (CHOP), a framework that harness a frozen ICON to OOD operator tasks without updating its parameters. Specifically, CHOP constructs a chain of operators consisting of explicit elementary transformations and the frozen ICON. Experiments on a scalar conservation law and a mean-field control problem show that CHOP reduces relative inference error over direct ICON evaluation, while each operator in the chain remains interpretable and in closed form. A chain constructed on one PDE family further generalizes to a different family, indicating shared mechanisms across harness systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用操作符链实现上下文内操作符学习
神经操作符可近似函数空间间的映射，但通常难以泛化至其他操作符，且通常需要微调或重新训练。上下文操作符网络（ICON）通过提供数值上下文提示模型，使模型从提示中学习特定操作符，并在不微调的情况下适应不同操作符。然而，ICON仍可能无法泛化至分布外（OOD）操作符任务。受大型语言模型（LLM）提示工程成功启发，我们引入操作符链（CHOP）框架，通过冻结ICON参数，使其在不更新参数的情况下处理OOD操作符任务。具体而言，CHOP构建由显式初等变换与冻结ICON组成的操作符链。在标量守恒律和平均场控制问题上的实验表明，CHOP相比直接ICON评估显著降低相对推理误差，且链中每个操作符保持可解释性与闭式表达。在一类偏微分方程家族上构建的链可泛化至另一类家族，表明提示系统间存在共享机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of neural operators to out-of-distribution tasks and the success of harnessing LLMs, this work introduces Chain of Operators (CHOP), a framework that leverages a frozen In-Context Operator Network (ICON) to solve new operator tasks without parameter updates. CHOP constructs a sequence of elementary transformations combined with the frozen ICON, enabling the model to adapt to unseen operators through composition. Experiments on scalar conservation laws and mean-field control problems demonstrate that CHOP reduces relative inference error compared to direct ICON evaluation, while maintaining interpretability and closed-form structure for each operator in the chain. Furthermore, a chain trained on one PDE family generalizes to another, suggesting shared underlying mechanisms across different operator domains.
为解决神经算子在分布外任务上泛化能力差的问题，受大语言模型调用工程成功启发，本文提出链式算子（CHOP）框架，通过组合显式初等变换与冻结的上下文算子网络（ICON），在不更新参数的情况下适应新算子任务。实验表明，CHOP在标量守恒律与平均场控制问题上相较直接ICON评估显著降低相对推理误差，且每个链式算子保持可解释与闭式结构；在不同偏微分方程族间亦能迁移，表明调用系统间存在共享机制。
Slots, Transitions, Loops: Learning Composable World Models for ARC
Authors: Gege Gao, Bernhard Schölkopf, Andreas Geiger
First: 2026-06-10T16:51:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:51:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ARC tests in-context rule induction: given a few input-output demonstrations, a model must infer the hidden rule and apply it to a new query. While many approaches express ARC rules through language, code, or symbolic programs, ARC itself is visual-symbolic: rules appear as grid transitions over objects, colors, shapes, and spatial relations. We introduce Loop-OWM, an object-centric world-modeling architecture that learns these rules as composable transitions over structured states. It combines color-prototype slots, demonstration-conditioned task summaries, and a looped transition model with dense propagation and slot-conditioned correction. On both ARC-1 and ARC-2, Loop-OWM outperforms non-looped and looped baselines with comparable or fewer parameters. These results suggest that ARC rules can be learned not only as language descriptions or searched programs, but also as transitions over visual-symbolic world states.
中文标题/摘要
标题：槽位、转换与循环：为ARC学习可组合的世界模型
ARC测试在上下文中的规则归纳：给定少量输入-输出演示，模型必须推断隐藏规则并将其应用于新查询。虽然许多方法通过语言、代码或符号程序表达ARC规则，但ARC本身是视觉-符号的：规则表现为对象、颜色、形状和空间关系上的网格转换。我们引入Loop-OWM，一种以对象为中心的世界建模架构，它将这些规则学习为结构化状态上的可组合转换。该架构结合颜色原型槽位、演示条件任务摘要和带密集传播与槽位条件修正的循环转换模型。在ARC-1和ARC-2上，Loop-OWM在参数数量相当或更少的情况下，优于非循环和循环基线模型。这些结果表明，ARC规则不仅可作为语言描述或搜索程序学习，也可作为视觉-符号世界状态上的转换学习。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model visual-symbolic rules in ARC tasks where rules manifest as grid transitions, the authors propose Loop-OWM, an object-centric architecture that learns rules as composable transitions over structured states using color-prototype slots, task summaries conditioned on demonstrations, and a looped transition model with dense propagation and slot-conditioned correction. Experiments on ARC-1 and ARC-2 show that Loop-OWM outperforms both non-looped and looped baselines while using comparable or fewer parameters, indicating that visual-symbolic world state transitions can effectively capture ARC rules without relying on language or symbolic program search.
为应对ARC任务中规则以网格状态转换形式呈现的视觉符号特性，作者提出Loop-OWM架构，该架构以对象为中心，通过颜色原型槽、基于演示的任务摘要和循环转换模型（含密集传播与槽条件修正）学习可组合的状态转换规则。在ARC-1和ARC-2上的实验表明，Loop-OWM在参数量相当或更少的情况下优于非循环和循环基线模型，证明视觉符号世界状态转换可有效捕捉ARC规则，无需依赖语言描述或符号程序搜索。
RSTR: Reducing SpatioTemporal Redundancy in Diffusion Transformers
Authors: Ruitong Sun, Tianze Yang, Wei Niu, Jin Sun
Venue: ICML
First: 2025-12-16T05:11:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:47:57+00:00
Comments: International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have achieved remarkable success in image generation, yet their deployment is hindered by high computational costs. We identify two sources of redundancy. First, temporal redundancy: Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) applies costly dual forward passes at every timestep, yet guidance matters only at specific steps, and variable scales at critical steps can compensate for skipping others. Second, spatial redundancy: under variable guidance, different transformer blocks exhibit heterogeneous sensitivity, yet uniform calibration across all blocks wastes computation while failing to address their varying requirements. We present RSTR, the first framework to jointly reduce spatiotemporal redundancy in diffusion transformers. Stage-1 addresses temporal redundancy through evolutionary search, discovering sparse guidance schedules with variable scales. Stage-2 addresses spatial redundancy through adaptive rank allocation, assigning calibration capacities to transformer regions based on their sensitivity. Experiments on DiT-XL/2, PixArt-$α$, FLUX, and state-of-the-art Qwen-Image demonstrate 50%-70% compute savings while maintaining or improving quality. On DiT-XL/2, RSTR achieves 57% savings with 15% FID improvement; on Qwen-Image, 3.43$\times$ speedup with preserved quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RSTR：减少扩散Transformer中的时空冗余
扩散Transformer（DiTs）在图像生成方面取得了显著成功，但其部署受限于高昂的计算成本。我们识别出两种冗余来源：第一，时间冗余：分类器自由引导（CFG）在每个时间步均需进行昂贵的双前向传递，但引导仅在特定步骤重要，且在关键步骤使用可变尺度可补偿跳过其他步骤；第二，空间冗余：在可变引导下，不同Transformer块表现出异质敏感性，但对所有块统一校准浪费计算且无法满足其不同需求。我们提出RSTR，这是首个联合减少扩散Transformer中时空冗余的框架。第一阶段通过进化搜索解决时间冗余，发现具有可变尺度的稀疏引导调度；第二阶段通过自适应秩分配解决空间冗余，根据各区域敏感性分配校准能力。在DiT-XL/2、PixArt-α、FLUX及最新Qwen-Image上实验表明，计算节省50%-70%的同时保持或提升质量。在DiT-XL/2上，RSTR实现57%节省并提升15% FID；在Qwen-Image上，速度提升3.43倍且质量保持不变。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of Diffusion Transformers in image generation, this work introduces RSTR, a framework that jointly reduces spatio-temporal redundancy. It tackles temporal redundancy by using evolutionary search to discover sparse, variable-scale guidance schedules, and spatial redundancy by adaptively allocating calibration capacity to transformer blocks based on their sensitivity. Experiments on DiT-XL/2, PixArt-α, FLUX, and Qwen-Image show 50%-70% compute savings without quality degradation, with DiT-XL/2 achieving 57% savings and 15% FID improvement, and Qwen-Image gaining 3.43x speedup.
为降低扩散Transformer在图像生成中的高计算开销，本文提出RSTR框架，联合减少时空冗余。其通过进化搜索发现稀疏且可变尺度的引导调度以解决时间冗余，通过自适应秩分配根据各Transformer块的敏感度分配校准能力以解决空间冗余。在DiT-XL/2、PixArt-α、FLUX和Qwen-Image上实验表明，计算节省率达50%-70%，且质量未下降，其中DiT-XL/2节省57%并提升FID 15%，Qwen-Image实现3.43倍加速且质量保持不变。
FOCUS: DLLMs Know How to Tame Their Compute Bound
Authors: Kaihua Liang, Xin Tan, An Zhong, Hong Xu, Marco Canini
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-30T18:52:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:44:13+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) offer a compelling alternative to Auto-Regressive models, but their deployment is constrained by high decoding cost. In this work, we identify a key inefficiency in DLLM decoding: while computation is parallelized over token blocks, only a small subset of tokens is decodable at each diffusion step, causing most compute to be wasted on non-decodable tokens. We further observe a strong correlation between attention-derived token importance and token-wise decoding probability. Based on this insight, we propose FOCUS, an inference system designed for DLLMs. By dynamically focusing computation on decodable tokens and evicting non-decodable ones on-the-fly, FOCUS increases the effective batch size, alleviating compute limitations and enabling scalable throughput. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that FOCUS achieves up to 3.52$\times$ throughput improvement over the production-grade engine LMDeploy in large-batch settings, while preserving or improving generation quality across multiple benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：聚焦：扩散大语言模型懂得如何驾驭计算瓶颈
扩散大语言模型（DLLMs）是自回归模型的有力替代方案，但其部署受限于高昂的解码成本。本研究发现DLLM解码中的关键低效：虽然计算在token块上并行化，但在每个扩散步骤中仅一小部分token可解码，导致大部分计算浪费在不可解码的token上。我们进一步观察到，注意力衍生的token重要性与token级解码概率存在强相关性。基于此洞察，我们提出FOCUS，专为DLLM设计的推理系统。通过动态聚焦计算于可解码token并即时移除不可解码token，FOCUS提升有效批大小，缓解计算限制，实现可扩展吞吐量。实证评估表明，FOCUS在大批次设置下相较生产级引擎LMDeploy最高提升3.52倍吞吐量，同时在多个基准测试中保持或提升生成质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high decoding costs of Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs), this work identifies that most compute is wasted on non-decodable tokens during diffusion steps, despite parallel computation over token blocks. The authors leverage the correlation between attention-derived token importance and decoding probability to design FOCUS, an inference system that dynamically focuses computation on decodable tokens and evicts non-decodable ones. Experimental results show FOCUS achieves up to 3.52x throughput improvement over LMDeploy in large-batch settings while maintaining or enhancing generation quality across benchmarks.
鉴于扩散大语言模型（DLLM）解码成本高昂，本文发现尽管计算在令牌块上并行化，但每一步扩散中仅有少量令牌可解码，导致大部分计算资源被浪费。作者利用注意力衍生令牌重要性与令牌解码概率之间的强相关性，设计了FOCUS系统，该系统动态聚焦于可解码令牌并实时剔除不可解码令牌。实验表明，FOCUS在大批次设置下相较生产级引擎LMDeploy提升高达3.52倍吞吐量，同时保持或提升了多个基准测试中的生成质量。
UGV-Conditioned Multi-UAV Informative Planning on a Shared Exposure Belief
Authors: Lars Oerlemans, Moji Shi, Marija Popovic
First: 2026-06-10T16:42:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:42:08+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safe ground navigation in large, threat-augmented environments requires aerial support that actively reduces the risks that a ground vehicle faces along its route. Existing aerial reconnaissance systems focus on mapping or covering the environment, but do not direct sensing toward regions that are most relevant for ground vehicle safety. In this paper, we address the problem of coordinating a team of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to improve the safety of an unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) navigating through unknown threat zones. A key aspect of our approach is a shared exposure belief that is updated online from aerial observations and used jointly by the UAV team and the ground vehicle. This enables us to direct aerial sensing towards route-relevant regions while allowing the UGV to replan around newly revealed threats. We coordinate the UAV team through spatial region assignment to avoid redundant sensing. Simulation experiments show that our approach reduces cumulative UGV exposure by 38% compared to a system that does not account for hazard levels, and reduces redundant aerial coverage from 38.8% to 3.7% under our multi-UAV coordination scheme.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于UGV状态的多无人机信息规划与共享暴露信念
在大型威胁增强环境中，为保障地面车辆沿路径行驶的安全，需要空中支持主动降低其面临的风险。现有空中侦察系统侧重于环境测绘或覆盖，但未将传感资源导向对地面车辆安全最相关的区域。本文研究协调无人机团队以提升未知威胁区域中无人地面车辆（UGV）安全性的方法。核心在于建立一个共享暴露信念，该信念由空中观测实时更新，并由无人机团队与地面车辆共同使用，从而引导空中传感聚焦于路径相关区域，同时允许UGV根据新发现的威胁重新规划路径。我们通过空间区域分配协调无人机团队，避免冗余传感。仿真实验表明，与不考虑危害等级的系统相比，本方法可降低UGV累积暴露量38%，在多无人机协调方案下，冗余空中覆盖从38.8%降至3.7%。
Summary / 总结
To enhance UGV safety in hazardous, unknown environments, this work coordinates a team of UAVs to actively guide sensing toward threat-relevant areas rather than passively mapping the surroundings. The method employs a shared exposure belief updated in real-time from aerial observations, enabling both UAVs and the UGV to jointly adapt to newly revealed risks. UAVs are assigned spatial regions to avoid redundant sensing, improving efficiency. Experimental results show a 38% reduction in cumulative UGV exposure compared to a non-hazard-aware system and a 35.1% drop in redundant aerial coverage under multi-UAV coordination.
为提升UGV在危险未知环境中的安全性，本文协调多架UAV主动引导感知聚焦于UGV路径相关的威胁区域。方法采用实时更新的共享暴露信念，使UAV团队与UGV共同适应新发现的危险。通过空间区域分配协调UAV，避免重复覆盖。实验表明，相比不考虑危险等级的系统，UGV累积暴露降低38%，且在多UAV协调下重复覆盖比例从38.8%降至3.7%。
From 2D Grids to 1D Tokens: Reforming Shared Representations for Multimodal Image Fusion
Authors: Yuchen Xian, Yunqiu Xu, Yang He, Yi Yang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-10T16:40:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:40:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal image fusion aims to integrate complementary information from different modalities into a fused image that preserves rich local details while maintaining globally consistent appearance. Existing approaches build shared representations on 2D feature grids, which excel at modeling local structures but offer limited leverage over image-level global appearance factors. To balance these objectives, we introduce a compact 1D token interface based on a frozen pretrained image tokenizer for modeling non-local appearance/base factors. Rather than using the tokenizer as a reconstruction backbone, our design uses the 1D token space as a global carrier while retaining the 2D spatial pathway for local structure restoration. Specifically, we introduce Selective Token Editing (STE), which sparsely updates/replaces a small set of critical tokens, providing a lightweight mechanism to steer global appearance coherence while keeping the fusion backbone unchanged and avoiding extra losses. Experiments on four commonly used benchmarks show that our method achieves the best overall performance, with consistent, multi-metric improvements in both global coherence and local fidelity. Project page: https://zju-xyc.github.io/1D-Fusion-Project-Page/
中文标题/摘要
标题：从2D网格到1D令牌：为多模态图像融合重构共享表示
多模态图像融合旨在将不同模态的互补信息整合为一张融合图像，既保留丰富的局部细节，又保持全局一致的外观。现有方法在2D特征网格上构建共享表示，擅长建模局部结构，但对图像级全局外观因素的建模能力有限。为平衡这两项目标，我们引入一种基于冻结预训练图像分词器的紧凑1D令牌接口，用于建模非局部外观/基础因素。与将分词器作为重建骨干网络不同，我们的设计将1D令牌空间作为全局载体，同时保留2D空间路径以恢复局部结构。具体而言，我们提出选择性令牌编辑（STE），通过稀疏更新/替换少量关键令牌，提供一种轻量机制，引导全局外观一致性，同时保持融合骨干网络不变且无需额外损失。在四个常用基准上的实验表明，我们的方法在整体性能上表现最佳，且在全局一致性与局部保真度方面均实现多指标一致提升。项目页面：https://zju-xyc.github.io/1D-Fusion-Project-Page/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of 2D grid-based representations in capturing global image-level appearance factors during multimodal fusion, this work proposes a 1D token interface derived from a frozen pretrained image tokenizer to model non-local appearance cues. The method retains the 2D spatial pathway for local detail preservation while using the 1D token space as a global carrier, and introduces Selective Token Editing to sparsely update critical tokens without altering the fusion backbone or adding extra losses. Experimental results across four benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in both global coherence and local fidelity, achieving the best overall performance compared to existing methods.
为解决现有2D网格表示在建模图像级全局外观因素方面的不足，该研究提出基于冻结预训练图像tokenizer的1D token接口以捕捉非局部外观特征。方法保留2D空间路径用于局部细节恢复，同时将1D token空间作为全局载体，并引入稀疏更新关键token的Selective Token Editing（STE）机制，实现全局一致性引导而不改变融合主干或增加额外损失。在四个基准测试中，该方法在全局一致性与局部保真度上均取得一致提升，整体性能最优。
EvalStop: Using World Feedback to Detect and Correct Reward Overoptimization in Multi-Tenant RLHF Platforms
Authors: Guilin Zhang, Chuanyi Sun, Shahryar Sarkani, John M. Fossaceca
First: 2026-06-02T19:03:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:36:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cloud LLM fine-tuning platforms increasingly serve RLHF workloads, where a learned reward model is optimized as a proxy for human quality. As Gao et al. (2023) showed, this proxy diverges from world feedback (downstream eval metrics) under sustained optimization pressure, a phenomenon known as reward overoptimization. Existing platform schedulers ignore this divergence: non-clairvoyant schedulers optimize JCT without any quality signal, SLAQ-style quality-aware schedulers use training loss (a weaker proxy that drops monotonically through hacking), and classical per-job early stopping requires human monitoring and does not free shared GPUs. We propose EvalStop, a composable scheduling primitive that terminates jobs on k consecutive eval-score declines, releases GPUs, preserves the best checkpoint, and delegates to any base scheduler. We frame scheduler-level early stopping as a detection problem and evaluate it in a discrete-event simulator whose RLHF workload mixes reward-hacking and structurally healthy runs, with ground-truth labels hidden from schedulers. On RLHF-heavy workloads (80% RLHF, 64 GPUs), EvalStop achieves precision 98% / recall 99% / FPR 1.5% while improving JCT by 9% and cutting wasted compute by 22% over SRTF-Est (p<0.05). Trivial fixed-progress and loss-plateau competitors either incur 65% FPR on healthy RLHF or miss over half of true hacking cases. Gains compose across every base scheduler tested (9-25% JCT) and detection quality stays stable under eval noise (precision at least 91% at noise std <= 0.05) and hacking base rate (precision at least 89% across 20-80% hacking fractions).
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvalStop：利用世界反馈检测并纠正多租户RLHF平台中的奖励过优化
云端大语言模型微调平台日益承担RLHF任务，其中学习到的奖励模型被优化为人类质量的代理。正如Gao等人（2023）所示，在持续优化压力下，该代理与世界反馈（下游评估指标）出现偏离，这一现象称为奖励过优化。现有平台调度器忽视这一偏离：非预知调度器优化JCT但无质量信号；SLAQ式质量感知调度器使用训练损失（一个随黑客行为单调下降的较弱代理）；经典每任务早期停止需人工监控且无法释放共享GPU。我们提出EvalStop，一种可组合的调度原语，当连续k次评估分数下降时终止任务、释放GPU、保留最佳检查点，并委托给任意基础调度器。我们将调度级早期停止视为检测问题，并在离散事件模拟器中评估，该模拟器中的RLHF工作负载混合了奖励黑客行为与结构健康运行，且调度器无法获取真实标签。在RLHF密集型工作负载（80% RLHF，64 GPU）下，EvalStop实现精度98% / 召回率99% / 假阳性率1.5%，同时较SRTF-Est提升JCT 9%、减少浪费计算22%（p<0.05）。简单固定的进度和损失平台竞争者要么在健康RLHF中产生65%假阳性率，要么漏掉超过一半真实黑客案例。收益在所有测试的基础调度器中均可组合（JCT提升9-25%），且在评估噪声下检测质量稳定（噪声标准差≤0.05时精度至少91%），在黑客基础率变化下亦稳定（20-80%黑客比例时精度至少89%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue of reward overoptimization in RLHF platforms where reward models diverge from downstream performance, the paper introduces EvalStop, a scheduling primitive that terminates jobs when evaluation scores decline for k consecutive iterations, freeing GPUs and preserving the best checkpoint. EvalStop treats early stopping as a detection problem and is evaluated in a discrete-event simulator with mixed RLHF workloads and hidden ground-truth labels. It achieves 98% precision, 99% recall, and 1.5% false positive rate, improving job completion time by 9% and reducing wasted compute by 22% compared to SRTF-Est. Competing methods either misfire on healthy runs or miss hacking cases, while EvalStop maintains high detection quality across varying noise levels and hacking rates.
为解决RLHF平台中奖励模型因优化压力而偏离下游评估指标的问题，本文提出EvalStop，一种在连续k次评估分数下降时终止任务、释放GPU并保留最佳检查点的调度原语。EvalStop将早期停止视为检测问题，并在混合RLHF工作负载的离散事件模拟器中进行评估，且调度器无法访问真实标签。其在RLHF密集负载下实现98%精确率、99%召回率和1.5%误报率，较SRTF-Est提升9%作业完成时间并减少22%无效计算。对比方法要么漏检大量黑客行为，要么误报率高达65%，而EvalStop在不同调度器、评估噪声和黑客比例下均保持稳定检测性能。
Natural-Language Temporal Grounding in Hour-Long Videos is a Search Problem: A Benchmark and Empirical Decomposition
Authors: Sukmin Seo, Geewook Kim
First: 2026-06-10T16:35:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:35:04+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 6 figures, Code and benchmark: https://github.com/naver-ai/ExtremeWhenBench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Temporal grounding--returning the interval $[t_s, t_e]$ for a natural-language query over a video--is the language interface to long-form video, yet has been studied on short videos; the dynamics of hour-scale natural-language grounding remain underexplored. We take the position that at hour-scale, the binding constraint is search, not recognition: Video-LLMs are bottlenecked not by localizing a nearby event, but--given a natural-language query--by searching for the relevant region of a long video. To test this, we release ExtremeWhenBench, the first open hour-scale grounding benchmark (2,273 queries over 194 videos, mean 75.7 min, max 9 hr) with an open-form query distribution. Every open Video-LLM collapses while a frame-level retrieval baseline outperforms them; a failure taxonomy attributes 85% of failures to search; and a retrieve-then-ground hybrid recovers 6.7x over the monolithic Video-LLM--mirroring retrieve-then-read in open-domain QA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：小时级视频中的自然语言时间定位是一个搜索问题：一个基准测试与实证分解
时间定位——为视频中的自然语言查询返回时间区间 [t_s, t_e]——是长视频的语言接口，但此前研究主要集中在短视频上；小时级自然语言时间定位的动态特性尚未被充分探索。我们认为，在小时级尺度下，制约因素是搜索而非识别：视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）的瓶颈不在于定位附近事件，而在于根据自然语言查询搜索长视频中的相关区域。为验证这一点，我们发布了 ExtremeWhenBench，这是首个开放的小时级时间定位基准测试（覆盖194个视频、2273个查询，平均时长75.7分钟，最长9小时），采用开放形式的查询分布。所有开放的 Video-LLMs 均表现崩溃，而帧级检索基线反而优于它们；失败类型学分析表明85%的失败归因于搜索问题；而检索后定位的混合方法性能比单一 Video-LLM 提升6.7倍——这与开放域问答中的检索后阅读模式相呼应。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored challenge of temporal grounding in hour-long videos, this work argues that the core bottleneck is search rather than recognition. It introduces ExtremeWhenBench, the first open benchmark with 2,273 queries across 194 videos averaging 75.7 minutes in length. Experiments show that all tested Video-LLMs fail catastrophically, while a frame-level retrieval baseline outperforms them; a failure analysis reveals 85% of errors stem from search limitations, and a hybrid retrieve-then-ground approach achieves 6.7x better performance than monolithic Video-LLMs.
针对小时级视频中自然语言时间定位研究不足的问题，本文提出搜索是核心瓶颈而非识别。作者构建了首个开放小时级基准ExtremeWhenBench，包含194个视频上的2273个查询，平均时长75.7分钟。实验表明，所有Video-LLM均表现崩溃，而帧级检索基线表现更优；失败分析显示85%错误源于搜索能力不足，混合检索-定位方法性能提升6.7倍。
Learning What to Say to Your VLA: Mostly Harmless Vision Language Action Model Steering
Authors: Hyun Joe Jeong, Gokul Swamy, Andrea Bajcsy
First: 2026-06-10T16:34:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:34:49+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 14 tables, 14 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a natural language interface to robot control, but the mapping from language to behavior is often brittle and unintuitive: semantically similar instructions can induce drastically different behaviors, while some capabilities may not be elicitable through prompting alone. As a result, both human instructions and zero-shot language models can fail to reliably steer VLAs toward successful task execution. In this work, we propose a framework that interactively searches for language sequences that improve closed-loop VLA task performance, distills these sequences into a test-time language feedback policy (LFP), and learns an improvement head that predicts when language steering will improve performance. We conformalize this improvement head to prevent harmful steering interventions, where the LFP decreases task performance relative to the original instruction on out-of-distribution scenarios. Crucially, our approach operates on arbitrary frozen pre-trained VLAs, requiring neither access to the original training distribution nor fine-tuning of the underlying model. On seen environments, our conformalized LFP improves base VLA performance by 24.7% in simulation and 65.0% in hardware. On visual and semantic perturbations, our conformalized LFP has strong harmlessness guarantees, and produces recovery behaviors not observed with open-loop prompting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习如何对视觉-语言-动作模型说话：主要无害的视觉语言动作模型引导
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型为机器人控制提供了自然语言接口，但语言到行为的映射往往脆弱且不直观：语义相似的指令可能引发截然不同的行为，而某些能力可能无法仅通过提示即可激发。因此，人类指令和零样本语言模型都难以可靠地引导VLA完成任务。本文提出一种框架，交互式搜索能提升闭环VLA任务表现的语言序列，将其蒸馏为测试时语言反馈策略（LFP），并学习一个预测语言引导何时能提升性能的改进头。我们对改进头进行一致性校准，以防止有害引导干预——即在分布外场景中，LFP相比原始指令反而降低任务表现。关键的是，我们的方法适用于任意冻结的预训练VLA，无需访问原始训练分布或微调底层模型。在已知环境中，我们的校准LFP在仿真中提升基础VLA性能24.7%，在硬件上提升65.0%。在视觉和语义扰动下，我们的校准LFP具有强无害性保障，并产生未见于开环提示的恢复行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness and unintuitive behavior of Vision-Language-Action models when responding to natural language instructions, this work introduces an interactive framework that searches for optimal language sequences to steer VLA performance, distills them into a test-time language feedback policy, and trains an improvement head to predict when such steering helps. The improvement head is conformalized to avoid harmful interventions, especially in out-of-distribution scenarios. Experiments show the method improves base VLA performance by 24.7% in simulation and 65.0% in hardware, while maintaining harmlessness under visual and semantic perturbations and generating recovery behaviors not achievable via open-loop prompting.
该论文针对视觉-语言-动作模型在自然语言控制下行为脆弱、语义相似指令导致动作差异大的问题，提出一种交互式搜索框架，自动发现能提升闭环性能的语言序列，将其蒸馏为测试时语言反馈策略（LFP），并训练一个预测语言引导是否有效的改进头。该改进头经过一致性校准，防止在分布外场景下因引导导致性能下降。在无需微调的冻结预训练模型上测试，该方法在仿真中提升24.7%、硬件中提升65.0%的性能，且在视觉与语义扰动下保持无害性，生成开放循环提示无法实现的恢复行为。
Findings of the MAGMaR 2026 Shared Task
Authors: Alexander Martin, Dengjia Zhang, Joel Brogan, Francis Ferraro, Jeremy Gwinnup, Reno Kriz, Teng Long, Kenton Murray, Andrew Yates, Xiang Xiang
First: 2026-06-10T16:33:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:33:01+00:00
Comments: Findings of the 2nd workshop on Multimodal Augmented Generation via Multimodal Retrieval (MAGMaR); Resources at this url: https://github.com/rekriz11/MAGMAR_2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This overview paper presents the results of the shared task for the second workshop on Multimodal Augmented Generation via Multimodal Retrieval (MAGMaR). In this shared task participants submitted systems focused on either (i) video retrieval or (ii) grounded generation of articles given retrieved videos. Teams could submit to either task. For the retrieval task, we had 2 participating teams that submitted a total of 17 systems -- all of which beat a baseline derived from the winner of last year's shared task. On the generation side, we had 4 teams submit 16 systems. All teams had at least one generated report that was labeled the best by a human annotator.
中文标题/摘要

Summary / 总结
Motivated by advancing multimodal retrieval and grounded generation, the MAGMaR 2026 shared task invited participants to develop systems for either video retrieval or article generation based on retrieved videos. Four teams submitted 16 generation systems and two teams submitted 17 retrieval systems, all surpassing last year’s baseline. All generation systems produced at least one report judged best by human annotators, demonstrating strong performance across diverse approaches.
