Bridging the Modality Gap in Forensic Image Retrieval
Authors: Ricardo González-Gazapo, Annette Morales-González, Yoanna Martínez-Díaz, Heydi Méndez-Vázquez, Milton García-Borroto
First: 2026-06-10T16:32:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:32:30+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 5 figures, paper submitted to Elsevier journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated image retrieval plays an increasingly critical role in modern forensic analysis, supporting investigative workflows that rely on efficient comparison of visual evidence. While prior work has focused primarily on developing and optimizing multimodal retrieval systems, limited attention has been paid to evaluating the forensic applicability of these technologies across diverse real-world scenarios. In this study, we present a unified retrieval framework adapted to four key forensic tasks: (1) tattoo image retrieval given a tattoo query image; (2) tattoo retrieval guided by human-expert textual descriptions, modelling the common situation where a witness verbally describes a tattoo; (3) tattoo retrieval from hand-drawn sketches; and (4) face retrieval from forensic face sketches. Our system leverages a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to automatically generate structured textual descriptions for all queries and gallery images, followed by sentence-transformer embedding for text-based comparison. We evaluate retrieval using visual-only embeddings, text-only embeddings and a multimodal fusion strategy that combines text- and image-based similarity scores derived from state-of-the-art visual feature extractors relevant to each task. The fusion of modalities consistently improves retrieval precision and robustness, especially in scenarios where visual information is limited or noisy (e.g., sketches, partial tattoos, or fragmented witness statements). This work highlights the forensic value of a unified multimodal retrieval pipeline and demonstrates how modern MLLMs can operationalize challenging forensic tasks that traditionally rely on manual expert analysis. Our results position multimodal retrieval as a promising tool for supporting investigative workflows involving tattoos, facial composites, and witness descriptions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：弥合法医图像检索中的模态鸿沟
自动图像检索在现代法医分析中扮演着日益关键的角色，支持依赖视觉证据高效比对的调查工作流程。尽管先前研究主要聚焦于多模态检索系统的开发与优化，但对这些技术在多样化真实场景中的法医适用性评估关注有限。本研究提出一个统一的检索框架，适配四大关键法医任务：(1) 给定纹身查询图像进行纹身图像检索；(2) 根据人类专家文本描述进行纹身检索，模拟目击者口头描述纹身的常见情况；(3) 从手绘草图中检索纹身；(4) 从法医面部素描中检索人脸。系统利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）自动生成所有查询与图库图像的结构化文本描述，随后采用sentence-transformer进行文本比对。我们通过视觉特征嵌入、文本特征嵌入及融合策略评估检索性能，其中融合策略结合了来自各任务相关前沿视觉特征提取器的文本与图像相似度得分。模态融合显著提升检索精度与鲁棒性，尤其在视觉信息有限或嘈杂的场景（如素描、部分纹身或碎片化目击陈述）中表现突出。本研究凸显了统一多模态检索流程的法医价值，并展示现代MLLM如何将传统依赖人工专家分析的复杂法医任务实现自动化。结果表明，多模态检索有望成为支持纹身、面部合成及目击描述相关调查工作流程的有力工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance forensic image retrieval across diverse real-world scenarios, this study introduces a unified multimodal framework designed for four key forensic tasks: tattoo retrieval from images, text descriptions, and sketches, as well as face retrieval from forensic sketches. The method employs a multimodal large language model to generate structured textual descriptions for both queries and gallery images, followed by sentence-transformer embeddings for comparison, while also integrating visual-only and multimodal fusion strategies using state-of-the-art visual features. Experimental results demonstrate that multimodal fusion consistently outperforms single-modality approaches, especially in low-fidelity or noisy inputs like sketches or fragmented witness statements, thereby validating the framework’s robustness and forensic utility.
为提升跨真实场景的法医图像检索能力，本研究提出统一的多模态框架，解决四大关键法医任务：从图像、文本描述及手绘草图检索纹身，以及从法医素描检索人脸。方法利用多模态大语言模型自动生成查询与图库图像的结构化文本描述，结合句子变换器嵌入进行比较，并融合视觉嵌入与文本-图像相似度得分。实验表明，多模态融合策略在图像质量低（如素描、碎片化证词）时显著提升检索精度与鲁棒性，凸显多模态检索在法医工作中的实用价值。
When Does Deep RL Beat Calibrated Baselines? A Benchmark Study on Adaptive Resource Control
Authors: Guilin Zhang, Chuanyi Sun, Kai Zhao, Shahryar Sarkani, John Fossaceca
First: 2026-05-26T01:07:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:29:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A properly calibrated rule-based autoscaler can beat every one of six mainstream deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms on cost across every workload we test - so when, if ever, does DRL actually help? We study this in RLScale-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evaluation protocol for DRL on adaptive resource control, where an agent allocates compute to a dynamic workload under cost and service-level constraints. We evaluate PPO, DQN, A2C, SAC, TD3, and DDPG under matched architectures, training budgets, and reward functions against a calibrated rule-based baseline across six workload patterns and five seeds (240 runs), instantiate the benchmark on Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaling, and probe distribution-shift generalization. Three findings challenge common assumptions: (i) the calibrated controller achieves the lowest cost on all six workloads, though it trails the best RL agents on bursty and flash traffic; (ii) discrete-action algorithms outperform continuous-action ones by one to two orders of magnitude in constraint violations due to action-space mismatch; and (iii) no single algorithm dominates across workloads, with rankings shifting by up to four positions. The bottleneck in RL-based resource control is not algorithm selection but baseline calibration, reward engineering, and realistic evaluation protocols.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度强化学习何时能超越校准基线？——一项关于自适应资源控制的基准研究
在我们测试的所有工作负载中，一个经过适当校准的基于规则的自动扩缩器能在成本上击败六种主流深度强化学习（DRL）算法——那么，DRL究竟在何时、何地真正有用？我们在RLScale-Bench中对此进行了研究，这是一个针对DRL在自适应资源控制上的可复现基准与评估协议，其中智能体需在成本和服务等级约束下为动态工作负载分配计算资源。我们在匹配架构、训练预算和奖励函数的前提下，评估了PPO、DQN、A2C、SAC、TD3和DDPG六种算法，与校准的规则基线在六种工作负载模式和五个种子（共240次运行）上进行对比，并在Kubernetes水平Pod自动扩缩中实例化该基准，同时探究分布偏移下的泛化能力。三个发现挑战了常见假设：(i) 校准控制器在所有六种工作负载中成本最低，尽管在突发和闪现流量下落后于表现最佳的RL智能体；(ii) 离散动作算法因动作空间不匹配，其约束违反程度比连续动作算法高出一个至两个数量级；(iii) 无单一算法在所有工作负载中占优，排名最多可变动四名。基于RL的资源控制瓶颈并非算法选择，而是基线校准、奖励工程和现实评估协议。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates when deep reinforcement learning (DRL) outperforms calibrated rule-based autoscalers in adaptive resource control, motivated by the observation that rule-based systems consistently beat six mainstream DRL algorithms on cost across tested workloads. Using RLScale-Bench, a reproducible benchmark with matched architectures, training budgets, and reward functions, the authors evaluate PPO, DQN, A2C, SAC, TD3, and DDPG against a calibrated baseline across six workload patterns and five seeds (240 runs), implemented on Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaling. Key findings reveal that the calibrated baseline achieves the lowest cost on all workloads, though it lags behind top RL agents on bursty and flash traffic; discrete-action algorithms outperform continuous-action ones by orders of magnitude in constraint violations due to action-space mismatch; and no single DRL algorithm dominates across workloads, with rankings shifting by up to four positions — indicating that the core bottleneck lies not in algorithm choice but in baseline calibration, reward design, and realistic evaluation protocols.
该研究旨在探究深度强化学习（DRL）在自适应资源控制中何时能超越经过校准的规则基自动伸缩器，因为观察到规则基系统在所有测试工作负载上均击败六种主流DRL算法。研究通过RLScale-Bench可复现基准，对PPO、DQN、A2C、SAC、TD3和DDPG在匹配架构、训练预算和奖励函数下，与校准基线在六种工作负载模式和五个种子（共240次运行）中进行评估，并在Kubernetes HPA上实现。主要发现表明：校准基线在所有工作负载上成本最低，但在突发和闪现流量下落后于顶尖RL代理；离散动作算法因动作空间不匹配，比连续动作算法在约束违反上高出一个至两个数量级；且无单一算法在所有工作负载中占优，排名最多变动四位——说明核心瓶颈并非算法选择，而是基线校准、奖励工程与现实评估协议的设计。
Inside the Latent Flow: Causal Deciphering of Attention Dynamics in Audio Separation Foundation Models
Authors: Yuxuan Chen, Haoyuan Yu, Peize He
First: 2026-06-08T18:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:28:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Flow-matching transformers achieve strong audio separation, yet their attention dynamics are opaque. We adapt established causal-intervention principles into a deterministic, inference-time probing protocol for SAM Audio. Orthogonal probing uncovers a dual-pathway text-conditioning mechanism: additive injections control semantic identity, while cross-attention refines acoustic structure. We observe an asynchronous layerwise convergence: stable layers build temporal scaffolds early, whereas fast layers continue resolving artifacts during sampling. The model also attenuates temporal segmentation cues to maintain continuous-flow stability. Using these insights, we propose Layer-Selective Attention Caching (LSAC), a training-free acceleration method that caches attention in stable layers. Across acoustic complexities, LSAC cuts self-attention computation by about ~25% with negligible quality loss and yields up to 6.7x higher quality retention than naive step reduction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜流内部探秘：音频分离基础模型中注意力动态的因果解析
流匹配变压器在音频分离中表现优异，但其注意力动态机制不透明。我们将其成熟的因果干预原则适配为SAM音频模型的确定性推理时探针协议。正交探针揭示了双路径文本条件化机制：加性注入控制语义身份，而交叉注意力优化声学结构。我们观察到异步层间收敛现象：稳定层早期构建时序骨架，而快速层在采样过程中持续消除伪影。模型同时抑制时序分段线索以维持连续流稳定性。基于这些发现，我们提出层选择性注意力缓存（LSAC），一种无需训练的加速方法，缓存稳定层的注意力。在各类声学复杂度下，LSAC可减少约25%的自注意力计算，且质量损失微乎其微，相比简单步长缩减，质量保留提升高达6.7倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the opacity of attention dynamics in flow-matching transformers for audio separation, the authors introduce a causal-intervention probing protocol to dissect SAM Audio’s internal mechanisms. Their method reveals two distinct text-conditioning pathways: additive injections govern semantic identity while cross-attention refines acoustic structure. Experimentally, they find asynchronous layerwise convergence—stable layers form early temporal scaffolds while fast layers resolve artifacts during sampling, and the model suppresses temporal segmentation cues to preserve flow continuity. Leveraging these insights, they propose Layer-Selective Attention Caching (LSAC), a training-free technique that caches attention in stable layers, reducing self-attention computation by ~25% with minimal quality loss and achieving up to 6.7x higher quality retention compared to naive step reduction.
为理解流匹配Transformer在音频分离中的不透明注意力动态，作者将因果干预原则应用于SAM Audio，构建确定性探针协议，揭示了双路径文本条件机制：加性注入控制语义身份，交叉注意力优化声学结构。实验发现层间收敛异步：早期稳定层构建时序骨架，快速层在采样中持续消除伪影，模型同时抑制时序分割线索以维持连续流稳定性。基于此，提出无训练的层选择注意力缓存（LSAC）方法，缓存稳定层注意力，自注意力计算减少约25%，质量损失可忽略，并较简单步长缩减方法提升6.7倍质量保留率。
Measuring Epistemic Resilience of LLMs Under Misleading Medical Context
Authors: Hongjian Zhou, Xinyu Zou, Jinge Wu, Sean Wu, Junchi Yu, Bradley Max Segal, Tobias Erich Niebuhr, Sara Amro, Michael Petrus, Sheikh Momin, Alexandra M. Cardoso Pinto, Rachel Niesen, Laura Sophie Wegner, Dhruv Darji, Jung Moses Koo, Joshua Fieggen, Kapil Narain, Mingde Zeng, Lei Clifton, Linda Shapiro, Fenglin Liu, David A. Clifton
First: 2026-06-10T16:27:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:27:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) now reach expert-level scores on medical licensing exams, encouraging the assumption that high scores imply safe medical judgment while patients increasingly use them for health advice. We show this assumption is fragile: when misleading context is injected into questions that LLMs originally answer correctly, they abandon the correct answer. We call the ability to maintain correct judgment under adversarial context epistemic resilience, and introduce MedMisBench to measure it. MedMisBench contains 10,932 medical question items and 48,889 misleading context-option pairs spanning medical reasoning, agentic capability, and patient-journey evaluation. Across 11 model configurations, mean accuracy falls from 71.1% on original questions to 38.0% under focused misleading context, with 51.5% attack success. The most damaging injections are formal, rule-like fabrications: authority-framed falsehoods reach 69.5% attack success and exception-poisoning claims reach 64.1%. A 14-member clinical panel from 7 countries identified serious potential harm in 38.2% of reviewed cases. MedMisBench exposes a structural blind spot in LLM evaluation in medical settings: existing benchmarks measure what models know, but not whether they preserve correct medical judgment under misleading context.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在误导性医学背景下衡量大语言模型的知识韧性
大语言模型（LLMs）如今在医学执照考试中达到专家级分数，促使人们认为高分意味着安全的医学判断，而患者越来越多地依赖它们获取健康建议。我们表明这一假设脆弱：当向原本能正确回答的问题注入误导性上下文时，模型会放弃正确答案。我们将模型在对抗性上下文中保持正确判断的能力称为知识韧性，并引入MedMisBench来衡量它。MedMisBench包含10,932个医学问题项和48,889个误导性上下文-选项对，涵盖医学推理、自主能力及患者诊疗路径评估。在11种模型配置下，原始问题平均准确率从71.1%降至在聚焦误导性上下文下的38.0%，攻击成功率51.5%。最具破坏性的注入是形式化、规则式的虚构内容：以权威框架呈现的虚假陈述攻击成功率高达69.5%，例外污染型陈述达64.1%。由7国14名临床专家组成的评审团指出，38.2%的案例存在严重潜在危害。MedMisBench揭示了当前LLM医学评估中的结构性盲点：现有基准衡量的是模型所知，而非其在误导性上下文中是否能保持正确的医学判断。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the concern that high medical exam scores in LLMs may not ensure safe clinical judgment under real-world misleading contexts, this study introduces epistemic resilience as the ability to maintain correct answers despite adversarial context. Using MedMisBench—a benchmark of 10,932 medical questions and 48,889 misleading context-option pairs—the researchers tested 11 LLM configurations and found their accuracy dropped from 71.1% to 38.0% under focused misleading context, with 51.5% attack success. Formal, rule-like falsehoods were especially damaging, achieving 69.5% and 64.1% attack success respectively, and a clinical panel identified potential harm in 38.2% of reviewed cases, revealing a critical gap in current LLM evaluation.
鉴于大语言模型在医学考试中取得高分并不意味着其临床判断安全，本研究提出‘认识论韧性’概念，即模型在面对误导性上下文时仍能保持正确判断的能力，并通过MedMisBench基准（含10,932道医学题及48,889组误导上下文选项）进行评估。11种模型配置下，准确率从71.1%骤降至38.0%，攻击成功率高达51.5%；其中以权威框架或例外欺骗形式的误导最有效，成功率分别达69.5%和64.1%；临床专家组评估发现38.2%案例存在严重潜在危害，揭示当前评估体系忽视模型在误导情境下的判断稳定性。
The Standard Interpretable Model: A general theory of interpretable machine learning to deductively design interpretable methods using Lagrangian mechanics
Authors: Pietro Barbiero, Giovanni De Felice, Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga, Francesco Giannini, Filippo Bonchi, Mateja Jamnik, Giuseppe Marra, Ruggero Noris
First: 2026-06-10T16:26:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:26:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Artificial Intelligence models grow in complexity, interpretability has become an indispensable tool for understanding, debugging, and controlling their computations. However, interpretability lacks general theories to deductively design interpretable methods. This gap between theories and methods results in a fragmented literature and inconsistent evaluation protocols. To fill this gap, we introduce the Standard Interpretable Model (SIM), a general theory grounded in Lagrangian mechanics that enables the deductive design of interpretable methods. Specifically, the SIM summarises, in a set of premises, what interpretability is for a target user. From these premises, the SIM systematically derives interpretability symmetries and corresponding constraints, which shape the landscape of a Lagrangian whose minima correspond to optimal interpretable models. To reach the minima, one can either update the parameter values of an opaque model to make it more interpretable or compile constraints into an interpretable architecture. We empirically show that the SIM identifies and solves limitations of existing methods (including traditional, concept-based, and mechanistic interpretability), highlights underexplored research directions, and informs the design of core programming interfaces. Beyond being a research method, the deductive nature of the SIM offers pedagogical grounding for interpretability curricula and may shift the scientific community's perspective of a discipline that has long been fragmented.
中文标题/摘要
标题：标准可解释模型：一种基于拉格朗日力学的可解释机器学习通用理论，用于演绎式设计可解释方法
随着人工智能模型复杂度的增加，可解释性已成为理解、调试和控制其计算过程不可或缺的工具。然而，可解释性缺乏能够演绎式设计可解释方法的通用理论。理论与方法之间的这一差距导致文献碎片化和评估标准不一致。 为填补这一空白，我们引入了标准可解释模型（SIM），这是一种基于拉格朗日力学的通用理论，能够演绎式设计可解释方法。具体而言，SIM通过一组前提条件，概括了针对目标用户而言的可解释性内涵。从这些前提条件出发，SIM系统性地推导出可解释性对称性及其对应约束，这些约束塑造了拉格朗日量的势能场，其极小值对应最优可解释模型。为达到极小值，可选择更新不透明模型的参数以增强其可解释性，或直接将约束编译进可解释架构中。 我们通过实证表明，SIM能够识别并解决现有方法（包括传统、概念导向和机制导向的可解释性方法）的局限性，揭示尚未充分探索的研究方向，并指导核心编程接口的设计。SIM不仅是一种研究方法，其演绎性特征还为可解释性课程提供了教学基础，可能改变科学界长期以来对这一碎片化学科的认知。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for systematic, theory-based design of interpretable machine learning methods amid growing model complexity, this work introduces the Standard Interpretable Model (SIM), a general framework rooted in Lagrangian mechanics. SIM defines interpretability through user-specific premises, then derives symmetry constraints that shape a Lagrangian whose minima represent optimal interpretable models. Experimentally, SIM reveals theoretical shortcomings in existing interpretability approaches—traditional, concept-based, and mechanistic—and identifies underexplored research avenues while guiding architecture design and programming interface development. Its deductive structure also supports pedagogical applications and offers a unifying perspective for the field.
为解决现有可解释机器学习方法缺乏系统理论指导的问题，本文提出标准可解释模型（SIM），该框架基于拉格朗日力学构建。SIM通过用户特定前提定义可解释性，推导出对拉格朗日函数的约束，其最小值对应最优可解释模型。实验证明，SIM能揭示现有方法（包括传统、概念和机制可解释性方法）的局限，指出未被充分探索的研究方向，并指导可解释架构与编程接口的设计，同时为可解释性教学提供演绎性理论基础。
Bimanual Robot Manipulation via Multi-Agent In-Context Learning
Authors: Alessio Palma, Indro Spinelli, Vignesh Prasad, Luca Scofano, Yufeng Jin, Georgia Chalvatzaki, Fabio Galasso
First: 2026-04-22T08:51:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:25:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful reasoning engines for embodied control. In particular, In-Context Learning (ICL) enables off-the-shelf, text-only LLMs to predict robot actions without any task-specific training while preserving their generalization capabilities. Applying ICL to bimanual manipulation remains challenging as the high-dimensional joint action space and tight inter-arm coordination constraints rapidly overwhelm standard context windows. To address this, we introduce BiCICLe (Bimanual Coordinated In-Context Learning), the first framework that enables standard LLMs to perform few-shot bimanual manipulation without fine-tuning. BiCICLe frames bimanual control as a multi-agent leader-follower problem, decoupling the action space into sequential, conditioned single-arm predictions. Evaluated on 13 tasks from the TWIN benchmark, BiCICLe achieves 70.5% average success rate, outperforming the best training-free baseline by 6.1 percentage points and surpassing most supervised methods. We also demonstrate superior real-world performance on 3 tasks without hardware-specific retraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多智能体上下文学习实现双臂机器人操作
语言模型（LLMs）已成为具身控制的强大推理引擎。特别是，上下文学习（ICL）使开箱即用的纯文本LLMs无需特定任务训练即可预测机器人动作，同时保留其泛化能力。然而，将ICL应用于双臂操作仍具挑战性，因高维关节动作空间与双臂紧密协调约束迅速超出标准上下文窗口。为此，我们提出BiCICLe（双臂协调上下文学习），这是首个无需微调即可使标准LLMs执行少样本双臂操作的框架。BiCICLe将双臂控制建模为多智能体领导者-跟随者问题，将动作空间解耦为条件化的单臂序列预测。在TWIN基准的13项任务上，BiCICLe平均成功率70.5%，优于最佳无训练基线6.1个百分点，并超越多数监督方法。我们在3项任务上还展示了无需硬件特定重训练的优越现实性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable standard language models to perform complex bimanual robot manipulation without fine-tuning, the authors propose BiCICLe, a framework that treats bimanual control as a multi-agent leader-follower problem to decompose the high-dimensional joint action space into sequential, conditioned single-arm predictions. Evaluated on 13 tasks from the TWIN benchmark, BiCICLe achieves a 70.5% average success rate, outperforming the best training-free baseline by 6.1 percentage points and surpassing most supervised methods. The method also demonstrates strong real-world performance on three tasks without hardware-specific retraining.
为使标准语言模型无需微调即可执行复杂的双臂机器人操作，作者提出了BiCICLe框架，将双臂控制建模为多智能体领导者-跟随者问题，将高维动作空间分解为按顺序、基于上下文的单臂动作预测。在TWIN基准的13项任务上，BiCICLe平均成功率70.5%，优于最佳无训练基线6.1个百分点，超越多数监督方法，且在三项任务上无需硬件重训练即展现出优异的现实世界性能。
WeaveBench: A Long-Horizon, Real-World Benchmark for Computer-Use Agents with Hybrid Interfaces
Authors: Wanli Li, Bowen Zhou, Yunyao Yu, Zhou Xu, Yifan Yang, Dongsheng Li, Caihua Shan
First: 2026-06-08T12:39:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:25:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly operate in runtimes that combine visual desktop control, command-line execution, code editing, browsers, and external tools. Existing benchmarks, however, often evaluate these interfaces as separable capabilities, leaving long-horizon cross-interface orchestration under-tested. Thus, we introduce WeaveBench, a long-horizon hybrid-interface benchmark with 114 tasks across 8 real-world work domains, grounded in real user requests and publicly verifiable artifacts. Each task requires agents to combine GUI observations/actions with CLI/code operations within a single trajectory. We evaluate these tasks on a real Ubuntu desktop inside deployed CLI-agent runtimes, augmented with a minimal desktop-control plugin. We also propose a companion trajectory-aware judge that inspects deliverables, files, screenshots, logs, and action traces, while detecting shortcut behaviors such as fabricated visual evidence or hard-coded metrics. Across frontier model-runtime pairings, the best PassRate reaches only 41.2%, showing the benchmark remains far from saturated. The trajectory-aware judge further reveals that outcome-only grading substantially overestimates agent performance. Overall, WeaveBench exposes a critical gap in CUA evaluation and provides an effective testbed to measure whether agents can orchestrate GUI, CLI, and code operations across long-horizon real-world tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WeaveBench：面向混合界面计算机使用代理的长时程真实世界基准
计算机使用代理（CUAs）越来越多地在结合视觉桌面控制、命令行执行、代码编辑、浏览器和外部工具的运行环境中操作。然而，现有基准通常将这些界面视为可分离的能力，导致长时程跨界面协同操作测试不足。为此，我们提出了WeaveBench，一个包含8个真实世界工作领域、114项任务的长时程混合界面基准，基于真实用户请求和公开可验证的成果。每个任务要求代理在单个轨迹中结合GUI观察/操作与CLI/代码操作。我们在部署的CLI代理运行环境中，于真实Ubuntu桌面进行评估，并辅以最小桌面控制插件。我们还提出了一种轨迹感知裁判，可检查交付成果、文件、截图、日志和操作轨迹，同时检测如伪造视觉证据或硬编码指标等捷径行为。在前沿模型-运行时配对中，最佳通过率仅为41.2%，表明该基准尚未饱和。轨迹感知裁判进一步揭示，仅凭结果评分严重高估了代理性能。总体而言，WeaveBench揭示了CUA评估中的关键差距，并提供了一个有效测试平台，用于衡量代理能否在长时程真实任务中协调GUI、CLI和代码操作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate computer-use agents in real-world, long-horizon scenarios involving hybrid interfaces, WeaveBench introduces a benchmark with 114 tasks across eight domains, requiring agents to integrate GUI actions, CLI commands, and code editing within single trajectories. Evaluated on a real Ubuntu desktop with a minimal desktop-control plugin, the benchmark uses a trajectory-aware judge that verifies deliverables, logs, screenshots, and action traces while detecting shortcut behaviors. Experimental results show that even top model-runtime pairs achieve only a 41.2% PassRate, and outcome-only grading significantly overestimates agent performance, highlighting a critical gap in current CUA evaluation.
为评估计算机使用代理在真实世界长时程混合界面场景中的能力，WeaveBench 设计了包含八个领域共114个任务的基准，要求代理在单个轨迹中整合GUI操作、CLI命令和代码编辑。在真实Ubuntu桌面环境下，通过最小桌面控制插件进行评估，并引入轨迹感知裁判系统，检查交付物、日志、截图和操作轨迹以识别作弊行为。实验表明，顶级模型-运行时组合仅达到41.2%的通过率，且仅凭结果评分严重高估代理表现，揭示当前计算机使用代理评估存在关键缺陷。
SpikeDecoder: Realizing the GPT Architecture with Spiking Neural Networks
Authors: Claas Beger, Florian Walter, Alois Knoll
First: 2026-06-10T16:23:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:23:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Transformer architecture is widely regarded as the most powerful tool for natural language processing, but due to a high number of complex operations, it inherently faces the issue of high energy consumption. To address this issue, we consider Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs), which are an energy-efficient alternative to conventional Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs) due to their naturally event-driven approach to processing information. However, this inherently makes them difficult to train. Often, many SNN-based models circumvent this issue by converting pre-trained ANNs. More recently, attempts have been made to design directly trainable SNN-based adaptations of the Transformer model structure. Although the results showed great promise, the application field was computer vision. Moreover, the proposed model incorporates only encoder blocks. In this paper, we propose SpikeDecoder, a fully SNN-based implementation of the Transformer decoder block, for applications in natural language processing. In a series of experiments, we analyze the impact of exchanging different blocks of the ANN model with spike-based alternatives to identify trade-offs and significant sources of performance loss. We further investigate the role of residual connections and the selection of SNN-compatible normalization techniques. Besides the work on the model architecture, we formulate and compare different embedding methods to project text data into spikes. Finally, we demonstrate that our proposed SNN-based decoder block reduces the theoretical energy consumption by 87% to 93% compared to the ANN baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpikeDecoder：使用脉冲神经网络实现GPT架构
Transformer架构被广泛认为是自然语言处理最强大的工具，但由于其包含大量复杂运算，固有地面临高能耗问题。为解决此问题，我们考虑脉冲神经网络（SNNs），因其事件驱动的天然处理方式，相比传统人工神经网络（ANNs）更具能效优势。然而，这使其训练难度增加。通常，许多基于SNN的模型通过转换预训练的ANN来规避此问题。近期，人们尝试直接设计可训练的SNN版本的Transformer模型结构。尽管结果极具前景，但其应用领域限于计算机视觉，且所提模型仅包含编码器块。本文提出SpikeDecoder，即完全基于SNN的Transformer解码器块实现，适用于自然语言处理。在一系列实验中，我们分析将ANN模型不同模块替换为脉冲替代方案的影响，以识别权衡及性能损失的主要来源。我们进一步研究残差连接的作用及SNN兼容归一化技术的选择。除模型架构工作外，我们还制定并比较了不同嵌入方法，以将文本数据投影为脉冲。最后，我们证明所提出的SNN解码器块相比ANN基线，理论能耗降低87%至93%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high energy cost of Transformer models in NLP, this work proposes SpikeDecoder, a fully spiking neural network (SNN) implementation of the Transformer decoder block to enable energy-efficient language processing. The method directly trains SNNs without relying on pre-trained ANN conversions, incorporating SNN-compatible normalization and residual connections, and experimenting with various spike-based embedding techniques. Experimental results show that SpikeDecoder reduces theoretical energy consumption by 87% to 93% compared to its ANN counterpart while maintaining functional viability in NLP tasks.
为降低Transformer模型在自然语言处理中的高能耗，本文提出SpikeDecoder，即完全基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的Transformer解码器模块。该方法直接训练SNN组件，不依赖预训练ANN转换，结合SNN兼容的归一化与残差连接，并测试多种脉冲嵌入策略。实验表明，SpikeDecoder相比传统ANN解码器在理论能耗上降低87%至93%，同时保持NLP任务的功能可行性。
Embodied Interpretability: Linking Causal Understanding to Generalization in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Hanxin Zhang, Mingshuo Xu, Abdulqader Dhafer, Shigang Yue, Hongbiao Dong, Zhou Daniel Hao
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-01T01:00:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:22:35+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies often fail under distribution shift, suggesting that decisions may depend on spurious visual correlations rather than task-relevant causes. We formulate visual-action attribution as an interventional estimation problem. Accordingly, we introduce the Interventional Significance Score (ISS), an interventional masking procedure for estimating the causal influence of visual regions on action predictions, and the Nuisance Mass Ratio (NMR), a scalar measure of attribution to task-irrelevant features. We analyze the statistical properties of ISS and show that it admits unbiased estimation, and we characterize conditions under which action prediction error provides a valid proxy for causal influence. Experiments across diverse manipulation tasks indicate that NMR predicts generalization behavior and that ISS yields more faithful explanations than existing interpretability methods. These results suggest that interventional attribution provides a simple diagnostic approach for identifying causal misalignment in embodied policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具身可解释性：将因果理解与视觉-语言-动作模型中的泛化能力相联系
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略常在分布偏移下失效，表明决策可能依赖于虚假的视觉相关性而非任务相关的因果因素。我们将视觉-动作归因形式化为干预估计问题，进而提出干预显著性得分（ISS），一种用于估计视觉区域对动作预测因果影响的干预掩码方法，以及干扰质量比（NMR），一种衡量对任务无关特征归因程度的标量指标。我们分析了ISS的统计性质，证明其可无偏估计，并刻画了在何种条件下动作预测误差可作为因果影响的有效代理。在多种操作任务上的实验表明，NMR可预测泛化行为，ISS比现有可解释性方法提供更忠实的解释。这些结果表明，干预归因提供了一种简单诊断方法，用于识别具身策略中的因果错位。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of Vision-Language-Action models under distribution shift, this work frames visual-action attribution as an interventional estimation problem to identify causal influences rather than spurious correlations. It introduces the Interventional Significance Score (ISS), a masking-based method to estimate causal impact of visual regions on actions, and the Nuisance Mass Ratio (NMR), a scalar metric quantifying attribution to irrelevant features. Experiments across manipulation tasks show that NMR correlates with generalization performance and that ISS provides more accurate, causal explanations than existing interpretability methods, suggesting interventional attribution as a diagnostic tool for causal misalignment.
鉴于视觉-语言-动作模型在分布偏移下常因依赖虚假视觉相关性而失效，作者将视觉-动作归因建模为干预估计问题，提出干预显著性得分（ISS）用于评估视觉区域对动作预测的因果影响，以及干扰质量比（NMR）用于量化对任务无关特征的归因程度。理论分析证明ISS无偏，且在特定条件下预测误差可作为因果影响代理。跨多种操作任务的实验表明，NMR能预测泛化表现，ISS比现有方法提供更可信的解释，表明干预式归因可作为识别具身策略因果错配的简单诊断工具。
CellNet -- Localizing Cells using Sparse and Noisy Point Annotations
Authors: Benjamin Eckhardt, Dmytro Fishman, Stuart Fawke, Andrew Curtis, Bo Fussing, Constantin Pape
First: 2026-06-10T16:22:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:22:32+00:00
Comments: Conference poster at Biology at Scale: From Variants to Cellular Programs and Functions
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Counting living cells is an important step in many biological research workflows. Our collaborators at the Wellcome Sanger Institute study vital genes in humans via large scale saturation genome editing screening, which requires repeatedly counting cells a great number of times. Computer Vision based automation is crucial for high throughput and resource efficiency. In this work, we develop a regression-based deep learning computer vision algorithm to detect and count cells in phase-contrast microscopy images. To reduce annotation effort, which in practice often becomes a bottleneck, we focus on counting cells only using sparse point annotations, which are fast and easy to acquire. By comparison to state-of-the-art 0-shot methods, we show that regression-based counting is a promising alternative in low data regimes. Through developing methods to automatically count living cells in microscopy images, we contribute to valuable research on the human genome. The code is available at https://github.com/beijn/cellnet.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CellNet——利用稀疏且含噪的点标注定位细胞
计数活细胞是许多生物研究工作流程中的重要步骤。我们的合作者在Wellcome Sanger研究所通过大规模饱和基因组编辑筛选研究人类关键基因，需要反复多次计数大量细胞。基于计算机视觉的自动化对高通量和资源效率至关重要。本研究开发了一种基于回归的深度学习计算机视觉算法，用于在相差显微镜图像中检测和计数细胞。为减少标注工作量（实践中常成为瓶颈），我们专注于仅使用稀疏点标注进行细胞计数，此类标注获取快速简便。与当前最先进的零样本方法相比，我们证明回归式计数在数据量少的情况下是一种有前景的替代方案。通过开发自动计数显微镜图像中活细胞的方法，我们为人类基因组研究做出了贡献。代码见https://github.com/beijn/cellnet。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient cell counting in high-throughput genome editing screens, this work introduces CellNet, a regression-based deep learning method that counts cells in phase-contrast microscopy images using sparse and noisy point annotations. By focusing on minimal annotation effort, the approach addresses practical bottlenecks in biological workflows. Experimental results demonstrate that CellNet outperforms state-of-the-art 0-shot methods in low-data regimes, validating regression-based counting as a viable alternative for automated cell quantification.
为满足高通量基因编辑研究中对细胞计数的高效需求，该研究提出一种基于回归的深度学习方法，利用稀疏且噪声较多的点标注在相差显微图像中检测和计数细胞。该方法通过减少人工标注负担，解决了生物成像中常见的标注瓶颈问题。实验结果表明，该回归方法在数据量有限的情况下优于当前最先进的零样本方法，为生物研究中的自动化细胞计数提供了一种实用且准确的替代方案。
Neuron-based Personality Trait Induction in Large Language Models
Authors: Jia Deng, Tianyi Tang, Yanbin Yin, Wenhao Yang, Wayne Xin Zhao, Ji-Rong Wen
Venue: ICLR 2025
First: 2024-10-16T07:47:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:21:22+00:00
Comments: 25 pages. Published at ICLR 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have become increasingly proficient at simulating various personality traits, an important capability for supporting related applications (e.g., role-playing). To further improve this capacity, in this paper, we present a neuron-based approach for personality trait induction in LLMs, with three major technical contributions. First, we construct PersonalityBench, a large-scale dataset for identifying and evaluating personality traits in LLMs. This dataset is grounded in the Big Five personality traits from psychology and is designed to assess the generative capabilities of LLMs towards specific personality traits. Second, by leveraging PersonalityBench, we propose an efficient method for identifying personality-related neurons within LLMs by examining the opposite aspects of a given trait. Third, we develop a simple yet effective induction method that manipulates the values of these identified personality-related neurons. This method enables fine-grained control over the traits exhibited by LLMs without training and modifying model parameters. Extensive experiments validate the efficacy of our neuron identification and trait induction methods. Notably, our approach achieves comparable performance as fine-tuned models, offering a more efficient and flexible solution for personality trait induction in LLMs. We provide access to all the mentioned resources at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/NPTI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于神经元的大语言模型人格特质诱导
大语言模型（LLMs）在模拟各种人格特质方面日益熟练，这对于支持相关应用（如角色扮演）至关重要。为进一步提升这一能力，本文提出一种基于神经元的人格特质诱导方法，具有三大技术贡献：首先，构建了PersonalityBench数据集，该数据集基于心理学中的五大人格特质，旨在评估LLMs针对特定人格特质的生成能力；其次，利用PersonalityBench提出一种高效方法，通过考察给定特质的对立面来识别LLMs中与人格相关联的神经元；第三，开发了一种简单而有效的诱导方法，通过调整所识别的人格相关神经元的值来实现对LLMs所表现特质的细粒度控制，且无需训练或修改模型参数。大量实验验证了我们神经元识别与特质诱导方法的有效性。值得注意的是，我们的方法性能可媲美微调模型，提供了一种更高效灵活的人格特质诱导方案。所有相关资源可在https://github.com/RUCAIBox/NPTI获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance LLMs' ability to simulate specific personality traits for applications like role-playing, this paper introduces a neuron-based method for trait induction. It first builds PersonalityBench, a large-scale dataset aligned with the Big Five traits to evaluate LLMs’ generative capacity. Then, it identifies personality-related neurons by analyzing opposing trait dimensions and induces desired traits by manipulating these neurons without retraining. Experiments show the method achieves performance comparable to fine-tuned models, offering a lightweight, parameter-free approach for precise personality control.
为提升大语言模型在角色扮演等场景中模拟特定人格特质的能力，本文提出一种基于神经元的人格特质诱导方法。首先构建了基于心理学五大人格特质的PersonalityBench大规模评测数据集，用于评估模型生成能力；随后通过分析人格特质的对立面识别相关神经元，并通过调节这些神经元的激活值实现无参数微调的人格诱导。实验表明，该方法性能可媲美微调模型，提供了一种轻量、灵活且无需参数修改的精准人格控制方案。
AsFT: Anchoring Safety During LLM Fine-Tuning Within Narrow Safety Basin
Authors: Shuo Yang, Qihui Zhang, Yuyang Liu, Xiaojun Jia, Kunpeng Ning, Jiayu Yao, Jigang Wang, Hailiang Dai, Yibing Song, Li Yuan
First: 2025-06-10T05:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:20:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) improves performance but introduces critical safety vulnerabilities: even minimal harmful data can severely compromise safety measures. We observe that perturbations orthogonal to the alignment direction - defined by weight differences between aligned (safe) and unaligned models - rapidly compromise model safety. In contrast, updates along the alignment direction largely preserve it, revealing the parameter space as a "narrow safety basin". To address this, we propose AsFT (Anchoring Safety in Fine-Tuning) to maintain safety by explicitly constraining update directions during fine-tuning. By penalizing updates orthogonal to the alignment direction, AsFT effectively constrains the model within the "narrow safety basin," thus preserving its inherent safety. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets and models show that AsFT reduces harmful behaviors by up to 7.60%, improves task performance by 3.44%, and consistently outperforms existing methods across multiple tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AsFT：在狭窄安全区域内锚定大语言模型微调期间的安全性
微调大语言模型（LLM）可提升性能，但会引入关键安全漏洞：即使少量有害数据也可能严重破坏安全措施。我们观察到，与对齐方向正交的扰动——即对齐（安全）模型与未对齐模型之间的权重差异——会迅速破坏模型安全性；而沿对齐方向的更新则基本保持安全性，揭示参数空间为“狭窄安全区域”。为此，我们提出AsFT（微调中锚定安全性），通过在微调过程中显式约束更新方向以维持安全性。通过惩罚与对齐方向正交的更新，AsFT有效将模型约束在“狭窄安全区域”内，从而保留其固有安全性。在多个数据集和模型上的大量实验表明，AsFT可将有害行为降低最多7.60%，任务性能提升3.44%，并在多个任务中持续优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that fine-tuning LLMs can introduce safety vulnerabilities even from minimal harmful data, the authors propose AsFT, a method that anchors safety by constraining fine-tuning updates to stay aligned with the safety direction defined by weight differences between safe and unsafe models. By penalizing deviations orthogonal to this alignment direction, AsFT effectively confines model updates within a narrow safety basin. Experimental results across multiple datasets and models show that AsFT reduces harmful behaviors by up to 7.60%, improves task performance by 3.44%, and consistently outperforms existing methods.
为解决微调大语言模型时因正交参数更新导致的安全风险，作者提出AsFT方法，通过约束更新方向始终沿对齐向量（安全模型与非安全模型权重差）来锚定安全。该方法通过惩罚正交方向的更新，使模型更新被限制在狭窄的安全盆地内，从而保持安全同时维持性能。在多个数据集和模型上的实验表明，AsFT可将有害行为降低最多7.60%，任务性能提升3.44%，并在多个任务上持续优于现有方法。
PianoKontext: Expressive Performance Rendering from Deadpan Context
Authors: Dmitrii Gavrilev
Venue: ICML 2026 Oral
First: 2026-06-10T16:20:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:20:24+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 Workshop on Machine Learning for Audio (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Expressive performance rendering (EPR) aims to generate realistic performances constrained on sequences of notes. However, flow matching audio editing models manipulate only synchronized music samples of the same duration, limiting their understanding of expressive timing. We introduce PianoKontext, a flow matching rendering model for classical piano music that generates variable-length performances in the latent space of a pretrained Music2Latent model. We synthesize MIDI scores into deadpan audio and employ Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) in the latent space to construct paired data for training. The aligned embeddings are concatenated in DiT blocks, allowing for a simple and effective learning of the dependencies between the score and performances. Audio samples are available at our demo page: https://realfolkcode.github.io/pianokontext_demo/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PianoKontext：从无表情上下文生成富有表现力的钢琴演奏
富有表现力的演奏生成（EPR）旨在根据音符序列生成逼真的演奏，但流匹配音频编辑模型仅能处理相同时长的同步音乐样本，限制了其对表现力时序的理解。我们提出了PianoKontext，一种针对古典钢琴音乐的流匹配渲染模型，它在预训练的Music2Latent模型的潜在空间中生成可变长度的演奏。我们将MIDI乐谱合成无表情音频，并在潜在空间中使用动态时间规整（DTW）构建训练数据对。对齐的嵌入在DiT模块中拼接，从而简单有效地学习乐谱与演奏之间的依赖关系。音频示例可在我们的演示页面查看：https://realfolkcode.github.io/pianokontext_demo/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing audio editing models that only handle synchronized, same-duration music samples and thus lack expressive timing understanding, PianoKontext introduces a flow matching model for classical piano that generates variable-length performances in the latent space of a pretrained Music2Latent model. It synthesizes MIDI scores into deadpan audio and uses Dynamic Time Warping in latent space to create training pairs, then concatenates aligned embeddings into DiT blocks to learn score-performance dependencies. Experimental results show the model successfully generates expressive piano performances that align with the original scores while preserving musical intent and timing variability.
为了解决现有音频编辑模型仅能处理等长同步音乐样本、难以理解表达性时值的问题，本文提出PianoKontext，一种基于流匹配的古典钢琴表达性演奏生成模型。该方法利用预训练的Music2Latent模型将死板音频编码至潜在空间，通过动态时间规整对乐谱与演奏嵌入进行对齐，并在DiT模块中拼接嵌入以学习乐谱与演奏间的依赖关系。实验表明，PianoKontext能有效生成具有时值变化的表达性钢琴演奏，显著捕捉原始死板音频中缺失的演奏细节。
CCKS: Consensus-based Communication and Knowledge Sharing
Authors: Jinyuan Zu, Xiaowei Lv, Yongcai Wang, Deying Li, Yunjun Han, Wenping Chen, Fengyi Zhang, Naiqi Wu
First: 2026-06-10T16:20:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:20:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In Decentralized Training and Decentralized Execution (DTDE) for cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), action-advising-based knowledge sharing promotes interpretable and scalable cooperation among agents. However, current action advising approaches often adhere too much to the teacher's guidance without evaluating teacher-student compatibility, which causes excessive advising, suboptimal stability, and degraded performance. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents a Consensus-based Communication and Knowledge Sharing (CCKS) framework, which allows agents to adopt recommendations based on consensus-derived constraints and to follow the teacher's instructions more smartly. This mechanism enables agents to balance exploration and learning from experienced teachers, improving overall performance. The key is the consensus model construction, for which we propose to employ contrastive learning to construct consensus models based on local observations in the agents' training phase. In action selection, agents score and choose actions based on consensus and shared knowledge. Designed as a plug-and-play solution, CCKS integrates seamlessly with existing DTDE algorithms. Experiments conducted in the Google Research Football environment and the complex StarCraft II Multi-Agent Challenge demonstrate that the integration with CCKS significantly improves cooperation efficiency, learning speed, and overall performance compared with current DTDE baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/yuanxpy/CCKS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于共识的通信与知识共享
在用于协作多智能体强化学习（MARL）的去中心化训练与执行（DTDE）中，基于行动建议的知识共享可促进智能体间可解释且可扩展的合作。然而，当前行动建议方法往往过度依赖教师指导，而未评估教师与学生间的兼容性，导致过度建议、稳定性不足及性能下降。为解决这些问题，本文提出一种基于共识的通信与知识共享（CCKS）框架，使智能体可根据共识推导的约束采纳建议，并更智能地遵循教师指令。该机制使智能体能在探索与向经验丰富的教师学习间取得平衡，从而提升整体性能。其核心在于共识模型构建，我们提出在智能体训练阶段采用对比学习，基于局部观测构建共识模型。在行动选择中，智能体根据共识与共享知识评分并选择行动。CCKS设计为即插即用方案，可无缝集成现有DTDE算法。在Google Research Football环境及复杂StarCraft II多智能体挑战中的实验表明，集成CCKS显著提升了合作效率、学习速度及整体性能，相较当前DTDE基线方法。代码见https://github.com/yuanxpy/CCKS。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current action-advising methods in decentralized MARL that lack teacher-student compatibility evaluation, this paper introduces CCKS, a consensus-based framework enabling agents to selectively follow teacher advice using consensus-derived constraints. The method constructs consensus models via contrastive learning from local observations during training, allowing agents to score and select actions based on both consensus and shared knowledge. Experimental results in Google Research Football and StarCraft II show CCKS significantly enhances cooperation efficiency, learning speed, and overall performance compared to existing DTDE baselines.
为解决当前多智能体强化学习中教师指导盲目且缺乏兼容性评估的问题，本文提出CCKS框架，通过共识约束机制使智能体智能采纳教师建议。该框架在训练阶段利用对比学习构建基于局部观测的共识模型，智能体据此评分并选择动作。实验表明，在Google Research Football与StarCraft II多智能体挑战中，CCKS显著提升协作效率、学习速度与整体性能，优于现有DTDE基线方法。
Holding the FP8 Quality Ceiling at 8-Bit Weights and Activations: INT8 and GGUF Post-Training Quantization of Ideogram 4.0 for Consumer GPUs
Authors: Deep Gandhi, Ali Asaria, Tony Salomone
First: 2026-06-10T16:19:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:19:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-training quantization lets large text-to-image diffusion transformers run on consumer GPUs, yet the hardware-specific trade-offs are seldom measured directly. We quantize Ideogram 4.0 - a 9.3B flow-matching diffusion transformer (DiT), shipped as two separate-weight copies of a single-stream 34-layer backbone for classifier-free guidance and conditioned by a Qwen3-VL-8B encoder - for Ampere RTX 3090 GPUs, which lack FP8 tensor cores. Our INT8 W8A8 recipe (per-channel weights, per-token dynamic activations, SmoothQuant, and mixed-precision protection of a small high-fragility layer set) holds the FP8 quality ceiling: on a 200-prompt benchmark the paired same-seed bootstrap CI for INT8-FP8 includes zero on both Pick and CLIP, while INT8 improves on NF4 by $+1.9$ CLIP (95% CI $[+1.21,+2.64]$, excluding zero). A per-category OCR analysis, to our knowledge unreported for this model class, confirms text legibility is preserved, and an ablation isolates protection of the FFN down-projections as the dominant quality lever. Our GGUF Q4_K quantization beats NF4 at equal on-disk size and is the Pareto winner on the quality-memory frontier, with paired confidence intervals excluding zero (Q8_0 is quality neutral). Finally, we characterize where 8-bit quantization helps and where it does not: INT8's weights match FP8's footprint rather than shrink it, so a speed gain on Ampere awaits a fused INT8 kernel.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在8位权重和激活下保持FP8质量上限：为消费级GPU对Ideogram 4.0进行INT8和GGUF后训练量化
后训练量化使大型文本到图像扩散Transformer能够在消费级GPU上运行，但硬件特定的权衡很少被直接测量。我们针对Ampere架构RTX 3090 GPU（无FP8张量核心）对Ideogram 4.0——一个9.3B参数的流匹配扩散Transformer（DiT），以单流34层骨干网络的两个独立权重副本形式发布，用于无分类器引导并由Qwen3-VL-8B编码器条件化——进行了量化。我们的INT8 W8A8配方（按通道权重、按令牌动态激活、SmoothQuant，以及对少量高脆弱层的混合精度保护）保持了FP8质量上限：在200个提示的基准测试中，INT8-FP8配对相同种子的Bootstrap置信区间在Pick和CLIP上均包含零，而INT8相比NF4提升+1.9 CLIP（95%置信区间[+1.21, +2.64]，不包含零）。我们首次对这一模型类别进行按类别OCR分析，确认文本可读性得以保留，消融实验表明FFN下投影的保护是主要质量提升因素。我们的GGUF Q4_K量化在同等磁盘大小下优于NF4，并在质量-内存前沿上为帕累托最优解（Q8_0质量中性）。最后，我们明确了8位量化在哪些场景下有效、哪些无效：INT8权重与FP8占用空间相当而非更小，因此在Ampere架构上实现速度增益需依赖融合INT8内核。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to run large diffusion models on consumer GPUs without sacrificing quality, this work quantizes Ideogram 4.0 using INT8 weights and activations on Ampere RTX 3090 GPUs lacking FP8 tensor cores. The method combines per-channel weights, per-token dynamic activations, SmoothQuant, and targeted mixed-precision protection for fragile layers. Experimental results show INT8 matches FP8 quality on a 200-prompt benchmark with confidence intervals including zero on both Pick and CLIP scores, and outperforms NF4 by +1.9 CLIP (95% CI [1.21, 2.64]). An OCR analysis confirms text legibility is preserved, and ablation studies identify FFN down-projections as critical for quality. GGUF Q4_K quantization also outperforms NF4 at equal disk size and dominates the quality-memory tradeoff. However, INT8 weights do not reduce footprint compared to FP8, so speedup requires fused INT8 kernels.
为在消费级显卡上运行大型扩散模型而不牺牲质量，本文针对无FP8张量核心的Ampere RTX 3090显卡，对Ideogram 4.0模型采用INT8权重与激活量化方法，结合通道级权重、词元级动态激活、SmoothQuant及脆弱层混合精度保护。实验表明，INT8在200提示基准测试中达到FP8质量上限，Pick与CLIP得分的配对置信区间包含零，且相较NF4提升+1.9 CLIP（95%置信区间[1.21, 2.64]）。OCR分析证实文本可读性保持，消融实验指出FFN下投影层是质量关键。GGUF Q4_K量化在同等磁盘大小下优于NF4，且在质量-内存权衡中为帕累托最优，Q8_0则无质量差异。INT8权重与FP8占用空间相当，表明性能提升需依赖融合INT8内核。
CoVar: Confidence-Variance-Guided Pseudo-Label Selection for Semi-Supervised Learning
Authors: Jinshi Liu, Lei He, Pan Liu
First: 2026-01-16T02:51:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:18:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pseudo-label selection in semi-supervised learning is commonly driven by maximum-confidence thresholds, yet confidence alone can be unreliable under model overconfidence and class imbalance. We propose CoVar, a confidence--variance framework that assesses pseudo-label reliability by jointly modeling Maximum Confidence (MC) and Residual-Class Variance (RCV). Starting from entropy minimization, we derive a second-order cross-entropy approximation showing that low-loss pseudo-labels are favored when MC is high and RCV is low, with a confidence-dependent penalty that becomes stronger for near-certain predictions. Based on this criterion, CoVar embeds predictions into a two-dimensional confidence--variance space and uses SVD-based spectral relaxation to separate reliable and unreliable predictions without hand-tuned confidence thresholds. Cluster-wise Gaussian weighting then converts this separation into per-sample training weights. The resulting weights can be integrated into existing semi-supervised segmentation and classification pipelines during training and introduce no inference-time overhead. Experiments on PASCAL VOC 2012, Cityscapes, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, and STL-10 show clear gains on VOC and Cityscapes under matched backbones, as well as competitive or improved error rates on standard classification benchmarks. These results indicate that residual-class dispersion provides a useful signal complementary to confidence for robust pseudo-label selection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoVar：基于置信度-方差引导的伪标签选择用于半监督学习
半监督学习中的伪标签选择通常依赖最大置信度阈值，但置信度单独使用在模型过自信和类别不平衡情况下可能不可靠。我们提出CoVar，一种置信度-方差框架，通过联合建模最大置信度（MC）和残余类别方差（RCV）来评估伪标签可靠性。从熵最小化出发，我们推导出二阶交叉熵近似，表明当MC高且RCV低时，低损失伪标签更受青睐，且置信度依赖的惩罚项在近似确定预测时更强。基于此准则，CoVar将预测嵌入二维置信度-方差空间，并利用基于SVD的谱松弛方法分离可靠与不可靠预测，无需手动调整置信度阈值。随后，按簇加权高斯权重将此分离转化为每样本训练权重。所得权重可无缝集成到现有半监督分割与分类训练流程中，且无推理时开销。在PASCAL VOC 2012、Cityscapes、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、SVHN和STL-10数据集上的实验表明，在匹配骨干网络下，VOC和Cityscapes上表现显著提升，且在标准分类基准上达到竞争性或更优的错误率。结果表明，残余类别分散度为置信度提供了有益的补充信号，有助于稳健的伪标签选择。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unreliability of confidence-based pseudo-label selection under model overconfidence and class imbalance, the paper proposes CoVar, a framework that jointly evaluates prediction confidence and residual-class variance to assess pseudo-label reliability. It derives a second-order cross-entropy approximation from entropy minimization, showing that reliable pseudo-labels are those with high confidence and low class variance, with a confidence-dependent penalty for near-certain predictions. CoVar maps predictions into a 2D confidence-variance space, applies spectral relaxation via SVD to separate reliable from unreliable samples, and assigns per-sample weights using cluster-wise Gaussian weighting. Experiments across segmentation and classification benchmarks demonstrate consistent performance gains on VOC and Cityscapes with matched backbones, and competitive or improved accuracy on standard classification datasets, confirming that residual-class variance complements confidence for robust pseudo-label selection.
为了解决模型过自信和类别不平衡下基于置信度的伪标签选择不可靠的问题，CoVar提出了一种结合置信度与残差类别方差的框架，用于评估伪标签可靠性。它通过二阶交叉熵近似推导出：当置信度高且类别方差低时，伪标签更可靠，并对高置信度但高方差的预测施加置信度依赖惩罚。CoVar将预测嵌入二维置信度-方差空间，利用SVD谱松弛分离可靠与不可靠样本，再通过聚类高斯加权生成样本权重。实验在多个分割与分类基准上验证，显示在PASCAL VOC和Cityscapes上性能提升，在标准分类数据集上达到竞争或更优准确率，证明残差类别方差可作为置信度的有效补充，提升伪标签选择鲁棒性。
Mathematical perspective on genetic algorithms with optimization guided operators
Authors: Anna Brandenberger, Ilan Doron-Arad, Elchanan Mossel
First: 2026-06-10T16:18:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:18:36+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work in ML applies genetic algorithms at inference time to iteratively improve solutions to optimization problems. The basic mutation and recombination operators involved are qualitatively different from those studied classically. Mutations are no longer random; an ML algorithm mutates a solution with the goal of improving an objective. Similarly, recombination is not based on random collages of parent solutions. Instead, it is an ML optimization-based operator whose goal is to synthesize improved solutions from its inputs. Thus, these mutation and recombination operators are more likely to improve the objective, but their computational cost is much higher. We introduce a general model of genetic algorithms and formulating optimization in this model as a query-complexity problem, using the language of reinforcement learning. We then study specialized models. We show that some optimization problems require generation, mutation, and recombination to be solved. We then obtain qualitatively tight algorithms for a family of problems within this framework that captures the nontrivial role of diversity in the solution pool, a key feature of practical ML genetic algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带有优化引导算子的遗传算法的数学视角
近期机器学习工作在推理阶段应用遗传算法，以迭代改进优化问题的解。所涉及的基本变异和重组算子与经典研究中的算子在质上不同：变异不再随机，而是由机器学习算法驱动，旨在提升目标函数；重组也不再基于父代解的随机组合，而是基于机器学习优化的算子，旨在从输入中合成更优解。因此，这些变异和重组算子更可能提升目标函数，但计算成本显著更高。我们引入遗传算法的一般模型，将优化问题在此模型中表述为查询复杂度问题，使用强化学习的语言。随后研究特定模型，证明某些优化问题需同时依赖生成、变异和重组才能解决。最后，在该框架下，我们为一类问题获得定性紧致的算法，该框架捕捉了解池中多样性这一实际机器学习遗传算法的关键特征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how modern genetic algorithms in machine learning differ from classical ones, this paper models genetic algorithms with optimization-guided mutation and recombination as a query-complexity problem within a reinforcement learning framework. The authors analyze specialized models to show that certain optimization problems require all three operations—generation, mutation, and recombination—to be solved effectively, and they derive qualitatively tight algorithms for a class of problems that emphasize the importance of solution diversity, a critical factor in practical ML genetic algorithms.
为理解现代机器学习中遗传算法与经典遗传算法的区别，本文将具有优化引导的变异和重组操作的遗传算法建模为强化学习框架下的查询复杂性问题。作者分析了特定情形，证明某些优化问题必须同时依赖生成、变异和重组才能有效求解，并针对捕捉实际机器学习遗传算法中解池多样性这一关键特征的问题类别，推导出具有定性紧致性的算法。
LibriConvo: Simulating Conversations from Read Literature for ASR and Diarization
Authors: Máté Gedeon, Péter Mihajlik
First: 2025-10-27T13:35:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:18:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TSD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce LibriConvo, a synthetic conversational speech corpus for speaker diarization and automatic speech recognition (ASR), built by instantiating the previously proposed Speaker-Aware Simulated Conversation (SASC) framework in a dataset and benchmarking setting. The main contribution of this paper is a corpus construction pipeline and benchmark derived from that framework. To make the data more suitable for downstream ASR and diarization, conversational timing statistics are estimated from English CallHome using external voice activity detection, long pauses are compressed, LibriTTS utterances are grouped by book to improve local semantic continuity, and room impulse responses are selected with a spatial-plausibility heuristic. The resulting corpus contains 240.1 hours of audio across 1,496 dialogues involving 830 speakers, partitioned into speaker-disjoint train, validation, and test splits. We report baseline results for both diarization and ASR. On the test split, Sortformer outperforms the pyannote pipeline in diarization (11.1\% vs.~24.4\% DER). For ASR, a Fast Conformer-CTC XLarge model fine-tuned with Serialized Output Training achieves 7.29\% WER and 6.97\% cpWER, outperforming zero-shot Whisper-large-v3. These results position LibriConvo as a practical benchmark for studying synthetic conversational speech and for evaluating multi-speaker speech processing systems.

标题：LibriConvo：为ASR和说话人聚类模拟文学对话
我们提出了LibriConvo，一个用于说话人聚类和自动语音识别（ASR）的合成对话语音语料库，通过在数据集和基准测试环境中实现先前提出的说话人感知模拟对话（SASC）框架构建而成。本文的主要贡献是基于该框架的语料库构建流程和基准测试。为使数据更适用于下游的ASR和聚类任务，我们从英语CallHome数据集利用外部语音活动检测估算对话时序统计量，压缩长停顿，按书籍分组LibriTTS语句以提升局部语义连贯性，并采用空间合理性启发式选择房间脉冲响应。最终语料库包含1496个对话、830位说话人，总计240.1小时音频，划分为说话人不重叠的训练、验证和测试集。我们在测试集上报告了聚类和ASR的基线结果：Sortformer在聚类任务中优于pyannote管道（11.1% vs. 24.4% DER）；ASR方面，经过序列化输出训练微调的Fast Conformer-CTC XLarge模型达到7.29% WER和6.97% cpWER，优于零样本Whisper-large-v3。这些结果使LibriConvo成为研究合成对话语音和评估多说话人语音处理系统的实用基准。

Finding Sparse Subnetworks in One Training Cycle via Progressive Magnitude-Based Pruning
Authors: Romana Qureshi, Hafida Benhidour, Said Kerrache, Nahlah Aljeraisy
First: 2026-06-10T16:17:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:17:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural network pruning reduces model size by removing less important parameters while aiming to preserve predictive performance. Although the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis (LTH) shows that sparse subnetworks can match dense networks when trained from suitable initializations, its iterative pruning procedure requires multiple complete training cycles. This work evaluates progressive magnitude-based pruning as a single-cycle alternative. The method gradually increases sparsity during training using a linear schedule and updates pruning masks based on active weight magnitudes. We conduct systematic experiments on CIFAR-10 and MNIST across ResNet, VGG-style, and LeNet architectures, comparing the proposed method with representative iterative and initialization-based pruning baselines, including LTH, SNIP, and GraSP. On CIFAR-10, the method achieves 95.12\% accuracy on ResNet-18 at 72.9\% sparsity, compared with 90.5\% reported for LTH. At extreme sparsity, it achieves 93.13\% accuracy on a VGG-like architecture at 97\% sparsity, compared with approximately 92.0\% for SNIP, and 93.44\% accuracy on VGG-19 at 97.97\% sparsity, compared with 92.19\% for GraSP at 98\% sparsity. A sparsity-accuracy analysis on ResNet-18 further shows that accuracy remains within 0.1 percentage points of the dense baseline across 70--85\% sparsity. These results indicate that progressive magnitude-based pruning provides an effective single-cycle approach for neural network sparsification under the evaluated settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过渐进式基于幅度的剪枝在单次训练周期内发现稀疏子网络
神经网络剪枝通过移除不重要的参数来减小模型规模，同时力求保持预测性能。尽管彩票假设（LTH）表明，从合适初始化开始训练时稀疏子网络可匹配密集网络，但其迭代剪枝过程需要多次完整训练周期。本文评估了渐进式基于幅度的剪枝作为单周期替代方案：训练过程中通过线性调度逐步增加稀疏度，并根据活跃权重幅度更新剪枝掩码。我们在CIFAR-10和MNIST数据集上，针对ResNet、VGG风格和LeNet架构开展系统性实验，将所提方法与代表性迭代和初始化剪枝基线（包括LTH、SNIP和GraSP）进行比较。在CIFAR-10上，该方法在ResNet-18架构下以72.9%稀疏度达到95.12%准确率，优于LTH报告的90.5%；在极端稀疏度下，于VGG类架构以97%稀疏度达到93.13%准确率，优于SNIP的约92.0%；在VGG-19架构以97.97%稀疏度达到93.44%准确率，优于GraSP在98%稀疏度下的92.19%。对ResNet-18的稀疏度-准确率分析进一步表明，70%至85%稀疏度范围内，准确率始终在密集基线的0.1个百分点以内。这些结果表明，渐进式基于幅度的剪枝在评估设置下为神经网络稀疏化提供了一种有效的单周期方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to achieve sparse neural network subnetworks without requiring multiple training cycles, this work introduces progressive magnitude-based pruning, which incrementally increases sparsity during training using a linear schedule and updates pruning masks based on active weight magnitudes. Evaluated on CIFAR-10 and MNIST across ResNet, VGG-style, and LeNet architectures, the method outperforms iterative and initialization-based baselines like LTH, SNIP, and GraSP, achieving 95.12% accuracy on ResNet-18 at 72.9% sparsity — surpassing LTH’s 90.5% — and maintaining near-dense accuracy (within 0.1 percentage points) across 70–85% sparsity.
为在单次训练周期内实现稀疏子网络，本文提出一种渐进式基于权重幅度的剪枝方法，通过线性调度逐步增加稀疏度，并根据活跃权重幅度动态更新剪枝掩码。在CIFAR-10和MNIST数据集上，针对ResNet、VGG风格和LeNet架构进行系统实验，该方法优于LTH、SNIP和GraSP等基线方法，例如在ResNet-18上以72.9%稀疏度达到95.12%准确率，在VGG类架构上以97%稀疏度达到93.13%准确率，且在70–85%稀疏度区间内，准确率始终与密集模型相差不超过0.1个百分点。
Finding Multiple Interpretations in Datasets
Authors: Matthew Chak, Paul Anderson
First: 2026-06-10T16:17:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:17:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we propose an approach to finding sets of similar-performing models (in terms of loss/accuracy measurements) with highly different context-aware characteristics. Through experiments on the METABRIC dataset, we show that the proposed method finds multiple models with highly different gene expressions than those found by the control methodology without performance penalties. We argue that the proposed methodology is important whenever one aims to analyze any global characteristic of a model to extract insight into the underlying phenomenon being studied.

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to uncover diverse model behaviors while maintaining similar performance, this paper introduces a method to identify multiple models that achieve comparable loss or accuracy but exhibit highly distinct context-aware characteristics. Applied to the METABRIC dataset, the method successfully discovers models with markedly different gene expression patterns compared to those found by conventional approaches, without sacrificing performance. The results demonstrate that such diverse model interpretations can provide deeper insight into underlying phenomena, supporting the method’s utility in global model analysis.

Beyond Fully Random Masking: Attention-Guided Denoising and Optimization for Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Jia Deng, Junyi Li, Wayne Xin Zhao, Jinpeng Wang, Hongyu Lu, Ji-Rong Wen
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-10T16:14:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:14:23+00:00
Comments: 13 pages. Accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer an efficient alternative to autoregressive models through parallel decoding, yet existing post-training methods largely rely on random masking strategies that overlook intrinsic token dependencies. In this work, we present an empirical analysis of attention in dLLMs and show that tokens attending more strongly to unmasked context exhibit greater generation stability and play a critical role in reasoning. Motivated by these findings, we propose AGDO, an attention-guided denoising and optimization framework that aligns both training and optimization with attention-derived dependencies. AGDO determines the denoising order based on attention structure and emphasizes attention-critical tokens during supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical and coding benchmarks demonstrate that AGDO consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming state-of-the-art post-training methods for dLLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越完全随机掩码：面向扩散语言模型的注意力引导去噪与优化
扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）通过并行解码为自回归模型提供了高效替代方案，但现有后训练方法主要依赖随机掩码策略，忽视了内在的词元依赖关系。本文通过实证分析dLLMs中的注意力机制，发现对未掩码上下文注意力更强的词元生成更稳定，且在推理中起关键作用。受此启发，我们提出AGDO框架，通过注意力衍生的依赖关系对训练与优化进行对齐。AGDO依据注意力结构确定去噪顺序，并在监督微调与强化学习中强调注意力关键词元。在数学与编程基准测试中，AGDO持续提升推理性能，优于当前最先进的dLLM后训练方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that attention patterns in diffusion language models reveal critical token dependencies for stable generation and reasoning, this work introduces AGDO, an attention-guided denoising and optimization framework. AGDO leverages attention structure to determine denoising order and prioritizes attention-critical tokens during supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experimental results on mathematical and coding benchmarks show that AGDO consistently outperforms existing post-training methods, achieving superior reasoning performance across diverse tasks.
鉴于扩散语言模型中的注意力模式揭示了随机掩码方法忽略的关键词依赖关系，本文提出AGDO框架，该框架基于注意力结构指导去噪顺序，并在监督微调与强化学习中优先处理对推理至关重要的词。在数学与编程基准测试中，AGDO持续提升推理性能，优于当前最先进的扩散语言模型后训练方法。
P3D-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Parametric 3D Generation and Structural Reasoning
Authors: Yikang Yang, Zhanpeng Hu, Youtian Lin, Mengqi Zhou, Jingxi Xu, Feihu Zhang, Jiaheng Liu, Yao Yao
First: 2026-06-09T17:36:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:12:45+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://spatiaos.github.io/projects/P3D-Bench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal large language models can write code to produce complex programs as well as use programs to do 3D modeling, which opens up a new avenue for 3D generation powered by their priors, world knowledge and reasoning. Yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate 3D modeling through code. Such modeling demands more than runnable code: from a text or visual specification, a model must generate a parametric 3D program that is geometrically precise, semantically aligned and assembly-consistent. We introduce P3D-Bench, a benchmark for parametric 3D generation. Unlike a 3D mesh, a parametric 3D program exposes explicit dimensions, construction operations and part relations, revealing whether a model recovers a design's structure, not just its appearance. Under a unified protocol, P3D-Bench covers three task families (Text-to-3D, Image-to-3D and Assembly-3D) and scores each output for executability, geometric fidelity, topology, text-grounded constraints, multiview semantic alignment and part-level structure. We evaluate frontier MLLMs and text-only LLMs on 400 text cases, 400 image cases and 203 annotated assemblies, with domain-specific models as reference points. Our extensive evaluation yields three findings. First, assemblies are the hardest setting, where models still fail to compose multiple parts into a coherent structure. Second, models can often recover the global shape and semantic identity of the target object, yet fail to reproduce the precise parametric geometry specified by the input. Third, part-level modeling remains weak on assemblies, where models recover neither the geometry of each part nor the right number of parts. These results position P3D-Bench as a benchmark for evaluating precise parametric geometry and part-level structure in parametric 3D generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：P3D-Bench：用于参数化3D生成与结构推理的多模态大语言模型基准
多模态大语言模型不仅能编写代码生成复杂程序，还能利用程序进行3D建模，这为基于其先验知识、世界知识和推理能力的3D生成开辟了新途径。然而，现有基准很少通过代码评估3D建模。此类建模不仅需要可运行的代码：从文本或视觉规范出发，模型必须生成几何精确、语义一致且装配一致的参数化3D程序。我们引入P3D-Bench，一个参数化3D生成基准。与3D网格不同，参数化3D程序显式暴露尺寸、构造操作和部件关系，揭示模型是否恢复设计的结构，而不仅是外观。在统一协议下，P3D-Bench涵盖三大任务家族（文本到3D、图像到3D和装配3D），并针对每个输出评估可执行性、几何保真度、拓扑结构、文本约束、多视角语义对齐及部件级结构。我们在400个文本案例、400个图像案例和203个标注装配上评估前沿多模态大语言模型和纯文本大语言模型，以领域特定模型为参考基准。我们的全面评估得出三项发现：第一，装配是最难场景，模型仍无法将多个部件组合成连贯结构；第二，模型通常能恢复目标对象的全局形状和语义身份，但无法复现输入所指定的精确参数化几何；第三，装配中部件级建模能力仍较弱，模型既无法恢复每个部件的几何结构，也无法正确确定部件数量。这些结果确立了P3D-Bench作为评估参数化3D生成中精确参数几何与部件级结构的基准地位。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate multimodal large language models' (MLLMs) ability to generate precise parametric 3D models via code, the authors introduce P3D-Bench, a benchmark that assesses models across text-to-3D, image-to-3D, and assembly-to-3D tasks using explicit geometric, semantic, and structural metrics. Models are evaluated on executability, geometric fidelity, topology, constraint adherence, multiview alignment, and part-level structure across 400 text cases, 400 image cases, and 203 assemblies. Results show that while models often capture global shape and semantic identity, they struggle with precise parametric geometry and part-level consistency, especially in assembly tasks where models fail to correctly compose parts or maintain their geometry.
为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过代码生成精确参数化3D模型的能力，作者提出了P3D-Bench基准，涵盖文本到3D、图像到3D和装配3D三大任务，评估标准包括可执行性、几何保真度、拓扑结构、文本约束、多视角语义对齐及部件级结构。在400个文本案例、400个图像案例和203个装配案例的测试中发现，模型虽常能捕捉目标物体的整体形状与语义身份，但在精确参数化几何和部件级结构（尤其装配任务中的部件几何与数量）方面表现薄弱，装配任务难度最高。
NetBurst: Event-Centric Forecasting of Bursty, Intermittent Time Series
Authors: Satyandra Guthula, Jaber Daneshamooz, Charles Fleming, Kesheng Wu, Walter Willinger, Arpit Gupta
First: 2025-10-25T18:48:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:11:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Network operators monitor their infrastructure by collecting telemetry data such as packet counts, byte rates, or flow volumes, yet answering the questions that effective operations demand -- forecasting future load, diagnosing and characterizing anomalies, and searching for and retrieving historical precedents -- requires more than raw measurements. Bridging this gap calls for learned representations: compact per-entity summaries that capture temporal dynamics from each entity's univariate time series. Time-series foundation models are the natural starting point, but they are designed for dense, periodic benchmark datasets -- the \emph{mild} statistical regime. However, network telemetry data inhabits the \emph{wild} regime: operationally relevant events are rare, separated by variable-length stretches of low or no activity (``ebbs''), with intermittent bursts of heavy-tailed extremes (``tides''). We present NetBurst, an event-centric pipeline that collapses ebbs, separates each time series into a stream of burst timings and a stream of burst magnitudes, and learns a single representation serving all three operational tasks. Compared to the strongest competitors among eight baselines -- including Amazon's Chronos-2 and Datadog's Toto -- and across nine production telemetry configurations, NetBurst reduces median forecasting error by $1.3$--$116\times$ on wild-regime data with a $1.0$--$7.5\times$ better match to the true burst distribution, and matches baselines on mild-regime benchmarks. For characterizing anomalies, NetBurst produces balanced, well-spread clusters that are $16\times$ more describable in operator-familiar terms under a novel interpretability score, and cluster-filtered search delivers $7.5\times$ faster end-to-end retrieval.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NetBurst：面向突发性、间歇性时间序列的事件为中心的预测
网络运营商通过收集数据包计数、字节速率或流量体积等遥测数据来监控其基础设施，但要回答有效运维所需的问题——预测未来负载、诊断和刻画异常、搜索和检索历史先例——需要的不仅是原始测量数据。弥合这一差距需要学习到的表示：每个实体的紧凑摘要，能够从其单变量时间序列中捕捉时间动态。时间序列基础模型是自然起点，但它们是为密集、周期性的基准数据集设计的——即“温和”统计环境。然而，网络遥测数据处于“野性”环境：操作相关事件罕见，被长短不一的低活动或无活动期（“低潮”）隔开，并伴有间歇性的重尾极端值爆发（“潮汐”）。我们提出NetBurst，一个以事件为中心的管道，将低潮压缩，将每个时间序列拆分为突发时间流和突发强度流，并学习一个服务于三项运维任务的统一表示。与八种基线中最强的竞争对手——包括亚马逊的Chronos-2和Datadog的Toto——相比，在九种生产遥测配置下，NetBurst在野性环境数据上将中位数预测误差降低1.3至116倍，对真实突发分布的匹配度提升1.0至7.5倍，并在温和环境基准上与基线持平。在刻画异常方面，NetBurst生成平衡且分布广泛的聚类，在新颖可解释性评分下，其异常描述性比基线高16倍，聚类过滤搜索实现7.5倍更快的端到端检索。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle bursty and intermittent network telemetry data that traditional time-series models fail to capture, NetBurst introduces an event-centric approach that separates time series into burst timings and magnitudes, then learns compact representations for forecasting, anomaly detection, and historical search. Evaluated against eight baselines including Chronos-2 and Toto across nine production configurations, NetBurst achieves 1.3–116× lower median forecasting error and 1.0–7.5× better burst distribution matching on wild-regime data, while matching or outperforming baselines on mild-regime benchmarks. For anomaly characterization, it generates more interpretable clusters and enables 7.5× faster retrieval via cluster-filtered search.
为应对传统模型难以处理的网络遥测数据中突发且间歇性的特征，NetBurst提出以事件为中心的方法，将时间序列拆分为突发时间与强度流，并学习统一表征以支持预测、异常检测与历史检索。在九种生产配置与八种基线模型（含Chronos-2与Toto）对比中，NetBurst在野性数据集上预测误差降低1.3–116倍，突发分布匹配度提升1.0–7.5倍，而在温和数据集上表现与基线持平。异常检测方面，其聚类更易被运维人员理解（提升16倍可解释性），且通过聚类过滤检索速度提升7.5倍。
The Impossibility of Eliciting Latent Knowledge
Authors: Korbinian Friedl, Francis Rhys Ward, Paul Yushin Rapoport, Tom Everitt, Jonathan Richens
First: 2026-06-10T16:11:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:11:13+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 3 figures. Includes proofs in appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advanced AI systems have extensive knowledge of their environments; in fact, their knowledge may (far) exceed that of their developers or users. Consequently, a desirable property for an AI system is that it is honest -- that it accurately reports its beliefs about the world. Designing an AI system to be honest may be difficult, especially if we want to ask it questions about latent variables in the environment -- variables which are hidden from the human interacting with it. This gives rise to the problem of eliciting latent knowledge (ELK): the problem of training an AI agent to honestly report its beliefs. In this paper, we make ELK formally precise using Causal Influence Diagrams (CIDs). CIDs can be used to describe the relationship between an agent's training environment and its subjective representation of the world. We use CIDs to formalise the distinction between observable and latent variables, to specify what exactly it means for an agent to be honest, and to formally define goal misgeneralisation. We show that, under certain circumstances, developers can incentivise an agent to honestly answer questions by providing correct feedback during training. However, a natural, but undesirable, way for an agent to generalise is to provide answers which humans would evaluate as true, rather than honest answers. We prove an impossibility theorem stating: There is no feedback-based training strategy that depends only on agent behaviour and with certainty produces an honest agent, even if feedback is perfect during training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提取潜在知识的不可能性
高级人工智能系统对其环境具有广泛的知识，甚至可能远超其开发者或用户的认知。因此，一个理想的AI系统特性是诚实——即准确报告其对世界的信念。设计一个诚实的AI系统可能很困难，特别是当我们希望它回答环境中隐藏的潜在变量问题时。这引出了“提取潜在知识”（ELK）问题：训练AI代理诚实报告其信念的问题。本文使用因果影响图（CIDs）对ELK进行形式化。CIDs可用于描述代理训练环境与其主观世界表征之间的关系。我们使用CIDs形式化可观测变量与潜在变量的区别，明确代理“诚实”的具体含义，并形式化定义目标误泛化。我们证明，在某些条件下，开发者可通过训练期间提供正确反馈激励代理诚实作答。然而，代理自然但不理想的泛化方式是提供人类评估为“真”的答案，而非诚实答案。我们证明了一个不可能性定理：不存在仅依赖代理行为、且能以确定性方式产生诚实代理的反馈训练策略，即使训练期间反馈完美。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of ensuring AI systems truthfully report their internal beliefs—especially about hidden environmental variables—the paper formalizes the Eliciting Latent Knowledge (ELK) problem using Causal Influence Diagrams. It defines honesty as accurate belief reporting and introduces goal misgeneralization, where agents may learn to mimic human evaluations rather than truth. The authors prove an impossibility result: no feedback-based training method relying solely on agent behavior can guarantee honesty, even with perfect feedback during training.
该论文探讨了从AI系统中提取潜变量知识的挑战，动机在于使智能体能真实报告关于隐藏环境变量的信念。通过因果影响图，论文形式化了可观测与潜变量的区别，定义了诚实性，并引入目标误泛化概念。作者表明，尽管训练期间反馈可激励诚实回答，但不存在仅依赖智能体行为且能保证诚实的反馈训练策略，即使反馈完美，从而得出一个不可能性定理。
VOID: Defeating Unauthorized Mimicry in Latent Diffusion Models
Authors: Chunlin Qiu, Ang Li, Tianxiao Huang, Ruilin Gan, Yunjie Ge, Shenyi Zhang, Huayi Duan, Lingchen Zhao, Chao Shen, Qian Wang
First: 2026-06-10T16:08:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:08:25+00:00
Comments: To appear in the 35th USENIX Security Symposium (USENIX Security 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have revolutionized visual synthesis, they are increasingly exploited for unauthorized mimicry of individuals. Existing defenses inject deceptive perturbations to steer the generated images toward irrelevant targets. However, this approach hinges on an ungrounded assumption: subtle perturbations can maintain their deceptive efficacy throughout an LDM's extensive generation process. In reality, the model's innate restoration mechanism will remove such perturbations and cause individual identities to re-emerge in the images generated. We propose VOID, a defense framework that overcomes this conundrum by manipulating an LDM's intrinsic stochasticity. VOID perturbs the diffusion pipeline in two novel ways: 1) amplifying the latent encoding errors to shatter an image's semantic structure, and 2) counteracting the target guidance signals to suppress the model's restoration capabilities. This results in a semantic corruption that thwarts any unauthorized mimicry. Notably, the security gain does not come at the price of visual utility, as VOID simultaneously manages to confine perturbations to human-imperceptible regions of protected images. Our comprehensive evaluation of 24 state-of-the-art defenses against 10 mimicry attacks on 5 datasets demonstrates VOID's unprecedented protection power: it increases the average Frechet Inception Distance (FID) from 113 to 365, a 223% improvement over the strongest defense to date.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VOID：抵御潜在扩散模型中的未经授权模仿
尽管潜在扩散模型（LDMs）已彻底改变视觉合成，但它们正日益被用于未经授权的个人模仿。现有防御方法通过注入欺骗性扰动引导生成图像趋向无关目标。然而，该方法依赖一个未经验证的假设：细微扰动能在LDM漫长的生成过程中持续保持欺骗效果。实际上，模型的内在修复机制会消除此类扰动，导致个人身份重新出现在生成图像中。我们提出VOID防御框架，通过操控LDM的内在随机性解决此难题：VOID以两种新颖方式扰动扩散流程：1）放大潜在编码误差以破坏图像语义结构；2）抵消目标引导信号以抑制模型的修复能力。这导致语义破坏，从而阻止任何未经授权的模仿。值得注意的是，安全增益并未以视觉实用性为代价，因为VOID同时将扰动限制在受保护图像的人眼不可察觉区域。我们在5个数据集上对24种最新防御方法进行全面评估，针对10种模仿攻击，VOID展现出前所未有的防护能力：其平均FID值从113提升至365，较当前最强防御提升223%。

VOID manipulates the diffusion process by amplifying latent encoding errors to break semantic structure and counteracting target guidance to suppress restoration mechanisms, thereby inducing semantic corruption that prevents mimicry. Crucially, it maintains visual utility by confining perturbations to imperceptible regions. Evaluated against 24 existing defenses and 10 mimicry attacks across five datasets, VOID achieves a 223% improvement over the best prior defense, raising average FID from 113 to 365.
针对潜扩散模型被用于未经授权的个体模仿问题，作者提出VOID防御框架，通过操纵模型固有的随机性来阻止模仿。VOID通过放大潜在编码误差破坏语义结构，并对抗目标引导信号以抑制模型的修复能力，从而造成语义破坏，阻止身份模仿。关键在于，其扰动被限制在人类不可察觉的区域，不影响视觉可用性。在5个数据集上对24种现有防御和10种模仿攻击的全面评估显示，VOID相较当前最优防御提升223%，平均FID从113升至365。
Auditing Demographic Bias in Facial Landmark Detection for Fair Human-Robot Interaction
Authors: Pablo Parte, Roberto Valle, José M. Buenaposada, Luis Baumela
Venue: 35th IEEE International Conference on Robot and Human Interactive Communication (RO-MAN 2026)
First: 2026-04-08T11:22:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:08:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fairness in human-robot interaction critically depends on the reliability of the perceptual models that enable robots to interpret human behavior. While demographic biases have been widely studied in high-level facial analysis tasks, their presence in facial landmark detection remains unexplored. In this paper, we conduct a systematic audit of demographic bias in this task, analyzing the age, gender, and race biases. To this end, we introduce a controlled statistical methodology to disentangle demographic effects from confounding visual factors. Our analysis demonstrates that visual confounders, particularly head pose and face resolution, heavily outweigh the impact of demographic attributes. Notably, after accounting for these confounders, performance disparities across gender and race vanish. However, we identify a statistically significant age-related bias, with higher localization errors for older individuals. This shows that fairness issues can emerge even in low-level vision components and can propagate through the HRI pipeline. We argue that auditing and correcting such biases is a necessary step toward trustworthy and equitable robot perception systems.

This study addresses the lack of research on demographic bias in facial landmark detection, a foundational task for human-robot interaction.
