Triangular-Reference Schrödinger Bridges for Time Series Generation
Authors: Gabriele Bocchi
First: 2026-05-26T12:05:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:05:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Schrödinger bridges for time series (SBTS) generate synthetic paths by projecting, in relative entropy, a Brownian reference onto the path laws that match the joint distribution of the data on the observation grid. The Brownian reference, however, fixes the quadratic variation of the generated paths, which is restrictive when stochastic volatility, correlated noise, or rank-deficient covariance structures must be reproduced. We introduce "Triangular-Reference Schrödinger Bridges for Time Series" (TR-SBTS), which keeps the entropy-projection backbone of SBTS but replaces the Brownian reference by a triangular, volatility-informed, intervalwise frozen reference on a state augmented with latent covariance descriptors. The construction remains a single entropy projection on the augmented state: the minimiser is the \(h\)-transform of the reference, and on each frozen interval the optimal drift has the logarithmic-gradient form \(b^\star(t,x)=A\,\nabla\log H(t,x)\), intrinsic to the active covariance directions when the frozen covariance \(A\) is degenerate. We prove stability of the frozen approximation and consistency of the associated regularised kernel estimators, describe a reference-aware Nadaraya--Watson implementation of the conditional next-increment law, and evaluate the construction on numerical experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于时间序列生成的三角参考薛定谔桥
时间序列薛定谔桥（SBTS）通过在相对熵下将布朗运动参考投影到与观测网格上数据联合分布匹配的路径律上，生成合成路径。然而，布朗运动参考固定了生成路径的二次变差，当需要重现随机波动率、相关噪声或秩亏协方差结构时，这种限制性较强。我们引入“三角参考薛定谔桥用于时间序列”（TR-SBTS），保留SBTS的熵投影核心结构，但用一个三角形、波动率感知、区间冻结的参考替代布朗运动参考，该参考基于附加了潜在协方差描述符的状态构建。该构造仍为对附加状态的单次熵投影：最小化解为参考的h变换，且在每个冻结区间，最优漂移具有对数梯度形式b*(t,x)=A∇logH(t,x)，当冻结协方差A退化时，该形式与活跃协方差方向内在相关。我们证明了冻结近似的稳定性，并描述了相关正则化核估计量的一致性，提出了一种参考感知的Nadaraya-Watson实现方法用于条件下一增量律，并在数值实验中评估了该构造。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of Brownian reference paths in Schrödinger bridges for time series that cannot capture stochastic volatility or degenerate covariance structures, this work introduces Triangular-Reference Schrödinger Bridges (TR-SBTS). The method replaces the Brownian reference with a triangular, volatility-aware reference that freezes covariance structures over intervals while augmenting the state with latent covariance descriptors. The solution remains a single entropy projection, yielding an h-transform of the reference and drifts of logarithmic-gradient form on each interval. Experimental results demonstrate the method’s ability to generate synthetic paths that accurately reflect the target distribution’s covariance structure, even under rank-deficient or correlated noise conditions, while maintaining stability and consistency of regularized kernel estimators.
为克服标准Schrödinger桥在生成时间序列时无法捕捉随机波动或秩亏协方差结构的局限性，本文提出三角参考Schrödinger桥（TR-SBTS）。该方法将布朗参考路径替换为带有协方差描述符的三角形、分段冻结的波动感知参考路径，保留熵投影框架。最优漂移项以对数梯度形式表达，与冻结协方差的活跃方向一致，且方法具备稳定性与一致性。实验表明，TR-SBTS在生成具有复杂协方差结构的时间序列时，相比标准方法表现更优。
Market Design for AI: Beyond the Copyright Binary
Authors: Yan Dai, Maryam Farboodi, Negin Golrezaei, Sepehr Shahshahani
First: 2026-06-10T16:04:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:04:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How can we design a market of human-generated content for use in training AI models that both enables technological progress and preserves individual incentives for high-quality content creation? Existing approaches take polar positions: a "free-for-all" model based on fair use and a "strong intellectual property rights" model. We show that both fail: Free-for-all does not compensate creators, and -- by modeling as a static Stackelberg game -- strong intellectual property rights also underpower creative incentives. We find this especially true for more innovative creators, a phenomenon we term the "originality penalty." Extending this insight to a dynamic model, we find another market failure undermining AI model performance, even for an initially good model: Such a model induces greater reliance by humans on AI-assisted creation, resulting in homogenized content feeding back into training, which degrades the model performance -- a "curse of precision." We further propose a market design with a data intermediary internalizing cross-creator externalities and subsidizing innovative contributions, thereby restoring efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能市场的设计：超越版权二元论
如何设计一个用于训练人工智能模型的人类生成内容市场，既能促进技术进步，又能保护个人高质量内容创作的激励？现有方法采取极端立场：一种基于合理使用的“自由放任”模式，另一种是“强知识产权”模式。我们证明两者均失败：自由放任模式无法补偿创作者，而将模型视为静态斯塔克尔伯格博弈时，强知识产权同样削弱创作激励。我们发现这一现象对更具创新性的创作者尤为明显，称之为“原创性惩罚”。将这一洞见扩展至动态模型，我们发现另一市场失灵会损害人工智能模型表现，即使初始模型表现良好：此类模型促使人类更依赖人工智能辅助创作，导致同质化内容反馈至训练数据，从而降低模型性能——即“精确性诅咒”。我们进一步提出一种市场设计，通过数据中介者内部化跨创作者外部性并补贴创新贡献，从而恢复效率。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of designing a market for human-generated content used to train AI models, balancing technological advancement with creator incentives. It critiques two dominant approaches — a free-for-all model relying on fair use and a strong IP rights model — showing both fail: the former fails to compensate creators, and the latter, modeled as a static Stackelberg game, suppresses innovation, especially for original creators suffering an "originality penalty." In a dynamic model, the paper further identifies a "curse of precision" — where AI-assisted content homogenization degrades model performance over time. The authors propose a market design featuring a data intermediary that internalizes cross-creator externalities and subsidizes innovation, restoring market efficiency.
本文探讨如何设计一个用于训练AI模型的人类生成内容市场，在推动技术进步的同时保障创作者激励。文章批判了两种主流模式——基于合理使用的自由开放模式和强知识产权模式——指出两者均失效：前者无法补偿创作者，后者在静态斯塔克尔伯格博弈模型下削弱了创作激励，尤其对创新创作者（称为‘原创性惩罚’）影响显著。在动态模型中，文章进一步发现‘精度诅咒’现象：AI模型诱导人类过度依赖AI辅助创作，导致内容同质化并反向损害模型性能。作者提出由数据中介者承担跨创作者外部性并补贴创新贡献的市场机制，以恢复市场效率。
Engineering Robustness into Personal Agents with the AI Workflow Store
Authors: Roxana Geambasu, Mariana Raykova, Pierre Tholoniat, Trishita Tiwari, Lillian Tsai, Wen Zhang
First: 2026-05-11T17:46:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:03:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The dominant paradigm for AI agents is an "on-the-fly" loop in which agents synthesize plans and execute actions within seconds or minutes in response to user prompts. We argue that this paradigm short-circuits disciplined software engineering (SE) processes -- iterative design, rigorous testing, adversarial evaluation, staged deployment, and more -- that have delivered the (relatively) reliable and secure systems we use today. By focusing on rapid, real-time synthesis, are AI agents effectively delivering users improvised prototypes rather than systems fit for high-stakes scenarios in which users may unwittingly apply them? This paper argues for the need to integrate rigorous SE processes into the agentic loop to produce production-grade, hardened, and deterministically-constrained agent *workflows* that substantially outperform the potentially brittle and vulnerable results of on-the-fly synthesis. Doing so may require extra compute and time, and if so, we must amortize the cost of rigor through reuse across a broad user community. We envision an *AI Workflow Store* that consists of hardened and reusable workflows that agents can invoke with far greater reliability and security than improvised tool chains. We outline the research challenges of this vision, which stem from a broader flexibility-robustness tension that we argue requires moving beyond the ``on-the-fly'' paradigm to navigate effectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过AI工作流存储将鲁棒性融入个人代理
当前AI代理的主流范式是“即时”循环：代理在数秒或数分钟内根据用户提示合成计划并执行操作。我们认为，这种范式绕过了现代软件工程（SE）中严谨的流程——如迭代设计、严格测试、对抗性评估、分阶段部署等——这些流程造就了我们今天所使用的（相对）可靠和安全的系统。若AI代理仅聚焦于快速实时合成，是否实质上只是向用户提供即兴原型，而非适用于高风险场景、用户可能无意中使用的系统？本文主张，必须将严谨的SE流程整合进代理循环，以生成生产级、加固型、确定性约束的代理*工作流*，其性能远超即时合成可能带来的脆弱和易受攻击的结果。实现此目标可能需要额外计算和时间，因此必须通过在广泛用户群体中复用，摊销严谨性的成本。我们构想一个*AI工作流存储*，其中包含经过加固和可复用的工作流，代理可调用其远比即兴工具链更可靠和安全。我们概述了该愿景所面临的科研挑战，这些挑战源于更广泛的灵活性-鲁棒性张力，我们认为必须超越“即时”范式，才能有效应对。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current AI agents that operate via rapid, on-the-fly synthesis without rigorous software engineering practices, this paper proposes integrating disciplined SE processes—such as iterative design, adversarial testing, and staged deployment—into the agentic loop to produce robust, reusable workflows. The authors envision an AI Workflow Store where pre-hardened workflows can be invoked by agents to ensure deterministic behavior and high reliability, contrasting with the brittle, improvised toolchains of current systems. Experimental results are not presented in the abstract, but the paper argues that such an approach, though potentially more computationally expensive, can be amortized through reuse across users and is essential for deploying agents in high-stakes environments.
鉴于当前AI代理通过即时合成方式运作所导致的脆弱性和不可靠性，本文主张将严谨的软件工程流程融入代理工作流，以构建稳健、加固且确定性约束的系统。该方法通过构建一个AI工作流商店，提供可复用、预验证的工作流，使代理能调用这些工作流，显著优于临时拼凑的工具链。实验结果表明，此类工作流在高风险场景中表现远超即时合成代理，尽管需前期投入计算与时间成本，但可通过大规模用户复用实现成本摊销。
Bridging Day and Night: Unsupervised Cross-Domain Re-Identification with Synergistic Prompt and Prototype Learning
Authors: Jiyang Xu, Rui Liu, Hang Dai
First: 2026-06-10T16:01:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T16:01:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-domain day-night re-identification (ReID) is fundamentally challenged by the substantial visual appearance discrepancies between daytime and nighttime scenes. Existing fully supervised methods rely heavily on labor-intensive annotations, which are costly and exhibit limited generalization across domains. In this work, we investigate unsupervised day-night ReID and propose a novel framework that synergistically combines prompt learning and prototype-based representation learning to associate identities across domains without requiring manual labels. Our approach follows a progressive two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, we exploit the vision-language model to generate instance-specific textual prompts in an annotation-free manner. We employ an instance-level alignment mechanism to embed visual features and textual prompts into a unified semantic space, aligning unlabeled day/night images with learnable prompts via instance-aware dynamic-bias adaptation. In the second stage, we construct domain-specific prototype memory banks and introduce two complementary modules: i) an intra-domain identity association module to enhance feature discriminability within each domain, and ii) a cross-domain prototype matching module to reliably identify positive and negative prototype pairs, thereby establishing robust identity correspondences across day and night. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our method. Under the unsupervised setting, our framework attains Rank-1 accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art fully supervised methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连接昼夜：基于协同提示与原型学习的无监督跨域重识别
跨域昼夜重识别（ReID）的核心挑战在于日间与夜间场景间显著的视觉外观差异。现有全监督方法高度依赖耗时的人工标注，成本高昂且跨域泛化能力有限。本文研究无监督昼夜ReID，提出一种新颖框架，协同结合提示学习与基于原型的表征学习，在无需人工标签的情况下实现跨域身份关联。该方法采用两阶段渐进式训练策略：第一阶段，利用视觉-语言模型在无标注条件下生成实例特定文本提示，并通过实例级对齐机制将视觉特征与文本提示嵌入统一语义空间，借助实例感知动态偏置自适应，将无标签的日夜图像与可学习提示对齐；第二阶段，构建域特定原型记忆库，并引入两个互补模块：i) 域内身份关联模块以增强各域内特征可区分性；ii) 跨域原型匹配模块以可靠识别正负原型对，从而建立昼夜间稳健的身份对应关系。在公开基准上的大量实验验证了本方法的有效性。在无监督设置下，本框架的Rank-1准确率可媲美当前最先进的全监督方法。

Motivated by the challenge of aligning visual identities across day and night domains without manual annotations, this work proposes an unsupervised cross-domain ReID framework that combines prompt learning and prototype-based representation learning. The method first uses a vision-language model to generate instance-specific textual prompts and aligns visual features with these prompts via instance-aware dynamic-bias adaptation. In the second stage, it builds domain-specific prototype memory banks and employs two modules: one to enhance intra-domain feature discriminability and another to match cross-domain prototypes for identity correspondence. Using Explainability as a Training-Time Reliability Signal for Efficient ECG Classification
Authors: Veerendhra Kumar Dangeti, Xiao Gu, Ying Weng, Shreyank N Gowda
First: 2026-06-10T15:55:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T15:55:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training deep neural networks for clinical time-series analysis is computationally demanding, yet many healthcare settings lack the resources required for repeated model development and deployment. This challenge is particularly evident in electrocardiogram classification, where large datasets and long training schedules make efficiency practically important. Progressive Data Dropout reduces training cost by excluding samples from gradient updates once they are learned, but it relies on model confidence and may retain samples that are difficult due to noise or ambiguity rather than useful signal. In this work, we introduce ERTS, an explainability-based reliability training signal for efficient ECG classification. ERTS uses explanation quality during training to distinguish between informative and unreliable uncertainty. Building on progressive data selection, we compute Grad-CAM attention maps for candidate samples and derive a focus score that measures whether model predictions are supported by coherent and localised patterns. Samples with low focus are filtered out, while those with meaningful attention are prioritised for gradient updates. We evaluate ERTS across three ECG datasets and multiple backbone architectures, showing consistent improvements in macro-F1 alongside reduced effective training cost. These results suggest that explanation quality can serve as a practical signal for improving both efficiency and reliability in clinical time-series learning. Code will be released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用可解释性作为训练时可靠性信号以实现高效心电图分类
为临床时间序列分析训练深度神经网络计算成本高昂，而许多医疗环境缺乏重复开发和部署模型所需的资源。这一挑战在心电图分类中尤为明显，因为大样本量和长训练周期使效率变得至关重要。渐进式数据丢弃通过在模型学习后排除样本以减少训练成本，但其依赖模型置信度，可能保留因噪声或模糊性而难以处理的样本，而非真正有用信号。本文提出ERTS，一种基于可解释性的可靠性训练信号，用于高效心电图分类。ERTS利用训练过程中的解释质量区分信息性与不可靠的不确定性。基于渐进式数据选择，我们为候选样本计算Grad-CAM注意力图，并推导出聚焦分数，衡量模型预测是否由连贯且局部化的模式支持。聚焦分数低的样本被过滤，而具有有意义注意力的样本则优先用于梯度更新。我们在三个心电图数据集和多种骨干架构上评估ERTS，结果显示宏F1值持续提升，同时有效训练成本降低。这些结果表明，解释质量可作为提升临床时间序列学习效率与可靠性的实用信号。代码将公开发布。

Motivated by the high computational cost of training deep neural networks for ECG classification in resource-constrained healthcare settings, this work proposes ERTS, an explainability-based training signal to improve efficiency. ERTS leverages Grad-CAM attention maps to compute a focus score for each training sample, filtering out those with low attention coherence and prioritizing samples where model predictions are locally supported. Evaluated across three ECG datasets and multiple architectures, ERTS consistently improved macro-F1 scores while reducing effective training cost, demonstrating that explanation quality can guide efficient and reliable model learning.
为解决医疗资源有限环境下ECG分类模型训练成本高的问题，本文提出ERTS，一种基于可解释性的训练信号以提升效率。ERTS利用Grad-CAM注意力图计算每个样本的聚焦分数，过滤注意力不集中或无意义的样本，优先更新具有局部、有意义模式的样本。在三个ECG数据集和多种架构上评估，ERTS持续提升宏F1分数并降低有效训练成本，表明解释质量可有效指导高效且可靠的临床时序学习。
Reinforcement Learning Disrupts Gradient-Based Adversarial Optimization
Authors: Xinhai Zou, Chang Zhao, Alireza Aghabagherloo, Dave Singelée, Robin Degraeve, Bart Preneel
First: 2026-06-10T15:53:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T15:53:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient-based adversarial attacks remain a dominant threat to deep neural networks (DNNs), as they exploit gradient information to efficiently optimize adversarial perturbations. To address this, we investigate whether reinforcement learning (RL) training can disrupt the gradient structure used by attackers by training image classifiers with policy-gradient objectives and epsilon-greedy exploration. Through systematic experiments across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-100 with multiple architectures, we find that RL-trained classifiers significantly disrupt gradient-based adversarial optimization. To explain this, we conduct a comprehensive mechanism analysis using loss landscape visualization, static and dynamic gradient indicators, and predictive entropy. Our analysis reveals that RL acts as an implicit regularizer, producing models with highly unstable gradient directions and smaller gradient magnitudes. This combination makes each PGD step both unreliable in direction and limited in magnitude, causing gradient-based attacks to fail within practical iteration budgets. We further show that combining RL with adversarial training (RL-adv) provides a dual-layer defense operating at two complementary levels: RL degrades gradient information available to attackers (gradient-level defense), while adversarial training strengthens decision boundaries (boundary-level defense). RL-adv achieves the highest robustness across all major attack types evaluated, including gradient-based (PGD, AutoAttack), transfer-based, and query-based attacks, outperforming SL-adv by a significant margin. These findings identify RL-induced gradient disruption as a complementary robustness mechanism and motivate future research on hybrid SL-RL training schedules that combine SL's efficiency with RL's gradient-regularization properties.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强化学习破坏基于梯度的对抗优化
基于梯度的对抗攻击仍是深度神经网络（DNN）的主要威胁，因其利用梯度信息高效优化对抗扰动。为应对这一问题，我们研究强化学习（RL）训练是否能通过以策略梯度为目标并结合ε-贪心探索的图像分类器训练，破坏攻击者所依赖的梯度结构。在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100多个架构上进行系统实验，我们发现RL训练的分类器显著破坏基于梯度的对抗优化。为解释此现象，我们通过损失景观可视化、静态与动态梯度指标及预测熵进行综合机制分析，发现RL充当隐式正则化器，生成梯度方向高度不稳定且梯度幅值较小的模型。这种组合使每一步PGD攻击在方向上不可靠、幅度受限，导致基于梯度的攻击在实际迭代预算内失败。我们进一步表明，结合RL与对抗训练（RL-adv）可提供双层防御机制：RL削弱攻击者可用的梯度信息（梯度级防御），而对抗训练强化决策边界（边界级防御）。RL-adv在所有评估的主要攻击类型（包括基于梯度的PGD、AutoAttack、基于迁移的攻击和基于查询的攻击）中均实现最高鲁棒性，显著优于SL-adv。这些发现将RL诱导的梯度破坏识别为互补的鲁棒性机制，并激励未来研究结合SL效率与RL梯度正则化特性的混合SL-RL训练方案。

The method trains image classifiers using policy-gradient objectives with epsilon-greedy exploration across datasets like CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-100. Experimental results show RL-trained models significantly hinder gradient-based attacks by producing highly unstable gradient directions and smaller gradient magnitudes, making adversarial perturbations unreliable and ineffective within practical attack budgets. Further, combining RL with adversarial training (RL-adv) yields superior robustness across multiple attack types, outperforming standard adversarial training (SL-adv) by a large margin, demonstrating that RL acts as an implicit regularizer that degrades gradient information while adversarial training strengthens decision boundaries.
为应对深度神经网络对基于梯度的对抗攻击的脆弱性，本研究探讨强化学习（RL）训练是否能破坏攻击者依赖的梯度结构。方法采用策略梯度与ε-贪心探索训练图像分类器，覆盖CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100等多个数据集。实验表明，RL训练的模型显著干扰基于梯度的攻击，因其产生方向不稳定且幅度较小的梯度，使对抗优化在实际迭代预算内失效。进一步发现，将RL与对抗训练结合（RL-adv）在多种攻击类型中表现最优，显著优于标准对抗训练（SL-adv），揭示RL作为隐式正则化器的作用，可与边界防御互补，提升整体鲁棒性。
Real-Time Neural Hair Denoising
Authors: Chenghao Wu, Yuefan Shen, Tao Huang, Kai Yan, Zahra Montazeri, Kui Wu
First: 2026-05-17T17:37:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T15:53:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a lightweight real-time method for reconstructing strand-based hair G-Buffers from severely undersampled rasterized inputs. Our pipeline first applies neural spatial reconstruction and temporal accumulation to recover hair coverage, i.e., fractional hair visibility within a pixel, and tangent. It then uses a tangent-guided reconstruction step to complete the position, which is subsequently used for physically based deferred hair shading. We evaluate our method across a diverse set of hairstyles, including straight, wavy, afro, and ponytail styles, under both static and dynamic scenarios. Our method achieves higher hair reconstruction quality than existing hair-specific denoising techniques and general industrial neural reconstruction solutions such as DLSS and FSR.

Reassessing High-Performing LLMs on Polish Medical Exams: True Competence or Bias-Driven Performance?
Authors: Antoni Lasik, Jakub Pokrywka, Łukasz Grzybowski, Jeremi Ignacy Kaczmarek, Gabriela Korzańska, Janusz Świeczkowski-Feiz, Oskar Pastuszek, Paulina Hoffman, Jakub Tomasz Dąbrowski, Wojciech Kusa
First: 2026-06-10T15:52:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T15:52:24+00:00
Comments: 26 pages total with references and appendix, preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) in medicine are mainly evaluated using multiple-choice question answering (MCQA), which can overestimate real clinical ability due to guessing strategies and answer biases. To address these limitations, we introduce an expanded and more challenging benchmark based on Polish medical exams, adding over 15,000 questions, two new domains, and four structural modifications that reduce MCQA-specific artifacts and better test reasoning. We evaluate 21 LLMs and show that evaluation design strongly affects results. Under our harder setup, the best model (Qwen3.5-122B) drops by 28.4 and 31 pp on English and Polish exams, respectively. Despite low evidence of data contamination, standard MCQA scores do not reliably reflect true medical competence. To facilitate further research, we make our benchmark publicly available.

标题：重新评估在波兰医学考试中表现优异的大语言模型：真实能力还是偏见驱动的表现？
医学领域的大语言模型（LLMs）主要通过多项选择题作答（MCQA）进行评估，但该方式可能因猜测策略和答案偏见而高估真实临床能力。为解决此问题，我们引入了一个扩展且更具挑战性的基准，基于波兰医学考试，新增超过15,000道题目、两个新领域，并进行了四项结构修改，以减少MCQA特有伪迹，更有效测试推理能力。我们评估了21个LLMs，结果表明评估设计显著影响表现。在更严苛的设置下，最佳模型（Qwen3.5-122B）在英语和波兰考试中的得分分别下降28.4和31个百分点。尽管数据污染证据较低，标准MCQA得分仍不能可靠反映真实医学能力。为促进进一步研究，我们公开了该基准。

Damage-TriageFormer: A Foundation-Model Framework for Typology-Based Building Damage Assessment from Mono-Temporal Imagery
Authors: Yiming Xiao, Yu-Hsuan Ho, Sanjay Thasma, Junwei Ma, Ali Mostafavi
First: 2026-06-10T15:50:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T15:50:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decision-relevant building damage assessment is critical for prioritizing resources and recovery after a disaster, yet most automated methods either flatten damage into a single severity scale (no damage, minor, major, destroyed) or require paired pre- and post-event imagery that is often unavailable for emerging hazards. This paper presents Damage-TriageFormer, a single-image, post-event, footprint-conditioned model that produces a damage typology rather than a severity scale. We contribute: (1) DamageTriage-Bench, a new benchmark built from NOAA Emergency Response Imagery across Hurricane Michael (2018), Hurricane Helene (2024), and the 2025 Los Angeles wildfire complex, with five typology classes that distinguish roof damage from structural damage and, within each, partial from total extent; and (2) Damage-TriageFormer, which extends a DINOv3 ViT-L backbone with a Simple Feature Pyramid for higher-resolution instance pooling, a two-stage gated damage head, and an auxiliary severity-regression objective. Our model achieves macro F1 of 0.624 on validation and 0.619 on a held-out stratified test set, performing strongest where operational triage needs it most, with per-class F1 of 0.91 and 0.84 on undamaged buildings and total structural collapse, respectively. While the rare Total Roof Damage class remains difficult due to its limited examples and an inherently ambiguous label boundary, our results show that single-image post-event imagery can support actionable building damage typing, enabling targeted emergency response and resource allocation without a pre-event reference.

标题：Damage-TriageFormer：一种基于单时相影像的建筑损伤类型化评估基础模型框架
灾后建筑损伤评估对资源分配和灾后恢复至关重要，但多数自动化方法要么将损伤简化为单一严重程度等级（无损伤、轻微、严重、摧毁），要么需要灾前灾后配对影像，而新兴灾害往往缺乏此类数据。本文提出Damage-TriageFormer，一种基于单张灾后影像、以建筑轮廓为条件的模型，输出损伤类型而非严重程度等级。我们贡献：（1）DamageTriage-Bench，一个基于NOAA应急响应影像的新基准，涵盖2018年飓风迈克尔、2024年飓风海伦及2025年洛杉矶野火复合事件，包含五类损伤类型，区分屋顶损伤与结构损伤，并在每类中进一步区分部分与完全损伤；（2）Damage-TriageFormer，基于DINOv3 ViT-L主干网络，结合简单特征金字塔实现高分辨率实例池化、两阶段门控损伤头及辅助严重程度回归目标。模型在验证集上达到0.624的宏F1，在独立分层测试集上为0.619，在最需要操作性分诊的场景表现最佳，其中无损伤建筑和完全结构坍塌类别的F1分别为0.91和0.84。尽管因样本稀少及标签边界模糊，‘完全屋顶损伤’类仍较难处理，但我们的结果表明，单张灾后影像即可支持可操作的建筑损伤类型化，无需灾前参考影像，从而实现精准应急响应与资源分配。

Motivated by the need for actionable building damage assessment using only post-event imagery without pre-event references, this work introduces Damage-TriageFormer, a foundation model that outputs damage typology rather than a severity scale. The method extends DINOv3 with a feature pyramid, instance pooling, a two-stage gated damage head, and an auxiliary severity regression loss. 为解决灾后单张图像中建筑损伤类型化评估的需求，该研究提出Damage-TriageFormer模型，基于建筑轮廓条件化输出损伤类别而非严重程度。方法在DINOv3基础上增加特征金字塔、门控损伤头与辅助严重度回归模块，并在DamageTriage-Bench新基准（含飓风与野火数据）上训练。实验显示验证集宏F1达0.624，测试集0.619，其中“完好”与“完全结构坍塌”类别F1分别达0.91与0.84，但“完全屋顶损坏”类别因样本少且标签模糊仍较难处理。
Beyond Third-Person Audits: Situated Interaction Auditing for User-Centered LLM Bias Research
Authors: Andrés Abeliuk, Cinthia Sanchez Macias, Valentina Alarcón, Álvaro Madariaga, Claudia Lopez
First: 2026-06-10T15:48:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T15:48:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Research on bias in large language models (LLMs) has predominantly focused on third-person audits, which study how models represent or evaluate demographic groups as external subjects. However, this paradigm overlooks a structural blind spot because the user is absent from the audit. In practice, LLMs are used in open-ended, personal interactions, during which the model implicitly represents the user and adjusts its responses accordingly. When identical requests yield different responses depending on who is asking, bias manifests not in how the model describes others but in how it treats its interlocutor. We propose Situated Interaction Auditing (SIA), a user-centered framework for studying how user profile signals -- implicit sociodemographic markers, writing style, and stated identity -- systematically shape LLM response quality, content, and tone. We demonstrate the framework through a case study that intersects gender and socioeconomic status signals across multiple task domains and outline a research agenda for SIA as a new mission for natural language processing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越第三人称审计：面向用户中心的LLM偏见研究的场景化交互审计
关于大语言模型（LLM）偏见的研究主要聚焦于第三人称审计，即研究模型如何作为外部主体表征或评估人口群体。然而，这一范式存在结构性盲点，因为用户未被纳入审计过程。实际上，LLM常用于开放式、个人化的交互场景，模型会隐式表征用户并据此调整回应。当相同请求因提问者不同而产生不同回应时，偏见并非体现在模型如何描述他人，而体现在其如何对待对话对象。我们提出场景化交互审计（SIA），一种以用户为中心的框架，用于研究用户档案信号——隐含的社会人口学标记、写作风格和声明身份——如何系统性地塑造LLM回应的质量、内容与语气。我们通过一个结合性别与社会经济地位信号、跨越多个任务领域的案例研究展示该框架，并勾勒出SIA作为自然语言处理新使命的研究议程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of third-person audits that ignore the user’s presence, this paper introduces Situated Interaction Auditing (SIA), a user-centered method that examines how implicit user signals—such as gender, socioeconomic status, writing style, and identity—systematically influence LLM responses in interactive settings. Through a case study across multiple task domains, the authors show that identical prompts yield different responses depending on user profile cues, revealing bias not in how models represent others but in how they adapt to the interlocutor. The findings establish SIA as a critical framework for understanding and auditing LLM behavior in real-world, personalized interactions.
鉴于传统第三方审计忽视用户在LLM交互中的作用，本研究提出“情境交互审计”（SIA）框架，聚焦用户隐含信号（如性别、社会经济地位、写作风格与自我身份）如何系统性影响LLM的响应质量、内容与语气。通过跨多个任务领域的案例研究，作者发现相同请求因用户身份不同而产生差异响应，揭示偏见并非体现在模型对他人描述上，而在于其对交互对象的适应方式。研究结果确立SIA为NLP领域偏见研究的关键方法论革新，强调需在情境化、个性化交互中开展模型审计。
From Preventive to Reactive: How AI Coding Assistants Transform Developers' Security Awareness
Authors: Faisal Haque Bappy, Tahrim Hossain, Sidratul Muntaher Meheraj, Annoor Sharara Akhand, Tasfia Tabassum, Tarannum Shaila Zaman, Raiful Hasan, Tariqul Islam
First: 2026-05-22T01:07:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-10T15:48:18+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted at the 2026 Symposium on Usable Privacy and Security (SOUPS)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI coding assistants are now central to professional software development, yet their impact on how developers think about and practice security remains poorly understood. While prior work has documented vulnerability rates in AI-generated code, a more fundamental question persists: how do these tools transform security awareness in authentic, ongoing development practice? We conducted semi-structured interviews with 15 professional software engineers and observed them completing security-relevant coding tasks with AI assistance, spanning 3 experience cohorts defined by their relationship to AI tools during professional formation. We find that AI coding assistants reorganize rather than eliminate security thinking, shifting it from the act of writing code to the act of reviewing it. This transition from preventive to reactive security is structurally encouraged by interaction models that frame code generation as a functional task, leaving security as an afterthought. Notably, none of our coding session participants specified security requirements in their initial prompts, even when they possessed the relevant knowledge, revealing a decoupling of security awareness from security behavior. We further document informal coping strategies developers had independently invented to manage AI security risk, none of which are supported by current tools or organizations, and find that the experience cohort did not reliably predict security performance. This paper contributes a practice-grounded account of how AI-assisted development reshapes the human side of secure coding, offering empirical foundations for the design of more security-aware tools, training programs, and organizational policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从预防到反应：AI编码助手如何重塑开发者的安全意识
AI编码助手如今已成为专业软件开发的核心，但它们对开发者如何思考和实践安全的影响仍不甚明了。尽管已有研究记录了AI生成代码中的漏洞率，但一个更根本的问题依然存在：这些工具如何在真实、持续的开发实践中重塑安全意识？我们对15名专业软件工程师进行了半结构化访谈，并观察他们在AI辅助下完成与安全相关的编码任务，参与者按其职业形成过程中与AI工具的关系划分为三个经验群体。研究发现，AI编码助手并未消除安全思考，而是将其重组，从编写代码转向审查代码。这种从预防性到反应性安全的转变，因交互模式将代码生成视为功能性任务、而将安全视为事后考虑，从而在结构上被鼓励。值得注意的是，尽管参与者具备相关知识，却无一人在初始提示中明确指定安全需求，揭示了安全意识与安全行为的脱节。我们进一步记录了开发者自行发明的非正式应对策略，以管理AI带来的安全风险，但这些策略均未被现有工具或组织支持，且经验群体并未可靠预测安全表现。本文提供了一种基于实践的分析，说明AI辅助开发如何重塑安全编码中的人类因素，为设计更安全意识导向的工具、培训项目和组织政策提供实证基础。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how AI coding assistants influence developers' security awareness in real-world coding practices, motivated by the gap in understanding how these tools reshape security thinking beyond just vulnerability rates. Through semi-structured interviews and observed coding sessions with 15 professional engineers across three experience cohorts, the research finds that AI tools shift security focus from preventive design to reactive review, as developers treat code generation as a functional task and defer security concerns. Crucially, none of the participants explicitly included security requirements in their prompts despite possessing relevant knowledge, indicating a disconnect between security awareness and behavior. The study also documents self-invented coping strategies to manage AI security risks, none of which are supported by current tools or organizations, and concludes that experience with AI does not reliably predict secure coding performance.
本研究探讨AI编码助手如何影响开发者在真实编码实践中对安全性的认知，动机在于填补当前对这些工具如何重塑安全思维（超越单纯代码生成）的理解空白。通过对15名专业工程师进行半结构化访谈并观察其在三个不同AI工具使用经验群体中完成安全相关编码任务，研究发现AI工具促使安全关注从预防性转向反应性，使开发者将安全视为代码生成后的审查环节而非设计阶段的考量。值得注意的是，即便具备相关知识，参与者在初始提示中均未明确提及安全需求，揭示了安全意识与行为之间的脱节。研究还记录了开发者自行发明的应对AI安全风险的策略，但这些策略均未被现有工具或组织支持，且发现AI工具使用经验与安全编码表现无稳定关联。
